    6: function findSavedEvidenceGalleryBlock(template: any) {
    7:   const pages = Array.isArray(template?.pages) ? template.pages : [];
    8: 
    9:   for (const page of pages) {
   10:     const blocks = Array.isArray(page?.blocks) ? page.blocks : [];
   11:     const block = blocks.find((item: any) => item?.kind === "evidence-gallery");
   12: 
   13:     if (block) {
   14:       return block;
   15:     }
   16:   }
   17: 
   18:   return null;

   20: export function normalizeFinalReportTemplate(
   21:   template: any,
   22:   editorialBlocks: Record<string, string>,
   23:   previewCaseData: PreviewCaseData | null,
   24: ) {
   25:   const pages = Array.isArray(template?.pages) ? template.pages : [];
   26:   const savedEvidenceBlock = findSavedEvidenceGalleryBlock(template);
   27:   const hasEvidence = getValidPreviewEvidences(previewCaseData).length > 0;
   28: 
   29:   return {
   30:     ...template,
   31:     pages: pages.flatMap((page: any, pageIndex: number) => {
   32:       if (!hasEvidence && page?.kind === "evidence") {
   33:         return [];
   34:       }
   35: 
   36:       const pageBlocks = Array.isArray(page?.blocks) ? page.blocks : [];
   37: 
   38:       const normalizedBlocks = pageBlocks
   39:         .map((block: any, blockIndex: number) =>
   40:           normalizeFinalReportBlock(template, block, blockIndex, editorialBlocks),
   41:         )
   42:         .filter((block: any) => hasEvidence || block.kind !== "evidence-gallery");
   43: 
   44:       const shouldAddEvidenceBlock =
   45:         hasEvidence &&
   46:         page?.kind === "evidence" &&
   47:         !savedEvidenceBlock &&
   48:         !normalizedBlocks.some((block: any) => block.kind === "evidence-gallery");
   49: 
   50:       return [{
   51:         ...page,
   52:         id: page?.id || "final-page-" + (pageIndex + 1),
   53:         title: page?.title || "صفحة " + (pageIndex + 1),
   54:         kind: page?.kind || "content",
   55:         blocks: shouldAddEvidenceBlock
   56:           ? [
   57:               ...normalizedBlocks,
   58:               {
   59:                 id: "auto-evidence-gallery-" + (pageIndex + 1),
   60:                 kind: "evidence-gallery",
   61:                 title: "الشواهد والمرفقات",
   62:                 content: "",
   63:                 variant: "card",
   64:                 align: "right",
   65:                 showTitle: true,
   66:                 placement: "flow",
   67:                 evidenceLayout: "GRID_2X2",
   68:                 evidenceFit: "contain",
   69:                 evidenceAspectRatio: "LANDSCAPE_4_3",
   70:                 evidenceShowCaptions: true,
   71:                 evidenceAutoCreatePages: true,
   72:                 evidenceEmptyBehavior: "message",
   73:               },
   74:             ]
   75:           : normalizedBlocks,
   76:       }];
   77:     }),
   78:   };
   79: }

   81: function normalizeFinalReportBlock(
   82:   template: any,
   83:   block: any,
   84:   index: number,
   85:   editorialBlocks: Record<string, string>,
   86: ) {
   87:   const settings = block?.settings || {};

   94:     "paragraph";

   96:   const blockId = block?.id || "final-block-" + (index + 1);

   98:   const editedContent = getFinalEditedBlockContent(
   99:     block,
  100:     blockId,
  101:     editorialBlocks,

  336: 
  337: function normalizeFinalBlockKind(kind: string) {
  338:   if (kind === "cover-title") return "hero-title";
  339:   if (kind === "paragraph") return "multi-paragraph";
  340:   if (kind === "custom-paragraph") return "multi-paragraph";
  341:   if (kind === "text-library") return "multi-paragraph";
  342:   if (kind === "case-meta") return "field-list";
  343:   if (kind === "student-summary") return "field-list";
  344:   if (kind === "service-summary") return "field-list";
  345:   if (kind === "field-list") return "field-list";
  346:   if (kind === "approval-signature") return "closing-note";
  347:   if (kind === "identity-header") return "section-text";
  348: 
  349:   return kind;
  350: }
  351: 
  352: function getDefaultFinalBlockTitle(kind: string) {
  353:   if (kind === "cover-title") return "عنوان التقارير";
  354:   if (kind === "case-meta") return "بيانات الحالة";
  355:   if (kind === "student-summary") return "بيانات الطالب/الطالبة";
  356:   if (kind === "service-summary") return "بيانات الخدمة";
  357:   if (kind === "field-list") return "حقول من الحالة";
  358:   if (kind === "text-library") return "نص من مكتبة النصوص";
  359:   if (kind === "custom-paragraph") return "فقرة مخصصة";
  360:   if (kind === "evidence-gallery") return "الشواهد والمرفقات";
  361:   if (kind === "approval-signature") return "الاعتماد";
  362: 
  363:   return "بلوك التقارير";
  364: }

  366: 
  367: function getFinalEditedBlockContent(
  368:   block: any,
  369:   blockId: string,
  370:   editorialBlocks: Record<string, string>,
  371: ) {
  372:   const candidateKeys = [
  373:     blockId,
  374:     block?.id ? String(block.id) : "",
  375:   ].filter(Boolean);
  376: 
  377:   for (const key of candidateKeys) {
  378:     const value = editorialBlocks[key];
  379: 
  380:     if (!value || !String(value).trim()) {
  381:       continue;
  382:     }
  383: 
  384:     if (isFinalLegacyRenderedReportDump(value)) {
  385:       continue;
  386:     }
  387: 
  388:     return value;
  389:   }
  390: 
  391:   return "";
  392: }
  393: 
  394: function isFinalLegacyRenderedReportDump(value: string) {
  395:   const text = String(value || "");
  396: 
  397:   if (!text.trim()) {
  398:     return false;
  399:   }
  400: 
  401:   const hasReplacementChars = /�/.test(text);
  402:   const hasMojibakeMarks = /[ØÙÃ]/.test(text);
  403:   const hasReportDumpLabels =
  404:     /program_name\s*:|semester\s*:|gregorian_date\s*:|beneficiaries\s*:|execution_action\s*:|execution_mechanism\s*:|performance_indicator\s*:|selectedStudent\s*:/.test(
  405:       text,
  406:     );
  407: 
  408:   const hasOldArabicDump =
  409:     text.includes("ملخص التقارير") ||
  410:     text.includes("بيانات الحالة") ||
  411:     text.includes("القيم المسجلة") ||
  412:     text.includes("الشواهد:") ||
  413:     text.includes("تقرير:");
  414: 
  415:   const tooLongForBlock = text.length > 1800;
  416: 
  417:   return (
  418:     hasReplacementChars ||
  419:     hasMojibakeMarks ||
  420:     hasReportDumpLabels ||
  421:     (hasOldArabicDump && tooLongForBlock)
  422:   );
  423: }

  425: 
  426: function resolveFinalBlockContent({
  427:   template,
  428:   block,
  429:   settings,
  430:   textLibrary,
  431:   rawKind,
  432: }: {
  433:   template: any;
  434:   block: any;
  435:   settings: any;
  436:   textLibrary: any;
  437:   rawKind: string;
  438: }) {
  439:   if (rawKind === "cover-title") {
  440:     return block?.content || settings?.content || "{{case.title}}";
  441:   }
  442: 
  443:   if (rawKind === "custom-paragraph") {
  444:     return block?.customContent || block?.content || settings?.content || "";
  445:   }
  446: 
  447:   if (rawKind === "paragraph") {
  448:     return block?.content || settings?.content || "";
  449:   }
  450: 
  451:   if (rawKind === "text-library") {
  452:     return resolveFinalTextLibraryContent(template, block, settings, textLibrary);
  453:   }
  454: 
  455:   const candidateContent =
  456:     block?.content ||
  457:     settings?.content ||
  458:     block?.customContent ||
  459:     block?.defaultContent ||
  460:     "";
  461: 
  462:   return isFinalLegacyRenderedReportDump(candidateContent)
  463:     ? ""
  464:     : candidateContent;
  465: }
  466: 
  467: function resolveFinalTextLibraryContent(
  468:   template: any,
  469:   block: any,
  470:   settings: any,
  471:   textLibrary: any,
  472: ) {
  473:   const snippets = collectFinalTextSnippets(template);
  474:   const snippetId =
  475:     textLibrary?.snippetId ||
  476:     settings?.snippetId ||
  477:     block?.snippetId ||
  478:     "";
  479: 
  480:   if (snippetId) {
  481:     const selected = snippets.find((snippet: any) => snippet.id === snippetId);
  482: 
  483:     if (selected) {
  484:       return selected.content || selected.text || selected.body || "";
  485:     }
  486:   }
  487: 
  488:   const category =
  489:     textLibrary?.category ||
  490:     settings?.category ||
  491:     block?.category ||
  492:     "مقدمة";
  493: 
  494:   const serviceSlug =
  495:     textLibrary?.serviceSlug ||
  496:     settings?.serviceSlug ||
  497:     template?.serviceSlug ||
  498:     "";
  499: 
  500:   const matching = snippets.find((snippet: any) => {
  501:     const matchesCategory =
  502:       !category ||
  503:       category === "all" ||
  504:       snippet.category === category;
  505: 
  506:     const matchesService =
  507:       !serviceSlug ||
  508:       !snippet.serviceSlug ||
  509:       snippet.serviceSlug === serviceSlug;
  510: 
  511:     return matchesCategory && matchesService;
  512:   });
  513: 
  514:   if (matching) {
  515:     return matching.content || matching.text || matching.body || "";
  516:   }
  517: 
  518:   return getFinalTextLibraryFallback(category);
  519: }
  520: 
  521: function collectFinalTextSnippets(template: any) {
  522:   const candidates = [
  523:     template?.snippets,
  524:     template?.textSnippets,
  525:     template?.textLibrary,
  526:     template?.library?.snippets,
  527:     template?.settings?.snippets,
  528:   ];
  529: 
  530:   for (const candidate of candidates) {
  531:     if (Array.isArray(candidate)) {
  532:       return candidate;
  533:     }
  534:   }
  535: 
  536:   return [];
  537: }
  538: 
  539: function getFinalTextLibraryFallback(category: string) {
  540:   if (category === "هدف") {
  541:     return "يهدف هذا التقارير إلى توثيق {{service.name}} للحالة {{case.title}}، وبيان أبرز البيانات والإجراءات المرتبطة بها.";
  542:   }
  543: 
  544:   if (category === "إجراء") {
  545:     return "تم تنفيذ الإجراءات المرتبطة بالحالة وفق البيانات المدخلة في النظام، مع توثيق ما يلزم من شواهد ومرفقات.";
  546:   }
  547: 
  548:   if (category === "نتيجة") {
  549:     return "تشير البيانات المدخلة إلى أن الحالة تم التعامل معها ضمن خدمة {{service.name}}، مع حفظ النتائج والتوصيات داخل التقارير.";
  550:   }
  551: 
  552:   if (category === "توصية") {
  553:     return "يوصى بمتابعة الحالة حسب الحاجة، وتحديث السجل عند وجود مستجدات، وربط الشواهد الداعمة بالتقارير.";
  554:   }
  555: 
  556:   if (category === "خاتمة") {
  557:     return "تم إعداد هذا التقارير من منصة التوجيه الطلابي اعتمادًا على بيانات الحالة والشواهد المرتبطة بها.";
  558:   }
  559: 
  560:   return "تم إعداد هذا التقارير لخدمة {{service.name}} بناءً على بيانات الحالة {{case.title}} والشواهد المرتبطة بها.";
  561: }
  562: 
  563: export function collectFinalValues(data: any): FinalReportValueItem[] {
  564:   if (Array.isArray(data?.values)) {
  565:     return data.values.map((item: any) => ({
  566:       fieldKey: item.fieldKey || item.key || "",
  567:       fieldLabel: item.fieldLabel || item.label || item.fieldKey || "",
  568:       value:
  569:         item.value === undefined || item.value === null
  570:           ? ""
  571:           : String(item.value),
  572:       valueItems: Array.isArray(item.valueItems)
  573:         ? item.valueItems.map((valueItem: unknown) => String(valueItem || "").trim()).filter(Boolean)
  574:         : undefined,
  575:     }));
  576:   }
  577: 
  578:   if (data?.values && typeof data.values === "object") {
  579:     return Object.entries(data.values).map(([key, value]) => ({
  580:       fieldKey: key,
  581:       fieldLabel: key,
  582:       value:
  583:         value === undefined || value === null
  584:           ? ""
  585:           : String(value),
  586:     }));
  587:   }
  588: 
  589:   return [];
  590: }
  591: 
  592: export function resolveContextVariable(key: string, context: Record<string, string>) {
  593:   const aliases: Record<string, string[]> = {
  594:     reportTitle: ["reportTitle", "case.title", "caseTitle"],
  595:     caseTitle: ["case.title", "caseTitle", "reportTitle"],
  596:     serviceName: ["service.name", "serviceName"],
  597:     studentName: ["student.name", "studentName"],
  598:     studentGrade: ["student.grade", "studentGrade"],
  599:     studentClassroom: ["student.classroom", "studentClassroom"],
  600:     guardianName: ["student.guardianName", "guardianName"],
  601:     guardianPhone: ["student.guardianPhone", "guardianPhone"],
  602:     schoolName: ["identity.schoolName", "schoolName"],
  603:     counselorName: ["identity.counselorName", "counselorName"],
  604:     principalName: ["identity.principalName", "principalName"],
  605:   };
  606: 
  607:   const lookupKeys = [
  608:     key,
  609:     ...(aliases[key] || []),
  610:     key.startsWith("field.") ? key : "field." + key,
  611:   ];
  612: 
  613:   for (const lookupKey of lookupKeys) {
  614:     const value = context[lookupKey];
  615: 
  616:     if (value !== undefined && value !== null && String(value).trim() !== "") {
  617:       return String(value);
  618:     }
  619:   }
  620: 
  621:   return "";
  622: }
  623: 
  624: export function getFinalFieldListItems(block: any, previewCaseData: PreviewCaseData | null) {
  625:   const data = (previewCaseData || {}) as any;
  626:   const values = collectFinalValues(data);
  627:   const group = block.finalFieldGroup || block.originalKind || "";
  628:   const selectedKeys = Array.isArray(block.fieldKeys) ? block.fieldKeys : [];
  629: 
  630:   if (group === "case-meta") {
  631:     return [
  632:       {
  633:         key: "service",
  634:         label: "الخدمة",
  635:         value: data.serviceName || data.service?.name || data.caseEntry?.service?.name || "",
  636:       },
  637:       {
  638:         key: "case-title",
  639:         label: "الحالة",
  640:         value: data.title || data.caseEntry?.title || "",
  641:       },
  642:       {
  643:         key: "status",
  644:         label: "الحالة الحالية",
  645:         value: data.status || data.caseEntry?.status || "",
  646:       },
  647:       {
  648:         key: "created-at",
  649:         label: "تاريخ الإنشاء",
  650:         value: formatFinalDate(data.createdAt || data.caseEntry?.createdAt),
  651:       },
  652:     ];
  653:   }
  654: 
  655:   if (group === "student-summary") {
  656:     const student = data.student || data.caseEntry?.student || {};
  657: 
  658:     return [
  659:       {
  660:         key: "student-name",
  661:         label: "الطالب/الطالبة",
  662:         value: student.name || student.fullName || "",
  663:       },
  664:       {
  665:         key: "grade",
  666:         label: "الصف",
  667:         value: student.grade || "",
  668:       },
  669:       {
  670:         key: "classroom",
  671:         label: "الفصل",
  672:         value: student.classroom || "",
  673:       },
  674:       {
  675:         key: "guardian",
  676:         label: "ولي الأمر",
  677:         value: student.guardianName || "",
  678:       },
  679:     ];
  680:   }
  681: 
  682:   if (selectedKeys.length) {
  683:     return values
  684:       .filter((item: FinalReportValueItem) =>
  685:         selectedKeys.some(
  686:           (key: string) =>
  687:             key === item.fieldKey ||
  688:             key === item.fieldLabel ||
  689:             "field." + key === item.fieldKey,
  690:         ),
  691:       )
  692:       .map((item: FinalReportValueItem) => ({
  693:         key: item.fieldKey,
  694:         label: item.fieldLabel || item.fieldKey,
  695:         value: item.value,
  696:         ...(Array.isArray(item.valueItems) && item.valueItems.length > 1
  697:           ? { valueItems: item.valueItems }
  698:           : {}),
  699:       }));
  700:   }
  701: 
  702:   return values.slice(0, 10).map((item: FinalReportValueItem) => ({
  703:     key: item.fieldKey,
  704:     label: item.fieldLabel || item.fieldKey,
  705:     value: item.value,
  706:     ...(Array.isArray(item.valueItems) && item.valueItems.length > 1
  707:       ? { valueItems: item.valueItems }
  708:       : {}),
  709:   }));
  710: }
  711: 
  712: function formatFinalEvidenceCount(count: number) {
  713:   if (count <= 0) return "0 شاهد";
  714:   if (count === 1) return "شاهد واحد";
  715:   if (count === 2) return "شاهدان";
  716:   if (count >= 3 && count <= 10) return count + " شواهد";
  717: 
  718:   return count + " شاهد";
  719: }
  720: 
  721: 
  722: function formatFinalDate(value?: string | null) {
  723:   if (!value) {
  724:     return new Date().toLocaleDateString("ar-SA");
  725:   }
  726: 
  727:   try {
  728:     return new Date(value).toLocaleDateString("ar-SA");
  729:   } catch {
  730:     return String(value);
  731:   }
  732: }

   52: export function PageBlocks({
   53:   page,
   54:   context,
   55:   previewCase,
   56:   designId,
   57:   className,
   58: }: {
   59:   page?: any;
   60:   context: Record<string, string>;
   61:   previewCase: PreviewCaseData | null;
   62:   designId: ReportDesignId;
   63:   className: string;
   64: }) {
   65:   const blocks = (page?.blocks || []).filter((block: any) => block.visible !== false);
   66:   const flowBlocks = blocks.filter(
   67:     (block: any) =>
   68:       isSignatureGridDesignBlock(block) || (block.placement || "flow") === "flow",
   69:   );
   70:   const fixedBlocks = blocks.filter(
   71:     (block: any) =>
   72:       !isSignatureGridDesignBlock(block) && (block.placement || "flow") !== "flow",
   73:   );
   74: 
   75:   return (
   76:     <main className={`relative flex flex-col ${className}`}>
   77:       <div className="flex min-h-[inherit] flex-1 flex-col gap-[var(--report-block-gap,1rem)]">
   78:         {flowBlocks.flatMap((block: any) => [
   79:           isSignatureGridDesignBlock(block) ? (
   80:             <div
   81:               key={`${block.id}-bottom-reservation`}
   82:               aria-hidden="true"
   83:               className="min-h-0 flex-1"
   84:               data-report-signature-bottom-reservation
   85:             />
   86:           ) : null,
   87:           <div
   88:             key={block.id}
   89:             data-report-smart-block={block.kind || "content"}
   90:             data-report-smart-placement={block.placement || "flow"}
   91:             data-report-smart-movable={
   92:               block.kind === "evidence-gallery" ? "evidence" : "content"
   93:             }
   94:             data-report-priority-block={
   95:               isSignatureGridDesignBlock(block) ? "signature" : undefined
   96:             }
   97:             className={
   98:               isSignatureGridDesignBlock(block)
   99:                 ? "break-inside-avoid pt-[var(--report-signature-top-gap,1.5rem)]"
  100:                 : ""
  101:             }
  102:             style={
  103:               isSignatureGridDesignBlock(block)
  104:                 ? { breakInside: "avoid", pageBreakInside: "avoid" }
  105:                 : undefined
  106:             }
  107:           >
  108:             <DesignBlock block={block} context={context} previewCase={previewCase} designId={designId} />
  109:           </div>,
  110:         ])}
  111:       </div>
  112: 
  113:       <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0">
  114:         {fixedBlocks.map((block: any) => (
  115:           <div
  116:             key={block.id}
  117:             data-report-smart-block={block.kind || "fixed-content"}
  118:             data-report-smart-placement={block.placement || "flow"}
  119:             data-report-smart-fixed="true"
  120:             className={getPlacementClass(block.placement || "flow")}
  121:           >
  122:             <div className="pointer-events-auto">
  123:               <DesignBlock block={block} context={context} previewCase={previewCase} designId={designId} />
  124:             </div>
  125:           </div>
  126:         ))}
  127:       </div>
  128:     </main>
  129:   );
  130: }

  134: function isDynamicFieldsDesignBlock(block: any) {
  135:   const kind = String(block?.kind || "").trim();
  136:   const smartKind = String(block?.settings?.smartBlockKind || "").trim();
  137:   const title = String(block?.title || "").trim();
  138: 
  139:   return (
  140:     kind === "dynamic-fields" ||
  141:     kind === "field-list" ||
  142:     kind === "case-meta" ||
  143:     kind === "student-summary" ||
  144:     kind === "service-summary" ||
  145:     smartKind === "dynamic-fields" ||
  146:     smartKind === "field-list" ||
  147:     title.includes("حقول") ||
  148:     title.includes("بيانات الحالة") ||
  149:     Array.isArray(block?.dynamicFields)
  150:   );
  151: }
  152: function getDynamicFieldCardsForBlock(block: any, previewCase: any) {
  153:   const configuredItems = Array.isArray(block?.dynamicFields)
  154:     ? block.dynamicFields
  155:     : [];
  156: 
  157:   if (configuredItems.length) {
  158:     return configuredItems
  159:       .map((item: any, index: number) => {
  160:         const id =
  161:           cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.id) ||
  162:           cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.key) ||
  163:           `dynamic-field-${index + 1}`;
  164:         const valueItems = Array.isArray(item.valueItems)
  165:           ? translateWorkflowDynamicValueItems(item.valueItems)
  166:           : translateWorkflowDynamicValueItems(item.value);
  167: 
  168:         return {
  169:           id,
  170:           key:
  171:             cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.key) ||
  172:             id,
  173:           label:
  174:             item.label !== undefined
  175:               ? cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.label)
  176:               : `حقل ${index + 1}`,
  177:           value:
  178:             item.value !== undefined
  179:               ? cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.value)
  180:               : "",
  181:           valueItems,
  182:           visible: item.visible !== false,
  183:         };
  184:       })
  185:       .filter((item: any) => item.visible && item.label)
  186:       .filter(
  187:         (item: any) =>
  188:           !previewCase?.hasStudentDataTable || !isStudentIdentityField(item),
  189:       );
  190:   }
  191: 
  192:   return getWorkflowDynamicFieldCards(previewCase)
  193:     .map((item: any, index: number) => {
  194:       const id =
  195:         cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.key) ||
  196:         cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.label) ||
  197:         `workflow-field-${index + 1}`;
  198: 
  199:       return {
  200:         id,
  201:         key: id,
  202:         label: cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.label),
  203:         value: cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.value),
  204:         valueItems: Array.isArray(item.valueItems)
  205:           ? item.valueItems.map((valueItem: unknown) => cleanWorkflowDynamicText(valueItem)).filter(Boolean)
  206:           : [],
  207:         visible: true,
  208:       };
  209:     })
  210:     .filter((item: any) => item.visible && item.label && item.value)
  211:     .filter(
  212:       (item: any) =>
  213:         !previewCase?.hasStudentDataTable || !isStudentIdentityField(item),
  214:     );
  215: }
  216: function isSignatureGridDesignBlock(block: any) {

  218:   const smartKind = String(block?.settings?.smartBlockKind || "").trim();

  223:     kind === "signatures" ||
  224:     kind === "approval-signatures" ||

  226:     smartKind === "signatures" ||
  227:     title.includes("توقيع") ||
  228:     title.includes("اعتماد") ||
  229:     Array.isArray(block?.signatures)

  233: function getDesignSignatureCards(block: any) {
  234:   const signatures = Array.isArray(block?.signatures) ? block.signatures : [];
  235: 
  236:   return signatures
  237:     .map((signature: any, index: number) => ({

  247: function DesignBlock({
  248:   block,
  249:   context,
  250:   previewCase,
  251:   designId,
  252: }: {
  253:   block: any;
  254:   context: Record<string, string>;
  255:   previewCase: PreviewCaseData | null;
  256:   designId: ReportDesignId;
  257: }) {
  258:   if (block.hideWhenMissing && block.boundFieldKey) {
  259:     const value = String(context[`field.${block.boundFieldKey}`] || "").trim();
  260:     if (!value) return null;
  261:   }
  262: 
  263:   const rendered = renderText(block.content || "", context);
  264:   const textAlign = block.align === "center" ? "text-center" : "text-right";
  265:   const implementation = getReportDesignImplementation(designId);
  266:   const accent = implementation.palette;
  267:   const BlockRenderer = implementation.BlockRenderer;
  268: 
  269:   if (BlockRenderer) {
  270:     return (
  271:       <BlockRenderer
  272:         block={block}
  273:         renderedContent={rendered}
  274:         textAlign={textAlign}
  275:         context={context}
  276:         splitLines={splitLines}
  277:         splitParagraphs={splitParagraphs}
  278:       />
  279:     );
  280:   }
  281: 
  282:   if (block.kind === "hero-title") {
  283:     return (
  284:       <section className={getBlockShellClass(implementation, "hero", "text-center")}>
  285:         <p
  286:           className={["text-sm font-black", accent.subtleTextClass].join(" ")}
  287:           style={{
  288:             fontSize: "calc(0.875rem * var(--report-content-font-scale, 1))",
  289:             lineHeight: "var(--report-content-line-height, 1.75)",
  290:           }}
  291:         >
  292:           {context["service.name"]}
  293:         </p>
  294:         <h1
  295:           className="mx-auto max-w-[145mm] font-black text-slate-950"
  296:           style={{
  297:             marginTop: "var(--report-heading-gap, 0.75rem)",
  298:             fontSize: `calc(1.875rem * var(--report-content-font-scale, 1) * ${getReportFontSizeMultiplier(getBlockSetting(block, "titleFontSize"))})`,
  299:             lineHeight: "var(--report-heading-line-height, 1.65)",
  300:           }}
  301:         >
  302:           {rendered}
  303:         </h1>
  304:       </section>
  305:     );
  306:   }
  307: 
  308:   if (block.kind === "meta-strip") {
  309:     return (
  310:       <section className={getBlockShellClass(implementation, "soft", textAlign)}>
  311:         <div
  312:           className="grid md:grid-cols-2"
  313:           style={{
  314:             gap: "var(--report-value-grid-gap, 0.5rem)",
  315:           }}
  316:         >
  317:           {splitLines(rendered).map((line) => (
  318:             <div
  319:               key={line}
  320:               className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white text-sm font-bold text-slate-700"
  321:               style={{
  322:                 paddingInline: "calc(1rem * var(--report-field-spacing-scale, 1))",
  323:                 paddingBlock: "calc(0.75rem * var(--report-field-spacing-scale, 1))",
  324:               }}
  325:             >
  326:               {line}
  327:             </div>
  328:           ))}
  329:         </div>
  330:       </section>
  331:     );
  332:   }
  333: 
  334:   if (block.kind === "bullet-list") {
  335:     const customBulletList = implementation.renderBulletList?.({
  336:       block,
  337:       renderedContent: rendered,
  338:       textAlign,
  339:       context,
  340:       splitLines,
  341:       splitParagraphs,
  342:     });
  343: 
  344:     if (customBulletList !== undefined) return customBulletList;
  345: 
  346:     const contentFontSizeClass = getReportFontSizeClass(
  347:       getBlockSetting(block, "contentFontSize"),
  348:       "text-sm",
  349:     );
  350: 
  351:     return (
  352:       <section className={getBlockShellClass(implementation, block.variant, textAlign)}>
  353:         {block.showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={block.title} fontSize={getBlockSetting(block, "titleFontSize")} /> : null}
  354:         <ul
  355:           style={{
  356:             display: "grid",
  357:             gap: "var(--report-bullet-gap, 0.5rem)",
  358:           }}
  359:         >
  360:           {splitLines(rendered).map((line) => (
  361:             <li
  362:               key={line}
  363:               className={["flex text-slate-700", contentFontSizeClass].join(" ")}
  364:               style={{
  365:                 gap: "var(--report-field-gap, 0.5rem)",
  366:                 lineHeight: "var(--report-bullet-line-height, 1.75)",
  367:                 fontSize: "calc(0.875rem * var(--report-bullet-font-scale, 1))",
  368:               }}
  369:             >
  370:               <span className={["mt-2 h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full", accent.dotClass].join(" ")} />
  371:               <span>{line}</span>
  372:             </li>
  373:           ))}
  374:         </ul>
  375:       </section>
  376:     );
  377:   }
  378:   if (block.kind === "multi-paragraph") {
  379:     const customNarrative = implementation.renderNarrative?.({
  380:       block,
  381:       renderedContent: rendered,
  382:       textAlign,
  383:       context,
  384:       splitLines,
  385:       splitParagraphs,
  386:     });
  387: 
  388:     if (customNarrative !== undefined) return customNarrative;
  389: 
  390:     return (
  391:       <section className={getBlockShellClass(implementation, block.variant, textAlign)}>
  392:         {block.showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={block.title} fontSize={getBlockSetting(block, "titleFontSize")} /> : null}
  393:         <div
  394:           style={{
  395:             display: "grid",
  396:             gap: "var(--report-paragraph-gap, 0.75rem)",
  397:           }}
  398:         >
  399:           {splitParagraphs(rendered).map((paragraph) => (
  400:             <p
  401:               key={paragraph}
  402:               className={["text-slate-700", getReportFontSizeClass(getBlockSetting(block, "contentFontSize"), "text-base")].join(" ")}
  403:               style={{
  404:                 margin: 0,
  405:                 lineHeight: "var(--report-narrative-line-height, 2)",
  406:                 fontSize: `calc(1rem * var(--report-content-font-scale, 1) * ${getReportFontSizeMultiplier(getBlockSetting(block, "contentFontSize"))})`,
  407:               }}
  408:             >
  409:               {paragraph}
  410:             </p>
  411:           ))}
  412:         </div>
  413:       </section>
  414:     );
  415:   }
  416:   if (block.kind === "report-one-table" || block.kind === "structured-table") {
  417:     const tableBlock = block.kind === "structured-table"
  418:       ? normalizeStructuredTableBlockPresentation(block)
  419:       : block;
  420:     const columns = tableBlock.kind === "structured-table"
  421:       ? (Array.isArray(tableBlock.columns) ? tableBlock.columns : [])
  422:       : Array.isArray(tableBlock.columns) && tableBlock.columns.length
  423:         ? tableBlock.columns
  424:         : ["المجال", "الإجراء", "ملاحظات"];
  425: 
  426:     const rows = tableBlock.kind === "structured-table"
  427:       ? (Array.isArray(tableBlock.rows) ? tableBlock.rows : [])
  428:       : Array.isArray(tableBlock.rows) && tableBlock.rows.length
  429:         ? tableBlock.rows
  430:         : [["", "", ""]];
  431: 
  432:     const tableSettings = tableBlock.tableSettings || {};
  433:     const compact = Boolean(tableSettings.compact);
  434:     const rounded = tableSettings.rounded !== false;
  435:     const highlightHeader = tableSettings.highlightHeader !== false;
  436:     const highlightFirstColumn = Boolean(tableSettings.highlightFirstColumn);

  744: function DesignFieldValueBlock({
  745:   title,
  746:   showTitle,
  747:   variant,
  748:   textAlign,
  749:   designId,
  750:   items,
  751: }: {
  752:   title: string;
  753:   showTitle?: boolean;
  754:   variant: string;
  755:   textAlign: string;
  756:   designId: ReportDesignId;
  757:   items: Array<{
  758:     key?: string;
  759:     label: string;
  760:     value?: string | null;
  761:   }>;
  762: }) {
  763:   const visibleItems = items.filter((item) => String(item.value || "").trim());
  764:   const implementation = getReportDesignImplementation(designId);
  765: 
  766:   return (
  767:     <section className={getBlockShellClass(implementation, variant, textAlign)}>
  768:       {showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={title} /> : null}
  769: 
  770:       {visibleItems.length ? (
  771:         <DesignValueGrid implementation={implementation} items={visibleItems} />
  772:       ) : (
  773:         <p className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-slate-500">
  774:           لا توجد قيم مرتبطة بهذا البلوك.
  775:         </p>
  776:       )}
  777:     </section>
  778:   );
  779: }

  167:           }}
  168:         >
  169:           هذه مربعات معاينة فقط. عند اختبار Case ID يحتوي شواهد، سيتم عرض الشواهد الفعلية هنا.
  170:         </p>
  171:       ) : null}
  172:     </section>
  173:   );
  174: }
  175:

FILE: components\report-engine\design-renderers\shared\report-auto-evidence.tsx

    1: import type { ReportDesignId } from "../report-design-types";
    2: import type { ReportPhysicalPageModel } from "../smart-layout/report-smart-physical-types";
    3: import { A4DesignPage } from "./report-blocks";
    4: import {
    5:   getEvidencePerPage,
    6:   getEvidenceStartIndex,
    7:   getValidPreviewEvidences,
    8: } from "./report-evidence-data";
    9: import type { PreviewCaseData } from "./report-types";
   10: 
   11: export type AutoEvidencePhysicalPage = {
   12:   page: ReportPhysicalPageModel;
   13:   pageLabel: string;
   14: };
   15: 
   16: export function buildAutoEvidencePhysicalPages({
   17:   activePage,
   18:   previewCase,
   19:   primaryEvidenceCount,
   20: }: {
   21:   activePage?: ReportPhysicalPageModel | null;
   22:   previewCase: PreviewCaseData | null;
   23:   primaryEvidenceCount?: number;
   24: }): AutoEvidencePhysicalPage[] {
   25:   const evidenceBlock = activePage?.blocks?.find(
   26:     (block) => block?.kind === "evidence-gallery",
   27:   );
   28: 
   29:   if (
   30:     !activePage ||
   31:     !evidenceBlock ||
   32:     evidenceBlock.evidenceAutoCreatePages === false
   33:   ) {
   34:     return [];
   35:   }
   36: 
   37:   const evidences = getValidPreviewEvidences(previewCase);
   38: 
   39:   if (!evidences.length) {
   40:     return [];
   41:   }
   42: 
   43:   const perPage = getEvidencePerPage(evidenceBlock);
   44:   const initialStartIndex = getEvidenceStartIndex(evidenceBlock);
   45:   const availableEvidenceCount = Math.max(
   46:     0,
   47:     evidences.length - initialStartIndex,
   48:   );
   49:   const legacyPrimaryCount = Math.min(availableEvidenceCount, perPage);
   50:   const consumedByPrimary = Number.isFinite(Number(primaryEvidenceCount))
   51:     ? Math.min(
   52:         availableEvidenceCount,
   53:         Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(primaryEvidenceCount))),
   54:       )
   55:     : legacyPrimaryCount;
   56:   const remainingCount = Math.max(
   57:     0,
   58:     availableEvidenceCount - consumedByPrimary,
   59:   );
   60: 
   61:   if (remainingCount <= 0) {
   62:     return [];
   63:   }
   64: 
   65:   const extraPagesCount = Math.ceil(remainingCount / perPage);
   66: 
   67:   return Array.from({ length: extraPagesCount }).map((_, index) => {
   68:     const pageNumber = index + 2;
   69:     const startIndex =
   70:       initialStartIndex + consumedByPrimary + index * perPage;
   71:     const pageEvidenceLimit = Math.min(
   72:       perPage,
   73:       evidences.length - startIndex,
   74:     );
   75:     const page: ReportPhysicalPageModel = {
   76:       ...activePage,
   77:       id: `${activePage.id}-evidence-${startIndex}`,
   78:       title: `${activePage.title} - صفحة ${pageNumber}`,
   79:       kind: "evidence",
   80:       corePhysicalPageId: activePage.corePhysicalPageId || activePage.id,
   81:       physicalPageRole: "evidence",
   82:       sourcePageIds: [...activePage.sourcePageIds],
   83:       blocks: [
   84:         {
   85:           ...evidenceBlock,
   86:           id: `${evidenceBlock.id}-auto-${startIndex}`,
   87:           title:
   88:             index === 0 && consumedByPrimary === 0
   89:               ? evidenceBlock.title
   90:               : `${evidenceBlock.title} - صفحة ${pageNumber}`,
   91:           evidenceStartIndex: startIndex,
   92:           evidenceLimit: pageEvidenceLimit,
   93:           evidenceAutoCreatePages: false,
   94:         },
   95:       ],
   96:     };
   97: 
   98:     return {
   99:       page,
  100:       pageLabel: `صفحة شواهد ${pageNumber}`,
  101:     };
  102:   });
  103: }
  104: 
  105: export function AutoEvidencePages({
  106:   designId,
  107:   activePage,
  108:   context,
  109:   previewCase,
  110:   primaryEvidenceCount,
  111:   preparedPages,
  112:   activePhysicalPageId,
  113:   renderMode = "stack",
  114: }: {
  115:   designId: ReportDesignId;
  116:   activePage?: ReportPhysicalPageModel;
  117:   context: Record<string, string>;
  118:   previewCase: PreviewCaseData | null;
  119:   primaryEvidenceCount?: number;
  120:   preparedPages?: AutoEvidencePhysicalPage[];
  121:   activePhysicalPageId?: string;
  122:   renderMode?: "single" | "stack";
  123: }) {
  124:   const pages = preparedPages || buildAutoEvidencePhysicalPages({
  125:     activePage,
  126:     previewCase,
  127:     primaryEvidenceCount,
  128:   });
  129:   const visiblePages = renderMode === "stack"
  130:     ? pages
  131:     : pages.filter(({ page }) => page.id === activePhysicalPageId);
  132: 
  133:   if (!visiblePages.length) {
  134:     return null;
  135:   }
  136: 
  137:   return (
  138:     <div
  139:       className={
  140:         renderMode === "stack"
  141:           ? "mt-6 space-y-6 print:mt-0 print:space-y-0"
  142:           : ""
  143:       }
  144:       data-report-auto-evidence-pages
  145:     >
  146:       {visiblePages.map(({ page, pageLabel }) => (
  147:         <A4DesignPage
  148:           key={page.id}
  149:           designId={designId}
  150:           page={page}
  151:           context={context}
  152:           previewCase={previewCase}
  153:           pageLabel={pageLabel}
  154:         />
  155:       ))}
  156:     </div>
  157:   );
  158: }

    1: "use client";

   32: type LogicalPage = {
   33:   id?: string;
   34:   title?: string;
   35:   kind?: string;
   36:   blocks?: LogicalReportBlock[];
   37:   [key: string]: any;
   38: };
