   55: const MAX_PAGE_COMPOSITION_ATTEMPTS = 40;
   56: 
   57: function isSignatureBlock(block: any) {
   58:   const kind =
   59:     String(block?.kind || "").trim();
   60: 
   61:   const smartKind =
   62:     String(
   63:       block?.settings?.smartBlockKind || "",
   64:     ).trim();
   65: 
   66:   const title =
   67:     String(block?.title || "").trim();
   68: 
   69:   return (
   70:     kind === "signature-grid" ||
   71:     kind === "signatures" ||
   72:     kind === "approval-signatures" ||
   73:     smartKind === "signature-grid" ||
   74:     smartKind === "signatures" ||
   75:     title.includes("توقيع") ||
   76:     title.includes("اعتماد") ||
   77:     Array.isArray(block?.signatures)
   78:   );
   79: }
   80: 
   81: function getSignatureBlocks(page: any) {
   82:   const blocks =
   83:     Array.isArray(page?.blocks)
   84:       ? page.blocks
   85:       : [];
   86: 
   87:   return blocks.filter(
   88:     isSignatureBlock,
   89:   );
   90: }
   91: 
   92: function getEvidenceBlock(page: any) {
   93:   const blocks =
   94:     Array.isArray(page?.blocks)
   95:       ? page.blocks
   96:       : [];
   97: 
   98:   return (
   99:     blocks.find(
  100:       (block: any) =>
  101:         block?.kind ===
  102:         "evidence-gallery",
  103:     ) || null
  104:   );
  105: }
  106: 
  107: function getCompositionResetKey(
  108:   designId: ReportDesignId,
  109:   page: any,
  110:   context: Record<string, string>,
  111:   previewCase: PreviewCaseData | null,
  112:   pageLabel: string,
  113: ) {
  114:   return createSemanticInputFingerprint({
  115:     designId,
  116:     page: {
  117:       blocks: Array.isArray(page?.blocks) ? page.blocks : [],
  118:       kind: page?.kind || "content",
  119:       title: page?.title || "",
  120:     },
  121:     pageLabel,
  122:     context,
  123:     previewCase,
  124:   });
  125: }
  126: 
  127: function makePrimaryPage(
  128:   page: ReportPhysicalPageModel | undefined,
  129:   evidenceBlock: any,
  130:   primaryEvidenceCount: number,
  131:   keepSignatureOnPrimary: boolean,
  132: ) {
  133:   if (!page) {
  134:     return page;
  135:   }
  136: 
  137:   const originalBlocks =
  138:     Array.isArray(page.blocks)
  139:       ? page.blocks
  140:       : [];
  141: 
  142:   let blocks =
  143:     keepSignatureOnPrimary
  144:       ? [...originalBlocks]
  145:       : originalBlocks.filter(
  146:           (block: any) =>
  147:             !isSignatureBlock(block),
  148:         );
  149: 
  150:   if (!evidenceBlock) {
  151:     return {
  152:       ...page,
  153:       blocks,
  154:     };
  155:   }
  156: 
  157:   /*
  158:    * Zero evidence means:
  159:    * remove evidence-gallery from the primary page.
  160:    *
  161:    * AutoEvidencePages still receives the ORIGINAL page,
  162:    * so no evidence is lost.
  163:    */
  164:   if (primaryEvidenceCount <= 0) {
  165:     blocks = blocks.filter(
  166:       (block: any) =>
  167:         block?.id !==
  168:         evidenceBlock.id,
  169:     );
  170: 
  171:     return {
  172:       ...page,
  173:       blocks,
  174:     };
  175:   }
  176: 
  177:   blocks = blocks.map(
  178:     (block: any) =>
  179:       block?.id === evidenceBlock.id
  180:         ? {
  181:             ...block,
  182:             evidenceStartIndex: getEvidenceStartIndex(evidenceBlock),
  183:             evidenceLimit:
  184:               primaryEvidenceCount,
  185:           }
  186:         : block,
  187:   );
  188: 
  189:   return {
  190:     ...page,
  191:     blocks,
  192:   };
  193: }
  194: 
  195: function makeSignatureOverflowPage(
  196:   page: ReportPhysicalPageModel,
  197:   signatureBlocks: any[],
  198: ) {
  199:   return {
  200:     ...page,
  201: 
  202:     id:
  203:       `${page?.id || "report"}-signature-overflow`,
  204: 
  205:     title:
  206:       "اعتماد التقرير",
  207: 
  208:     kind:
  209:       "signature",
  210: 
  211:     corePhysicalPageId:
  212:       page.corePhysicalPageId || page.id,
  213: 
  214:     physicalPageRole:
  215:       "signature" as const,
  216: 
  217:     sourcePageIds:
  218:       [...page.sourcePageIds],
  219: 
  220:     blocks:
  221:       signatureBlocks.map(
  222:         (block, index) => ({
  223:           ...block,
  224: 
  225:           id:
  226:             `${block?.id || "signature"}-overflow-${index + 1}`,
  227: 
  228:           /*
  229:            * This page is already an explicit overflow page.
  230:            */
  231:           placement:
  232:             "flow",
  233:         }),
  234:       ),
  235:   };
  236: }
  237: 
  238: export function SmartReportPageComposer({
  239:   designId,
  240:   page,
  241:   context,
  242:   previewCase,
  243:   pageLabel,
  244:   suppressAutoEvidencePages = false,
  245:   renderMode = "stack",
  246:   activePhysicalPageId,
  247:   onCompositionReady,
  248: }: SmartReportPageComposerProps) {
  249:   const hostRef =
  250:     useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(
  251:       null,
  252:     );
  253: 
  254:   const evidenceBlock =
  255:     useMemo(
  256:       () => getEvidenceBlock(page),
  257:       [page],
  258:     );
  259: 
  260:   const signatureBlocks =
  261:     useMemo(
  262:       () =>
  263:         getSignatureBlocks(page),
  264:       [page],
  265:     );
  266: 
  267:   const hasSignature =
  268:     signatureBlocks.length > 0;
  269: 
  270:   const validEvidences =
  271:     useMemo(
  272:       () =>
  273:         getValidPreviewEvidences(
  274:           previewCase,
  275:         ),
  276:       [previewCase],
  277:     );
  278: 
  279:   const evidenceAutoEnabled =
  280:     !suppressAutoEvidencePages &&
  281:     Boolean(evidenceBlock) &&
  282:     evidenceBlock
  283:       ?.evidenceAutoCreatePages !==
  284:       false;
  285: 
  286:   const maxPrimaryEvidenceCount =
  287:     evidenceBlock
  288:       ? Math.min(
  289:           Math.max(
  290:             0,
  291:             validEvidences.length - getEvidenceStartIndex(evidenceBlock),
  292:           ),
  293:           getEvidencePerPage(
  294:             evidenceBlock,
  295:           ),
  296:         )
  297:       : 0;
  298: 
  299:   const resetKey =
  300:     useMemo(
  301:       () =>
  302:         getCompositionResetKey(
  303:           designId,
  304:           page,
  305:           context,
  306:           previewCase,
  307:           pageLabel,
  308:         ),
  309:       [
  310:         designId,
  311:         page,
  312:         context,
  313:         previewCase,
  314:         pageLabel,
  315:       ],
  316:     );
  317: 
  318:   const [
  319:     primaryEvidenceCount,
  320:     setPrimaryEvidenceCount,
  321:   ] =
  322:     useState(
  323:       maxPrimaryEvidenceCount,
  324:     );
  325: 
  326:   const [
  327:     keepSignatureOnPrimary,
  328:     setKeepSignatureOnPrimary,
  329:   ] =
  330:     useState(true);
  331: 
  332:   const lastDecisionRef =
  333:     useRef("");
  334: 
  335:   const [planningPhase, setPlanningPhase] =
  336:     useState<ReportPagePlanningPhase>("DIRTY");
  337: 
  338:   const planningPhaseRef =
  339:     useRef<ReportPagePlanningPhase>("DIRTY");
  340: 
  341:   const stabilityRef = useRef({
  342:     signature: "",
  343:     count: 0,
  344:     attempts: 0,
  345:   });
  346: 
  347:   const finalizedPlacementRef = useRef<{
  348:     semanticInputFingerprint: string;
  349:     layoutResultFingerprint: string;
  350:     primaryEvidenceCount: number;
  351:     keepSignatureOnPrimary: boolean;
  352:   } | null>(null);
  353: 
  354:   const fontsSettledRef = useRef(false);
  355:   const settledAssetSourcesRef = useRef(new Set<string>());
  356:   const [invalidationReason, setInvalidationReason] = useState<
  357:     "initial" | "content-change"
  358:   >("initial");
  359: 
  360:   const setPhase = (next: ReportPagePlanningPhase) => {
  361:     planningPhaseRef.current = next;
  362:     setPlanningPhase((current) =>
  363:       current === next ? current : next,
  364:     );
  365:   };
  366: 
  367:   const isTerminalPhase = () =>
  368:     planningPhaseRef.current === "READY" ||
  369:     planningPhaseRef.current === "FROZEN";
  370: 
  371:   /*
  372:    * New report content:
  373:    *
  374:    * Start optimistically:
  375:    * - keep maximum allowed evidence
  376:    * - keep signature on primary page
  377:    *
  378:    * Real DOM measurement then negotiates downward.
  379:    */
  380:   useEffect(() => {
  381:     setInvalidationReason(
  382:       finalizedPlacementRef.current === null
  383:         ? "initial"
  384:         : "content-change",
  385:     );
  386:     finalizedPlacementRef.current = null;
  387:     lastDecisionRef.current = "";
  388:     stabilityRef.current = {
  389:       signature: "",
  390:       count: 0,
  391:       attempts: 0,
  392:     };
  393:     settledAssetSourcesRef.current.clear();
  394:     setPhase("DIRTY");
  395:     setPhase("PLANNING");
  396: 
  397:     setPrimaryEvidenceCount(
  398:       maxPrimaryEvidenceCount,
  399:     );
  400: 
  401:     setKeepSignatureOnPrimary(
  402:       true,
  403:     );
  404:   }, [
  405:     resetKey,
  406:     maxPrimaryEvidenceCount,
  407:   ]);
  408: 
  409:   const primaryPage =
  410:     useMemo(
  411:       () =>
  412:         makePrimaryPage(
  413:           page,
  414:           evidenceBlock,
  415:           primaryEvidenceCount,
  416:           keepSignatureOnPrimary,
  417:         ),
  418:       [
  419:         page,
  420:         evidenceBlock,
  421:         primaryEvidenceCount,
  422:         keepSignatureOnPrimary,
  423:       ],
  424:     );
  425: 
  426:   const signatureOverflowPage =
  427:     useMemo(
  428:       () =>
  429:         !keepSignatureOnPrimary &&
  430:         hasSignature &&
  431:         page
  432:           ? makeSignatureOverflowPage(
  433:               page,
  434:               signatureBlocks,
  435:             )
  436:           : null,
  437:       [
  438:         page,
  439:         signatureBlocks,
  440:         hasSignature,
  441:         keepSignatureOnPrimary,
  442:       ],
  443:     );
  444: 
  445:   const hasSignatureOverflowPage = Boolean(
  446:     signatureOverflowPage,
  447:   );
  448: 
  449:   const autoEvidencePhysicalPages = useMemo(
  450:     () =>
  451:       evidenceAutoEnabled
  452:         ? buildAutoEvidencePhysicalPages({
  453:             activePage: page,
  454:             previewCase,
  455:             primaryEvidenceCount,
  456:           })
  457:         : [],
  458:     [
  459:       evidenceAutoEnabled,
  460:       page,
  461:       previewCase,
  462:       primaryEvidenceCount,
  463:     ],
  464:   );
  465: 
  466:   const composedPhysicalPages = useMemo(
  467:     () => [
  468:       primaryPage,
  469:       ...autoEvidencePhysicalPages.map(({ page: evidencePage }) => evidencePage),
  470:       ...(signatureOverflowPage ? [signatureOverflowPage] : []),
  471:     ].filter((physicalPage): physicalPage is ReportPhysicalPageModel =>
  472:       Boolean(physicalPage),
  473:     ),
  474:     [autoEvidencePhysicalPages, primaryPage, signatureOverflowPage],
  475:   );
  476: 
  477:   useEffect(() => {
  478:     if (
  479:       planningPhase !== "FROZEN" ||
  480:       !page?.id ||
  481:       !onCompositionReady
  482:     ) {
  483:       return;
  484:     }
  485: 
  486:     onCompositionReady({
  487:       corePhysicalPageId: page.id,
  488:       pages: composedPhysicalPages,
  489:     });
  490:   }, [
  491:     composedPhysicalPages,
  492:     onCompositionReady,
  493:     page?.id,
  494:     planningPhase,
  495:   ]);
  496: 
  497:   useEffect(() => {
  498:     const host =
  499:       hostRef.current;
  500: 
  501:     if (!host) {
  502:       return;
  503:     }
  504: 
  505:     let frame:
  506:       | number
  507:       | null = null;
  508: 
  509:     let disposed = false;
  510:     let observer: MutationObserver | null = null;
  511: 
  512:     const resetStability = () => {
  513:       stabilityRef.current = {
  514:         signature: "",
  515:         count: 0,
  516:         attempts: stabilityRef.current.attempts,
  517:       };
  518:     };
  519: 
  520:     const recordStableResult = (signature: string) => {
  521:       const previous = stabilityRef.current;
  522: 
  523:       stabilityRef.current =
  524:         previous.signature === signature
  525:           ? {
  526:               signature,
  527:               count: previous.count + 1,
  528:               attempts: previous.attempts + 1,
  529:             }
  530:           : {
  531:               signature,
  532:               count: 1,
  533:               attempts: previous.attempts + 1,
  534:             };
  535: 
  536:       if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "development") {
  537:         host.dataset.reportComposerLayoutFingerprint =
  538:           getFingerprintPrefix(signature);
  539:         host.dataset.reportComposerStablePasses = String(
  540:           stabilityRef.current.count,
  541:         );
  542:         host.dataset.reportComposerAttempts = String(
  543:           stabilityRef.current.attempts,
  544:         );
  545:       }
  546: 
  547:       if (
  548:         stabilityRef.current.count >= 2 ||
  549:         stabilityRef.current.attempts >=
  550:           MAX_PAGE_COMPOSITION_ATTEMPTS
  551:       ) {
  552:         finalizedPlacementRef.current = {
  553:           semanticInputFingerprint: resetKey,
  554:           layoutResultFingerprint: signature,
  555:           primaryEvidenceCount,
  556:           keepSignatureOnPrimary,
  557:         };
  558:         observer?.disconnect();
  559:         setPhase("READY");
  560: 
  561:         queueMicrotask(() => {
  562:           if (
  563:             !disposed &&
  564:             planningPhaseRef.current === "READY" &&
  565:             finalizedPlacementRef.current
  566:               ?.semanticInputFingerprint === resetKey
  567:           ) {
  568:             setPhase("FROZEN");
  569:           }
  570:         });
  571:         return true;
  572:       }
  573: 
  574:       setPhase("STABILIZING");
  575:       return false;
  576:     };
  577: 
  578:     const inspect = () => {
  579:       if (isTerminalPhase()) {
  580:         return;
  581:       }
  582: 
  583:       if (frame !== null) {
  584:         window.cancelAnimationFrame(
  585:           frame,
  586:         );
  587:       }
  588: 
  589:       frame =
  590:         window.requestAnimationFrame(
  591:           () => {
  592:             frame = null;
  593: 
  594:             if (disposed) {
  595:               return;
  596:             }
  597: 
  598:             const smartViewports = Array.from(
  599:               host.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
  600:                 ".report-smart-a4-content",
  601:               ),
  602:             );
  603: 
  604:             /*
  605:              * Always bind composition decisions to the PRIMARY A4 page.
  606:              *
  607:              * The composer may also render evidence/signature overflow
  608:              * pages. Using smartViewports[0] blindly can make footer and
  609:              * signature decisions against the wrong A4 viewport.
  610:              */
  611:             const primaryPageElement =
  612:               host.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
  613:                 ".pdf-report-page",
  614:               );
  615: 
  616:             const smartViewport =
  617:               primaryPageElement?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
  618:                 ".report-smart-a4-content",
  619:               ) ||
  620:               smartViewports[0];
  621: 
  622:             if (!smartViewport) {
  623:               const physicalPageCount =
  624:                 host.querySelectorAll(".pdf-report-page").length;
  625: 
  626:               if (physicalPageCount > 0) {
  627:                 const legacySignature = [
  628:                   resetKey,
  629:                   primaryEvidenceCount,
  630:                   keepSignatureOnPrimary,
  631:                   physicalPageCount,
  632:                   host.scrollHeight,
  633:                 ].join("::");
  634: 
  635:                 if (!recordStableResult(legacySignature)) {
  636:                   inspect();
  637:                 }
  638:               }
  639: 
  640:               return;
  641:             }
  642: 
  643:             if (
  644:               smartViewport.dataset.smartA4Phase !== "FROZEN"
  645:             ) {
  646:               setPhase("MEASURING");
  647:               return;
  648:             }
  649: 
  650:             /*
  651:              * Wait for a REAL Smart A4 planning pass.
  652:              */
  653:             const candidate =
  654:               smartViewport.dataset
  655:                 .smartA4Candidate;
  656: 
  657:             const severity =
  658:               smartViewport.dataset
  659:                 .smartA4Severity;
  660: 
  661:             if (
  662:               !candidate ||
  663:               !severity
  664:             ) {
  665:               setPhase("MEASURING");
  666:               resetStability();
  667:               return;
  668:             }
  669: 
  670:             const overflowState =
  671:               smartViewport.dataset
  672:                 .reportOverflow;
  673: 
  674:             /*
  675:              * Final physical footer verification.
  676:              *
  677:              * The primary page may have received evidence and
  678:              * signatures after regular physical packing.
  679:              * Therefore verify the COMPOSED page itself against
  680:              * its real footer before deciding to keep those
  681:              * special blocks on the primary page.
  682:              */
  683:             const primaryPhysicalPage =
  684:               primaryPageElement ||
  685:               smartViewport.closest<HTMLElement>(
  686:                 ".pdf-report-page",
  687:               );
  688: 
  689:             const primaryFooter =
  690:               primaryPhysicalPage?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
  691:                 "[data-report-page-footer]",
  692:               ) || null;
  693: 
  694:             const footerSafetyGapPx =
  695:               2 * (96 / 25.4);
  696: 
  697:             const footerBoundaryTop =
  698:               primaryFooter
  699:                 ? primaryFooter.getBoundingClientRect().top -
  700:                   footerSafetyGapPx
  701:                 : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  702: 
  703:             const primarySmartBlocks =
  704:               primaryPhysicalPage
  705:                 ? Array.from(
  706:                     primaryPhysicalPage.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
  707:                       '[data-report-smart-block], [data-report-priority-block="signature"], [data-report-design-signature-block]',
  708:                     ),
  709:                   )
  710:                 : [];
  711: 
  712:             const crossesPhysicalFooter =
  713:               primaryFooter !== null &&
  714:               primarySmartBlocks.some((blockElement) => {
  715:                 const rect =
  716:                   blockElement.getBoundingClientRect();
  717: 
  718:                 return (
  719:                   rect.height > 0 &&
  720:                   rect.bottom > footerBoundaryTop + 1
  721:                 );
  722:               });
  723: 
  724:             /*
  725:              * Signature collision is a separate physical condition.
  726:              *
  727:              * If everything else fits but the signature crosses the
  728:              * footer, move the signature itself. Do NOT unnecessarily
  729:              * remove evidence first.
  730:              */
  731:             const primarySignatureBlocks =
  732:               primaryPhysicalPage
  733:                 ? Array.from(
  734:                     primaryPhysicalPage.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
  735:                       '[data-report-priority-block="signature"], [data-report-design-signature-block]',
  736:                     ),
  737:                   )
  738:                 : [];
  739: 
  740:             const signatureCrossesPhysicalFooter =
  741:               primaryFooter !== null &&
  742:               primarySignatureBlocks.some((signatureElement) => {
  743:                 const rect =
  744:                   signatureElement.getBoundingClientRect();
  745: 
  746:                 return (
  747:                   rect.height > 0 &&
  748:                   rect.bottom > footerBoundaryTop + 1
  749:                 );
  750:               });
  751: 
  752:             const doesNotFit =
  753:               overflowState !==
  754:                 "fit" ||
  755:               crossesPhysicalFooter;
  756: 
  757:             const preferEvidenceMove =
  758:               smartViewport.dataset
  759:                 .smartA4PreferEvidenceMove ===
  760:               "true";
  761: 
  762:             const density =
  763:               smartViewport.dataset
  764:                 .reportDensity ||
  765:               "normal";
  766: 
  767:             const fieldLayout =
  768:               smartViewport.dataset
  769:                 .reportFieldLayout ||
  770:               "comfortable";
  771: 
  772:             const minimumSafeSelected =
  773:               density === "minimum-safe";
  774: 
  775:             const overflowPx =
  776:               Number(
  777:                 smartViewport.getAttribute(
  778:                   "data-smart-a4-overflow-px",
  779:                 ) || "0",
  780:               );
  781: 
  782:             const decisionSignature = [
  783:               getFingerprintPrefix(resetKey),
  784:               primaryEvidenceCount,
  785:               keepSignatureOnPrimary,
  786:               doesNotFit,
  787:               signatureCrossesPhysicalFooter,
  788:               preferEvidenceMove,
  789:               severity,
  790:               density,
  791:               fieldLayout,
  792:               candidate,
  793:               Number.isFinite(
  794:                 overflowPx,
  795:               )
  796:                 ? overflowPx
  797:                 : 0,
  798:             ].join("::");
  799: 
  800:             /*
  801:              * =================================================
  802:              * PHASE 1:
  803:              * MOVE EVIDENCE BEFORE SACRIFICING CORE CONTENT
  804:              * =================================================
  805:              *
  806:              * Reduce one evidence at a time.
  807:              *
  808:              * Every reduction triggers a fresh complete Smart A4
  809:              * multi-plan evaluation.
  810:              */
  811:             if (
  812:               evidenceAutoEnabled &&
  813:               primaryEvidenceCount > 0 &&
  814:               !signatureCrossesPhysicalFooter &&
  815:               (
  816:                 doesNotFit ||
  817:                 preferEvidenceMove ||
  818:                 minimumSafeSelected
  819:               )
  820:             ) {
  821:               if (
  822:                 lastDecisionRef.current === decisionSignature
  823:               ) {
  824:                 return;
  825:               }
  826: 
  827:               lastDecisionRef.current = decisionSignature;
  828:               resetStability();
  829:               setPhase("MEASURING");
  830: 
  831:               setPrimaryEvidenceCount(
  832:                 (current) =>
  833:                   Math.max(
  834:                     0,
  835:                     current - 1,
  836:                   ),
  837:               );
  838: 
  839:               return;
  840:             }
  841: 
  842:             /*
  843:              * =================================================
  844:              * PHASE 2:
  845:              * SIGNATURE OVERFLOW FALLBACK
  846:              * =================================================
  847:              *
  848:              * Only after:
  849:              *
  850:              * - all movable evidence has left the primary page
  851:              * - Smart A4 still says the page does not fit
  852:              *
  853:              * do we move signature to a dedicated final page.
  854:              *
  855:              * We NEVER move regular narrative/fields just because
  856:              * the signature itself is the final obstruction.
  857:              */
  858:             if (
  859:               hasSignature &&
  860:               keepSignatureOnPrimary &&
  861:               (
  862:                 signatureCrossesPhysicalFooter ||
  863:                 (
  864:                   doesNotFit &&
  865:                   primaryEvidenceCount <= 0
  866:                 )
  867:               )
  868:             ) {
  869:               if (
  870:                 lastDecisionRef.current === decisionSignature
  871:               ) {
  872:                 return;
  873:               }
  874: 
  875:               lastDecisionRef.current = decisionSignature;
  876:               resetStability();
  877:               setPhase("MEASURING");
  878: 
  879:               setKeepSignatureOnPrimary(
  880:                 false,
  881:               );
  882: 
  883:               return;
  884:             }
  885: 
  886:             if (
  887:               minimumSafeSelected &&
  888:               primaryEvidenceCount <= 0 &&
  889:               hasSignature &&
  890:               keepSignatureOnPrimary
  891:             ) {
  892:               if (lastDecisionRef.current === decisionSignature) {
  893:                 return;
  894:               }
  895: 
  896:               lastDecisionRef.current = decisionSignature;
  897:               resetStability();
  898:               setPhase("MEASURING");
  899:               setKeepSignatureOnPrimary(false);
  900:               return;
  901:             }
  902: 
  903:             /*
  904:              * If signature has moved and core content now fits,
  905:              * planning is stable.
  906:              *
  907:              * If core content itself still does not fit,
  908:              * Smart Physical Engine owns that pagination problem;
  909:              * do not hide/truncate anything here.
  910:              */
  911:             const allSmartLayoutsFrozen = smartViewports.every(
  912:               (viewport) =>
  913:                 viewport.dataset.smartA4Phase === "FROZEN",
  914:             );
  915:             const allSmartLayoutsFit = smartViewports.every(
  916:               (viewport) => viewport.dataset.reportOverflow === "fit",
  917:             );
  918: 
  919:             const unsettledImages = Array.from(
  920:               host.querySelectorAll<HTMLImageElement>("img"),
  921:             ).filter((image) => !image.complete);
  922: 
  923:             if (
  924:               !allSmartLayoutsFrozen ||
  925:               !allSmartLayoutsFit ||
  926:               unsettledImages.length > 0 ||
  927:               !fontsSettledRef.current
  928:             ) {
  929:               setPhase("MEASURING");
  930:               return;
  931:             }
  932: 
  933:             const finalSignature =
  934:               createSemanticInputFingerprint({
  935:                 decisionSignature,
  936:                 evidenceCount: validEvidences.length,
  937:                 pageCount:
  938:                   host.querySelectorAll(".pdf-report-page").length,
  939:                 hasSignatureOverflowPage:
  940:                   hasSignatureOverflowPage,
  941:                 smartLayouts: smartViewports.map((viewport) => ({
  942:                   candidate:
  943:                     viewport.dataset.smartA4Candidate || "",
  944:                   fieldLayout:
  945:                     viewport.dataset.reportFieldLayout || "comfortable",
  946:                   mode:
  947:                     viewport.dataset.reportDensity || "normal",
  948:                   overflowPx: roundLayoutMetric(
  949:                     Number(viewport.dataset.smartA4OverflowPx || "0"),
  950:                   ),
  951:                 })),
  952:               });
  953: 
  954:             if (!recordStableResult(finalSignature)) {
  955:               inspect();
  956:             }
  957:           },
  958:         );
  959:     };
  960: 
  961:     observer =
  962:       new MutationObserver(
  963:         inspect,
  964:       );
  965: 
  966:     observer.observe(
  967:       host,
  968:       {
  969:         subtree: true,
  970:         childList: true,
  971:         attributes: true,
  972: 
  973:         attributeFilter: [
  974:           "data-smart-a4-phase",
  975:         ],
  976:       },
  977:     );
  978: 
  979:     inspect();
  980: 
  981:     const handleAssetLoad = (event: Event) => {
  982:       if (isTerminalPhase()) {
  983:         return;
  984:       }
  985: 
  986:       const image =
  987:         event.target instanceof HTMLImageElement
  988:           ? event.target
  989:           : null;
  990:       const source = image?.currentSrc || image?.src || "";
  991: 
  992:       if (
  993:         source &&
  994:         settledAssetSourcesRef.current.has(source)
  995:       ) {
  996:         return;
  997:       }
  998: 
  999:       if (source) {
 1000:         settledAssetSourcesRef.current.add(source);
 1001:       }
 1002: 
 1003:       inspect();
 1004:     };
 1005: 
 1006:     host.addEventListener("load", handleAssetLoad, true);
 1007:     host.addEventListener("error", handleAssetLoad, true);
 1008: 
 1009:     const fonts = document.fonts;
 1010:     if (!fonts) {
 1011:       fontsSettledRef.current = true;
 1012:       inspect();
 1013:     } else if (fontsSettledRef.current) {
 1014:       inspect();
 1015:     } else {
 1016:       void fonts.ready
 1017:         .then(() => {
 1018:           if (!disposed && !fontsSettledRef.current) {
 1019:             fontsSettledRef.current = true;
 1020:             inspect();
 1021:           }
 1022:         })
 1023:         .catch(() => {
 1024:           if (!disposed) {
 1025:             fontsSettledRef.current = true;
 1026:             inspect();
 1027:           }
 1028:         });
 1029:     }
 1030: 
 1031:     return () => {
 1032:       disposed = true;
 1033:       observer?.disconnect();
 1034:       host.removeEventListener("load", handleAssetLoad, true);
 1035:       host.removeEventListener("error", handleAssetLoad, true);
 1036: 
 1037:       if (frame !== null) {
 1038:         window.cancelAnimationFrame(
 1039:           frame,
 1040:         );
 1041:       }
 1042:     };
 1043:   }, [
 1044:     primaryEvidenceCount,
 1045:     keepSignatureOnPrimary,
 1046:     evidenceAutoEnabled,
 1047:     hasSignature,
 1048:     hasSignatureOverflowPage,
 1049:     resetKey,
 1050:     validEvidences.length,
 1051:   ]);
 1052: 
 1053:   const compositionSemanticFingerprint =
 1054:     createSemanticInputFingerprint({
 1055:       keepSignatureOnPrimary,
 1056:       primaryEvidenceCount,
 1057:       semanticInputFingerprint: resetKey,
 1058:     });
 1059: 
 1060:   const developmentDiagnostics =
 1061:     process.env.NODE_ENV === "development"
 1062:       ? {
 1063:           "data-report-composer-semantic-fingerprint":
 1064:             getFingerprintPrefix(resetKey),
 1065:           "data-report-composer-invalidation-reason":
 1066:             invalidationReason,
 1067:         }
 1068:       : {};
 1069: 
 1070:   return (
 1071:     <div
 1072:       ref={hostRef}
 1073:       data-report-smart-page-composer
 1074:       data-report-smart-page-phase={planningPhase}
 1075:       data-report-primary-evidence-count={
 1076:         primaryEvidenceCount
 1077:       }
 1078:       data-report-total-evidence-count={
 1079:         validEvidences.length
 1080:       }
 1081:       data-report-signature-on-primary={
 1082:         String(
 1083:           keepSignatureOnPrimary,
 1084:         )
 1085:       }
 1086:       {...developmentDiagnostics}
 1087:     >
 1088:       <ReportSmartSemanticFingerprintProvider
 1089:         fingerprint={compositionSemanticFingerprint}
 1090:       >
 1091:       {/*
 1092:        * ======================================================
 1093:        * PRIMARY PHYSICAL PAGE
 1094:        * ======================================================
 1095:        */}
 1096:       {renderMode === "stack" ||
 1097:       !activePhysicalPageId ||
 1098:       primaryPage?.id === activePhysicalPageId ? (
 1099:         <A4DesignPage
 1100:           designId={designId}
 1101:           page={primaryPage}
 1102:           context={context}
 1103:           previewCase={previewCase}
 1104:           pageLabel={pageLabel}
 1105:         />
 1106:       ) : null}
 1107: 
 1108:       {/*
 1109:        * ======================================================
 1110:        * EVIDENCE OVERFLOW
 1111:        * ======================================================
 1112:        *
 1113:        * AutoEvidencePages starts exactly after the count consumed
 1114:        * by the primary page.
 1115:        */}
 1116:       {evidenceAutoEnabled ? (
 1117:         <AutoEvidencePages
 1118:           designId={designId}
 1119:           activePage={page}
 1120:           context={context}
 1121:           previewCase={previewCase}
 1122:           primaryEvidenceCount={
 1123:             primaryEvidenceCount
 1124:           }
 1125:           preparedPages={autoEvidencePhysicalPages}
 1126:           activePhysicalPageId={activePhysicalPageId}
 1127:           renderMode={renderMode}
 1128:         />
 1129:       ) : null}
 1130: 
 1131:       {/*
 1132:        * ======================================================
 1133:        * SIGNATURE OVERFLOW
 1134:        * ======================================================
 1135:        *
 1136:        * This exists only when:
 1137:        * - evidence count already reached zero
 1138:        * - primary page still does not fit
 1139:        * - signature was the remaining protected block
 1140:        */}
 1141:       {signatureOverflowPage &&
 1142:       (renderMode === "stack" ||
 1143:         signatureOverflowPage.id === activePhysicalPageId) ? (
 1144:         <div
 1145:           className={
 1146:             renderMode === "stack"
 1147:               ? "mt-6 print:mt-0 print:break-before-page"
 1148:               : ""
 1149:           }
 1150:           data-report-signature-overflow-page
 1151:         >
 1152:           <A4DesignPage
 1153:             designId={designId}
 1154:             page={
 1155:               signatureOverflowPage
 1156:             }
 1157:             context={context}
 1158:             previewCase={previewCase}
 1159:             pageLabel="اعتماد التقرير"
 1160:           />
 1161:         </div>
 1162:       ) : null}
 1163:       </ReportSmartSemanticFingerprintProvider>
 1164:     </div>
 1165:   );
 1166: }

FILE: components\report-engine\design-renderers\smart-layout\report-smart-table-pagination.ts
============================================================
    1: import { paginateSmartReportTable } from "@/lib/report-engine/report-structured-table-extractor";
    2: import type { SmartReportTable } from "@/lib/report-engine/smart-report-types";
    3: 
    4: const COMMITTEE_ITEMS_SOURCE_FIELD_KEY = "committee_items";
    5: 
    6: export type LogicalReportBlock = {
    7:   id?: string;
    8:   kind?: string;
    9:   title?: string;
   10:   sourceBlockId?: string;
   11:   sourceTableId?: string;
   12:   sourceFieldKey?: string;
   13:   visible?: boolean;
   14:   placement?: string;
   15:   settings?: Record<string, unknown>;
   16:   signatures?: unknown[];
   17:   evidenceStartIndex?: number;
   18:   columns?: unknown[];
   19:   columnWidths?: unknown[];
   20:   rows?: unknown[];
   21:   rowMetadata?: Array<{ gender?: string | null }>;
   22:   tableSettings?: Record<string, unknown>;
   23:   [key: string]: unknown;
   24: };
   25: 
   26: function text(value: unknown) {
   27:   return String(value ?? "").trim();
   28: }
   29: 
   30: function isCommitteeItemsTable(block: LogicalReportBlock) {
   31:   return (
   32:     text(block.sourceFieldKey).toLowerCase() ===
   33:     COMMITTEE_ITEMS_SOURCE_FIELD_KEY
   34:   );
   35: }
   36: 
   37: /**
   38:  * The estimated paginator keeps large tables row-safe. When two estimated
   39:  * committee chunks are candidates for the same measured A4, combine them so
   40:  * the real DOM measurement decides whether they fit as one continuous table.
   41:  */
   42: export function mergeAdjacentCommitteeItemsTableBlocks(
   43:   blocks: LogicalReportBlock[],
   44: ): LogicalReportBlock[] {
   45:   return blocks.reduce<LogicalReportBlock[]>((merged, block) => {
   46:     const previous = merged[merged.length - 1];
   47:     const sameCommitteeTable =
   48:       previous &&
   49:       isCommitteeItemsTable(previous) &&
   50:       isCommitteeItemsTable(block) &&
   51:       text(previous.sourceBlockId) !== "" &&
   52:       text(previous.sourceBlockId) === text(block.sourceBlockId);
   53: 
   54:     if (!sameCommitteeTable) {
   55:       merged.push(block);
   56:       return merged;
   57:     }
   58: 
   59:     merged[merged.length - 1] = {
   60:       ...previous,
   61:       rows: [
   62:         ...(Array.isArray(previous.rows) ? previous.rows : []),
   63:         ...(Array.isArray(block.rows) ? block.rows : []),
   64:       ],
   65:       rowMetadata: [
   66:         ...(Array.isArray(previous.rowMetadata) ? previous.rowMetadata : []),
   67:         ...(Array.isArray(block.rowMetadata) ? block.rowMetadata : []),
   68:       ],
   69:     };
   70: 
   71:     return merged;
   72:   }, []);
   73: }
   74: 
   75: /**
   76:  * Splits only table rows into generic continuation blocks. It never creates
   77:  * logical or physical pages; SmartPhysicalReportComposer still performs the
   78:  * real DOM-driven A4 packing for every resulting block.
   79:  */
   80: export function paginateStructuredTableBlock(
   81:   block: LogicalReportBlock,
   82: ): LogicalReportBlock[] {
   83:   if (block.kind !== "structured-table") {
   84:     return [block];
   85:   }
   86: 
   87:   const columns = Array.isArray(block.columns)
   88:     ? block.columns.map((column) => text(column))
   89:     : [];
   90:   const rows = Array.isArray(block.rows) ? block.rows : [];
   91: 
   92:   if (!columns.length || !rows.length) {
   93:     return [block];
   94:   }
   95: 
   96:   const sourceBlockId = text(block.sourceBlockId) || text(block.id) || "structured-table";
   97:   const settings = block.tableSettings || {};
   98:   const committeeItemsTable = isCommitteeItemsTable(block);
   99:   const table: SmartReportTable = {
  100:     id: text(block.sourceTableId) || sourceBlockId,
  101:     sourceFieldKey: text(block.sourceFieldKey),
  102:     title: text(block.title),
  103:     columns: columns.map((label, index) => ({
  104:       key: `column_${index}`,
  105:       label,
  106:       ...(Number(block.columnWidths?.[index]) > 0
  107:         ? { width: Number(block.columnWidths?.[index]) }
  108:         : {}),
  109:     })),
  110:     rows: rows.map((rawRow, rowIndex) => {
  111:       const row = Array.isArray(rawRow) ? rawRow : [];
  112: 
  113:       return {
  114:         id: `${sourceBlockId}-row-${rowIndex + 1}`,
  115:         cells: Object.fromEntries(
  116:           columns.map((_, columnIndex) => [
  117:             `column_${columnIndex}`,
  118:             text(row[columnIndex]),
  119:           ]),
  120:         ),
  121:         metadata: {
  122:           gender: block.rowMetadata?.[rowIndex]?.gender || null,
  123:         },
  124:       };
  125:     }),
  126:     settings: {
  127:       repeatHeader: settings.repeatHeader !== false,
  128:       compact: Boolean(settings.compact),
  129:       stripedRows: settings.stripedRows !== false,
  130:       highlightFirstColumn: Boolean(settings.highlightFirstColumn),
  131:     },
  132:   };
  133: 
  134:   const chunks = paginateSmartReportTable(table);
  135: 
  136:   return chunks.map((chunk, index) => ({
  137:     ...block,
  138:     id: index === 0 ? block.id : `${sourceBlockId}-table-part-${index + 1}`,
  139:     // A true committee continuation repeats the column header, not the
  140:     // report-section title or a visible "- تكملة" heading.
  141:     title: committeeItemsTable ? table.title : chunk.title,
  142:     showTitle:
  143:       committeeItemsTable && index > 0
  144:         ? false
  145:         : block.showTitle,
  146:     sourceBlockId,
  147:     rows: chunk.rows.map((row) =>
  148:       chunk.columns.map((column) => row.cells[column.key] || ""),
  149:     ),
  150:     rowMetadata: chunk.rows.map((row) => ({
  151:       gender: row.metadata?.gender || null,
  152:     })),
  153:   }));
  154: }

   19: type TemplateStatus = "DRAFT" | "PUBLISHED" | "ARCHIVED";
   20: type TemplateScope = "GLOBAL" | "SERVICE" | "WORKFLOW" | "LOCATION";
   21: type PageKind = "content" | "recommendations" | "evidence" | "approval" | "custom";
   22: 
   23: 
   24: type BlockKind =
   25:   | "hero-title"
   26:   | "meta-strip"
   27:   | "plain-text"
   28:   | "section-text"
   29:   | "multi-paragraph"
   30:   | "highlight-note"
   31:   | "bullet-list"
   32:   | "dynamic-fields"
   33:   | "evidence-gallery"
   34:   | "closing-note";
   35: 
   36: type BlockVariant =
   37:   | "hero"
   38:   | "plain"
   39:   | "card"
   40:   | "soft"
   41:   | "highlight"
   42:   | "outline"
   43:   | "quote";
   44: type BlockPlacement =
   45:   | "flow"
   46:   | "top"
   47:   | "middle"
   48:   | "bottom"
   49:   | "top-right"
   50:   | "top-center"
   51:   | "top-left"
   52:   | "middle-right"
   53:   | "middle-center"
   54:   | "middle-left"
   55:   | "bottom-right"
   56:   | "bottom-center"
   57:   | "bottom-left";
   58: 
   59: type TextSource = "manual" | "library" | "workflow";
   60: 
   61: type EvidenceLayout = "ONE_PER_PAGE" | "TWO_PER_PAGE" | "GRID_2X2" | "ATTACHMENT_LIST";
   62: type EvidenceFit = "contain" | "cover";
   63: type EvidenceEmptyBehavior = "hide" | "message";
   64: type EvidenceAspectRatio = "LANDSCAPE_4_3" | "LANDSCAPE_16_9" | "PORTRAIT_3_4" | "SQUARE_1_1";
   65: 
   66: type StudioTextSnippet = {
   67:   id: string;
   68:   title: string;
   69:   category: "مقدمة" | "هدف" | "إجراء" | "نتيجة" | "توصية" | "خاتمة";
   70:   content: string;
   71: };

   73: type StudioBlock = {
   74:   id: string;
   75:   kind: BlockKind;
   76:   title: string;
   77:   content: string;
   78:   variant: BlockVariant;
   79:   source: TextSource;

   81:   boundFieldKey?: string;
   82:   hideWhenMissing?: boolean;
   83:   showTitle: boolean;
   84:   showMeta: boolean;
   85:   align: "right" | "center";
   86:   placement: BlockPlacement;
   87:   titleFontSize?: "xs" | "sm" | "base" | "lg" | "xl" | "2xl";
   88:   contentFontSize?: "xs" | "sm" | "base" | "lg" | "xl";
   89:   fieldLabelFontSize?: "xs" | "sm" | "base" | "lg";
   90:   fieldValueFontSize?: "xs" | "sm" | "base" | "lg" | "xl";
   91:   evidenceLayout?: EvidenceLayout;
   92:   evidenceFit?: EvidenceFit;
   93:   evidenceAspectRatio?: EvidenceAspectRatio;
   94:   evidenceShowCaptions?: boolean;
   95:   evidenceAutoCreatePages?: boolean;
   96:   evidenceEmptyBehavior?: EvidenceEmptyBehavior;
   97:   evidenceStartIndex?: number;
   98: };

  332: function makeId(prefix: string) {
  333:   return `${prefix}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`;
  334: }
  335: 
  336: function createBlock(kind: BlockKind): StudioBlock {
  337:   const item = blockLibrary.find((block) => block.kind === kind) || blockLibrary[2];
  338: 
  339:   return {
  340:     id: makeId(kind),
  341:     kind: item.kind,
  342:     title: item.title,
  343:     content: item.defaultContent,
  344:     variant: item.defaultVariant,
  345:     source: "manual",
  346:     boundFieldKey: undefined,
  347:     hideWhenMissing: false,
  348:     showTitle: item.kind !== "plain-text" && item.kind !== "hero-title",
  349:     showMeta: item.kind === "hero-title" || item.kind === "meta-strip",
  350:     align: item.defaultAlign || "right",
  351:     placement: item.kind === "hero-title" ? "middle-center" : "flow",
  352:     titleFontSize: "base",
  353:     contentFontSize: "base",
  354:     fieldLabelFontSize: "sm",
  355:     fieldValueFontSize: "base",
  356:     evidenceLayout: item.kind === "evidence-gallery" ? "TWO_PER_PAGE" : undefined,
  357:     evidenceFit: item.kind === "evidence-gallery" ? "contain" : undefined,
  358:     evidenceAspectRatio: item.kind === "evidence-gallery" ? "LANDSCAPE_4_3" : undefined,
  359:     evidenceShowCaptions: item.kind === "evidence-gallery" ? true : undefined,
  360:     evidenceAutoCreatePages: item.kind === "evidence-gallery" ? true : undefined,
  361:     evidenceEmptyBehavior: item.kind === "evidence-gallery" ? "message" : undefined,
  362:   };
  363: }
  364: 
  365: function createPage(kind: PageKind, index: number): StudioPage {
  366:   const presets: Record<PageKind, Omit<StudioPage, "id" | "blocks"> & { blockKinds: BlockKind[] }> = {
  367:     content: {
  368:       kind: "content",
  369:       title: index === 1 ? "صفحة العنوان والمحتوى" : `صفحة محتوى ${index}`,
  370:       description: "صفحة A4 رسمية تحتوي بلوكات نصية ذكية.",
  371:       blockKinds: index === 1 ? ["hero-title", "meta-strip", "section-text"] : ["section-text"],
  372:     },
  373:     recommendations: {
  374:       kind: "recommendations",
  375:       title: "التوصيات والنتائج",
  376:       description: "صفحة مخصصة لعرض النتائج والتوصيات.",
  377:       blockKinds: ["highlight-note", "bullet-list"],
  378:     },
  379:     evidence: {
  380:       kind: "evidence",
  381:       title: "الشواهد والمرفقات",
  382:       description: "صفحة مستقلة لعرض الشواهد وتقسيمها تلقائيًا على صفحات A4 عند كثرتها.",
  383:       blockKinds: ["evidence-gallery"],
  384:     },
  385:     approval: {
  386:       kind: "approval",
  387:       title: "الاعتماد والتوقيع",
  388:       description: "صفحة ختامية للاعتماد.",
  389:       blockKinds: ["closing-note"],
  390:     },
  391:     custom: {
  392:       kind: "custom",
  393:       title: `صفحة مخصصة ${index}`,
  394:       description: "صفحة مخصصة يمكن بناؤها بالبلوكات.",
  395:       blockKinds: ["plain-text"],
  396:     },
  397:   };
  398: 
  399:   const preset = presets[kind];
  400: 
  401:   return {
  402:     id: makeId(`page-${kind}`),
  403:     kind: preset.kind,
  404:     title: preset.title,
  405:     description: preset.description,
  406:     blocks: preset.blockKinds.map(createBlock),
  407:   };
  408: }

  429: function buildRuntimeContext(template: StudioTemplate, previewCase: PreviewCaseData | null) {
  430:   const values: Record<string, string> = {};
  431: 
  432:   for (const item of previewCase?.values || []) {
  433:     if (item.fieldKey) {
  434:       values[`field.${item.fieldKey}`] = item.value || "";
  435:     }
  436:   }
  437: 
  438:   return {
  439:     "identity.ministryName": "وزارة التعليم",
  440:     "identity.educationDepartment": "الإدارة العامة للتعليم",
  441:     "identity.educationOffice": "مكتب التعليم",
  442:     "identity.schoolName": "اسم المدرسة",
  443:     "identity.counselorName": "اسم الموجه/الموجهة",
  444:     "identity.principalName": "اسم مدير/مديرة المدرسة",
  445: 
  446:     "template.name": template.name,
  447:     "template.description": template.description,
  448: 
  449:     "case.id": previewCase?.caseId || "CASE-ID",
  450:     "case.title": previewCase?.title || "عنوان التقارير",
  451:     "case.status": previewCase?.status || "مسودة",
  452:     "case.createdAt": formatDate(previewCase?.createdAt) || "1447/01/01 هـ",
  453:     "case.updatedAt": formatDate(previewCase?.updatedAt) || "1447/01/01 هـ",
  454: 
  455:     "service.name": previewCase?.serviceName || getServiceName(template.serviceSlug) || "الخدمة الإرشادية",
  456:     "service.slug": previewCase?.serviceSlug || template.serviceSlug || "general",
  457: 
  458:     "student.name": previewCase?.student?.name || "اسم الطالب/الطالبة",
  459:     "student.grade": previewCase?.student?.grade || "الصف",
  460:     "student.classroom": previewCase?.student?.classroom || "الفصل",
  461:     "student.stage": previewCase?.student?.stage || "المرحلة",
  462:     "student.guardianName": previewCase?.student?.guardianName || "اسم ولي الأمر",
  463:     "student.guardianPhone": previewCase?.student?.guardianPhone || "رقم ولي الأمر",
  464: 
  465:     "evidence.count": String(
  466:       filterValidReportEvidenceItems(previewCase?.evidences || [], {
  467:         allowSampleEvidence: !previewCase?.caseId,
  468:       }).length,
  469:     ),
  470: 
  471:     ...values,
  472:   };
  473: }
  474: 
  475: 
  476: 
  477: function getServiceName(slug?: string) {
  478:   return SERVICE_OPTIONS.find((service) => service.slug === slug)?.name || "";
  479: }
  480: 
  481: function formatDate(value?: string) {
  482:   if (!value) return "";
  483: 
  484:   try {
  485:     return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("ar-SA", {
  486:       dateStyle: "medium",
  487:     }).format(new Date(value));
  488:   } catch {
  489:     return value;
  490:   }
  491: }
  492: 
  493: function renderText(text: string, context: Record<string, string>) {
  494:   return text.replace(/{{\s*([^}]+)\s*}}/g, (_, key: string) => {
  495:     const cleanKey = key.trim();
  496:     return context[cleanKey] || `{{${cleanKey}}}`;
  497:   });
  498: }
  499: 
  500: function splitLines(text: string) {
  501:   return text
  502:     .split(/\n+/)
  503:     .map((line) => line.trim())
  504:     .filter(Boolean);
  505: }
  506: 
  507: function splitParagraphs(text: string) {
  508:   return text
  509:     .split(/\n\s*\n/)
  510:     .map((line) => line.trim())
  511:     .filter(Boolean);
  512: }
  513: 
  514: function extractUsedWorkflowFieldKeys(template: StudioTemplate) {
  515:   const keys = new Set<string>();
  516:   const regex = /{{\s*field\.([^}\s]+)\s*}}/g;
  517: 
  518:   for (const page of template.pages) {
  519:     for (const block of page.blocks) {
  520:       const content = `${block.title}\n${block.content}`;
  521:       let match = regex.exec(content);
  522: 
  523:       while (match) {
  524:         keys.add(match[1]);
  525:         match = regex.exec(content);
  526:       }
  527:     }
  528:   }
  529: 
  530:   return keys;
  531: }
  532: 
  533: function groupWorkflowFields(fields: WorkflowFieldOption[]) {
  534:   const groups = new Map<string, WorkflowFieldOption[]>();
  535: 
  536:   for (const field of fields) {
  537:     const groupName = field.stepTitle || "حقول عامة";
  538:     const current = groups.get(groupName) || [];
  539:     current.push(field);
  540:     groups.set(groupName, current);
  541:   }
  542: 
  543:   return Array.from(groups.entries()).map(([stepTitle, items]) => ({
  544:     stepTitle,
  545:     fields: items,
  546:   }));
  547: }
  548: 
  549: function parseSavedTemplateJson(item: any) {
  550:   const raw = item?.templateJson ?? item?.content ?? null;
  551: 
  552:   if (!raw) return null;
  553: 
  554:   if (typeof raw === "string") {
  555:     try {
  556:       return JSON.parse(raw);
  557:     } catch {
  558:       return null;
  559:     }
  560:   }
  561: 
  562:   if (typeof raw === "object") {
  563:     return raw;
  564:   }
  565: 
  566:   return null;
  567: }
  568: 
  569: function hydrateStudioTemplateFromSavedItem(item: any): StudioTemplate {
  570:   const templateJson = parseSavedTemplateJson(item) || {};
  571:   const smartStudio = templateJson?.smartStudio || {};
  572:   const fallback = createInitialTemplate();
  573: 
  574:   const pages =
  575:     Array.isArray(smartStudio?.pages) && smartStudio.pages.length
  576:       ? smartStudio.pages
  577:       : Array.isArray(templateJson?.pages) && templateJson.pages.length
  578:         ? templateJson.pages.map((page: any, pageIndex: number) => ({
  579:             id: page.id || makeId("page-loaded"),
  580:             kind:
  581:               page.kind === "approval"
  582:                 ? "approval"
  583:                 : page.kind === "evidence"
  584:                   ? "evidence"
  585:                   : pageIndex === 0
  586:                     ? "content"
  587:                     : "custom",
  588:             title: page.title || `صفحة ${pageIndex + 1}`,
  589:             description: page.description || "صفحة مستوردة من قالب محفوظ.",
  590:             blocks: Array.isArray(page.blocks)
  591:               ? page.blocks.map((block: any) => ({
  592:                   id: block.id || makeId("block-loaded"),
  593:                   kind:
  594:                     block.settings?.smartBlockKind ||
  595:                     block.source?.fieldKey ||
  596:                     "section-text",
  597:                   title: block.title || block.customTitle || "بلوك",
  598:                   content:
  599:                     block.customContent ||
  600:                     block.content ||
  601:                     block.source?.description ||
  602:                     "",
  603:                   variant: block.settings?.style || "card",
  604:                   source:
  605:                     block.source?.source === "library" ||
  606:                     block.source?.source === "workflow"
  607:                       ? block.source.source
  608:                       : "manual",
  609:                   snippetId: block.settings?.snippetId || undefined,
  610:                   boundFieldKey: block.settings?.boundFieldKey || undefined,
  611:                   hideWhenMissing: Boolean(block.settings?.hideWhenMissing),
  612:                   showTitle: block.settings?.showTitle !== false,
  613:                   showMeta: Boolean(block.settings?.showMeta),
  614:                   align: block.settings?.align || "right",
  615:                   placement: block.settings?.placement || "flow",
  616:                   titleFontSize: block.settings?.titleFontSize || block.titleFontSize || "base",
  617:                   contentFontSize: block.settings?.contentFontSize || block.contentFontSize || "base",
  618:                   fieldLabelFontSize: block.settings?.fieldLabelFontSize || block.fieldLabelFontSize || "sm",
  619:                   fieldValueFontSize: block.settings?.fieldValueFontSize || block.fieldValueFontSize || "base",
  620:                   evidenceLayout: block.settings?.evidenceLayout || undefined,
  621:                   evidenceFit: block.settings?.evidenceFit || undefined,
  622:                   evidenceAspectRatio: block.settings?.evidenceAspectRatio || "LANDSCAPE_4_3",
  623:                   evidenceShowCaptions: block.settings?.evidenceShowCaptions !== false,
  624:                   evidenceAutoCreatePages: block.settings?.evidenceAutoCreatePages !== false,
  625:                   evidenceEmptyBehavior: block.settings?.evidenceEmptyBehavior || "message",
  626:                 }))
  627:               : [createBlock("section-text")],
  628:           }))
  629:         : fallback.pages;
  630: 
  631:   return {
  632:     ...fallback,
  633:     id: templateJson.id || item?.id || fallback.id,
  634:     designTemplateId:
  635:       templateJson.designTemplateId ||
  636:       smartStudio.designTemplateId ||
  637:       "ministry-form",
  638:     name: item?.name || templateJson.name || fallback.name,
  639:     description:
  640:       item?.description || templateJson.description || fallback.description,
  641:     status:
  642:       templateJson.status === "PUBLISHED" ||
  643:       templateJson.status === "ARCHIVED" ||
  644:       templateJson.status === "DRAFT"
  645:         ? templateJson.status
  646:         : item?.isActive === false
  647:           ? "ARCHIVED"
  648:           : "DRAFT",
  649:     scope:
  650:       templateJson.scope ||
  651:       templateJson?.workflowBinding?.scope ||
  652:       (item?.serviceSlug ? "SERVICE" : "GLOBAL"),
  653:     serviceSlug:
  654:       item?.serviceSlug ||
  655:       templateJson.serviceSlug ||
  656:       templateJson?.workflowBinding?.serviceSlug ||
  657:       undefined,
  658:     workflowSlug:
  659:       templateJson.workflowSlug ||
  660:       templateJson?.workflowBinding?.workflowSlug ||
  661:       undefined,
  662:     locationKey:
  663:       templateJson.locationKey ||
  664:       templateJson?.workflowBinding?.locationKey ||
  665:       undefined,
  666:     previewCaseId: templateJson.previewCaseId || "",
  667:     updatedAt:
  668:       item?.updatedAt ||
  669:       templateJson.updatedAt ||
  670:       new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
  671:     designConfig: {
  672:       header: normalizeReportHeaderSettings(
  673:         templateJson?.designConfig?.header ??
  674:           smartStudio?.designConfig?.header ??
  675:           DEFAULT_REPORT_HEADER_SETTINGS,
  676:       ),
  677:     },
  678:     pages,
  679:   } as StudioTemplate;
  680: }
  681: 
  682: export function ReportTemplateStudio() {
  683:   const [template, setTemplate] = useState<StudioTemplate>(() => createInitialTemplate());
  684:   const [activePageId, setActivePageId] = useState(() => template.pages[0]?.id || "");
  685:   const [selectedBlockId, setSelectedBlockId] = useState(() => template.pages[0]?.blocks[0]?.id || "");
  686:   const [savedTemplateId, setSavedTemplateId] = useState("");
  687:   const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  688:   const [testingCase, setTestingCase] = useState(false);
  689:   const [previewCase, setPreviewCase] = useState<PreviewCaseData | null>(null);
  690:   const [workflowFields, setWorkflowFields] = useState<WorkflowFieldOption[]>([]);
  691:   const [workflowMessage, setWorkflowMessage] = useState("اختر خدمة أو Workflow لعرض الحقول المتاحة.");
  692:   const [loadingWorkflow, setLoadingWorkflow] = useState(false);
  693:   const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState<{
  694:     type: "success" | "error" | "info";
  695:     message: string;
  696:   } | null>(null);
  697: 
  698:   const activePage = useMemo(
  699:     () => template.pages.find((page) => page.id === activePageId) || template.pages[0],
  700:     [template.pages, activePageId],
  701:   );
  702: 
  703:   const selectedBlock = useMemo(
  704:     () =>
  705:       activePage?.blocks.find((block) => block.id === selectedBlockId) ||
  706:       template.pages.flatMap((page) => page.blocks).find((block) => block.id === selectedBlockId) ||
  707:       null,
  708:     [activePage?.blocks, selectedBlockId, template.pages],
  709:   );
  710: 
  711:   const runtimeContext = useMemo(
  712:     () => buildRuntimeContext(template, previewCase),
  713:     [template, previewCase],
  714:   );
  715: 
  716:   useEffect(() => {
  717:     let isMounted = true;
  718: 
  719:     async function loadTemplateFromQuery() {
  720:       const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
  721:       const templateId = params.get("templateId");
  722:       const designTemplateId = params.get("designTemplateId");
  723: 
  724:       if (!templateId) {
  725:         if (
  726:           designTemplateId &&
  727:           isSelectableReportDesignId(designTemplateId)
  728:         ) {
  729:           setTemplate((current) => ({
  730:             ...current,
  731:             designTemplateId: designTemplateId as ReportDesignId,
  732:           }));
  733: 
  734:           setFeedback({
  735:             type: "success",
  736:             message: "تم تطبيق التصميم المختار داخل الاستديو.",
  737:           });
  738:         }
  739: 
  740:         return;
  741:       }
  742: 
  743:       try {
  744:         const cached = sessionStorage.getItem("template-studio-selected");
  745: 
  746:         if (cached) {
  747:           const cachedItem = JSON.parse(cached);
  748: 
  749:           if (String(cachedItem?.id || "") === templateId) {
  750:             const hydrated = hydrateStudioTemplateFromSavedItem(cachedItem);
  751: 
  752:             if (!isMounted) return;
  753: 
  754:             setTemplate(hydrated);
  755:             setSavedTemplateId(templateId);
  756:             setActivePageId(hydrated.pages[0]?.id || "");
  757:             setSelectedBlockId(hydrated.pages[0]?.blocks[0]?.id || "");
  758:             sessionStorage.removeItem("template-studio-selected");
  759: 
  760:             setFeedback({
  761:               type: "success",
  762:               message: "تم فتح القالب المحفوظ داخل الاستديو.",
  763:             });
  764: 
  765:             return;
  766:           }
  767:         }
  768: 
  769:         const response = await fetch(
  770:           `/api/dashboard/report-templates/${encodeURIComponent(templateId)}`,
  771:           { cache: "no-store" },
  772:         );
  773: 
  774:         const result = await response.json();
  775: 
  776:         if (!response.ok) {
  777:           throw new Error(result?.error || result?.message || "تعذر تحميل القالب.");
  778:         }
  779: 
  780:         const item = result?.template || result?.data || result;
  781:         const hydrated = hydrateStudioTemplateFromSavedItem(item);
  782: 
  783:         if (!isMounted) return;
  784: 
  785:         setTemplate(hydrated);
  786:         setSavedTemplateId(templateId);
  787:         setActivePageId(hydrated.pages[0]?.id || "");
  788:         setSelectedBlockId(hydrated.pages[0]?.blocks[0]?.id || "");
  789:         setFeedback({
  790:           type: "success",
  791:           message: "تم تحميل القالب المحفوظ داخل الاستديو.",
  792:         });
  793:       } catch (error) {
  794:         if (!isMounted) return;
  795: 
  796:         setFeedback({
  797:           type: "error",
  798:           message:
  799:             error instanceof Error
  800:               ? error.message
  801:               : "تعذر فتح القالب من المكتبة.",
  802:         });
  803:       }
  804:     }
  805: 
  806:     loadTemplateFromQuery();
  807: 
  808:     return () => {
  809:       isMounted = false;
  810:     };
  811:   }, []);
  812: 
  813:   const usedWorkflowFieldKeys = useMemo(
  814:     () => extractUsedWorkflowFieldKeys(template),
  815:     [template],
  816:   );
  817: 
  818:   useEffect(() => {
  819:     let isMounted = true;
  820: 
  821:     async function loadWorkflowFields() {
  822:       if (template.scope === "GLOBAL" || template.scope === "LOCATION") {
  823:         setWorkflowFields([]);
  824:         setWorkflowMessage("هذا القالب غير مربوط بخدمة أو Workflow حاليًا.");
  825:         return;
  826:       }
  827: 
  828:       if (!template.serviceSlug) {
  829:         setWorkflowFields([]);
  830:         setWorkflowMessage("اختر الخدمة أولًا حتى تظهر حقول الـ Workflow المرتبطة بها.");
  831:         return;
  832:       }
  833: 
  834:       try {
  835:         setLoadingWorkflow(true);
  836: 
  837:         const response = await fetch(
  838:           `/api/admin/report-templates/workflow-fields?serviceSlug=${encodeURIComponent(
  839:             template.serviceSlug,
  840:           )}`,
  841:           { cache: "no-store" },
  842:         );
  843: 
  844:         const result = await response.json();
  845: 
  846:         if (!isMounted) return;
  847: 
  848:         setWorkflowFields(Array.isArray(result?.fields) ? result.fields : []);
  849:         setWorkflowMessage(
  850:           result?.message ||
  851:             "تم جلب الحقول المتاحة. اختر المتغيرات المناسبة وأدرجها في البلوكات.",
  852:         );
  853:       } catch {
  854:         if (!isMounted) return;
  855: 
  856:         setWorkflowFields([]);
  857:         setWorkflowMessage("تعذر جلب حقول الـ Workflow. يمكن الاستمرار بالنصوص اليدوية مؤقتًا.");
  858:       } finally {
  859:         if (isMounted) {
  860:           setLoadingWorkflow(false);
  861:         }
  862:       }
  863:     }
  864: 
  865:     loadWorkflowFields();
  866: 
  867:     return () => {
  868:       isMounted = false;
  869:     };
  870:   }, [template.scope, template.serviceSlug, template.workflowSlug]);
  871: 
  872:   function updateTemplate(next: Partial<StudioTemplate>) {
  873:     setTemplate((current) => ({
  874:       ...current,
  875:       ...next,
  876:       updatedAt: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
  877:     }));
  878:   }
  879: 
  880:   function updatePage(pageId: string, updater: (page: StudioPage) => StudioPage) {
  881:     setTemplate((current) => ({
  882:       ...current,
  883:       updatedAt: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
  884:       pages: current.pages.map((page) => (page.id === pageId ? updater(page) : page)),
  885:     }));
  886:   }
  887: 
  888:   function updateBlock(blockId: string, updater: (block: StudioBlock) => StudioBlock) {
  889:     setTemplate((current) => ({
  890:       ...current,
  891:       updatedAt: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
  892:       pages: current.pages.map((page) => ({
  893:         ...page,
  894:         blocks: page.blocks.map((block) =>
  895:           block.id === blockId ? updater(block) : block,
  896:         ),
  897:       })),
  898:     }));
  899:   }
  900: 
  901:   function addPage(kind: PageKind) {
  902:     const page = createPage(kind, template.pages.length + 1);
  903: 
  904:     setTemplate((current) => ({
  905:       ...current,
  906:       updatedAt: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
  907:       pages: [...current.pages, page],
  908:     }));
  909: 
  910:     setActivePageId(page.id);
  911:     setSelectedBlockId(page.blocks[0]?.id || "");
  912:   }
  913: 
  914:   function removeActivePage() {
  915:     if (!activePage || template.pages.length <= 1) {
  916:       return;
  917:     }
  918: 
  919:     const remainingPages = template.pages.filter((page) => page.id !== activePage.id);
  920: 
  921:     setTemplate((current) => ({
  922:       ...current,
  923:       updatedAt: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
  924:       pages: remainingPages,
  925:     }));
  926: 
  927:     setActivePageId(remainingPages[0]?.id || "");
  928:     setSelectedBlockId(remainingPages[0]?.blocks[0]?.id || "");
  929:   }
  930: 
  931:   function addBlock(kind: BlockKind) {
  932:     if (!activePage) return;
  933: 
  934:     const block = createBlock(kind);
  935: 
  936:     updatePage(activePage.id, (page) => ({
  937:       ...page,
  938:       blocks: [...page.blocks, block],
  939:     }));
  940: 
  941:     setSelectedBlockId(block.id);
  942:   }
  943: 
  944:   function removeSelectedBlock() {
  945:     if (!selectedBlock || !activePage || activePage.blocks.length <= 1) {
  946:       return;
  947:     }
  948: 
  949:     const remaining = activePage.blocks.filter((block) => block.id !== selectedBlock.id);
  950: 
  951:     updatePage(activePage.id, (page) => ({
  952:       ...page,
  953:       blocks: remaining,
  954:     }));
  955: 
  956:     setSelectedBlockId(remaining[0]?.id || "");
  957:   }
  958: 
  959:   function moveBlock(direction: "up" | "down") {
  960:     if (!selectedBlock || !activePage) return;
  961: 
  962:     const index = activePage.blocks.findIndex((block) => block.id === selectedBlock.id);
  963:     if (index === -1) return;
  964: 
  965:     const targetIndex = direction === "up" ? index - 1 : index + 1;
  966:     if (targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= activePage.blocks.length) return;
  967: 
  968:     const nextBlocks = [...activePage.blocks];
  969:     const [block] = nextBlocks.splice(index, 1);
  970:     nextBlocks.splice(targetIndex, 0, block);
  971: 
  972:     updatePage(activePage.id, (page) => ({
  973:       ...page,
  974:       blocks: nextBlocks,
  975:     }));
  976:   }
  977: 
  978:   function applySnippet(snippetId: string) {
  979:     if (!selectedBlock) return;
  980: 
  981:     const snippet = textSnippets.find((item) => item.id === snippetId);
  982:     if (!snippet) return;
  983: 
  984:     updateBlock(selectedBlock.id, (block) => ({
  985:       ...block,
  986:       source: "library",
  987:       snippetId,
  988:       title: block.title || snippet.title,
  989:       content: snippet.content,
  990:     }));
  991:   }
  992: 
  993:   function insertVariable(variable: string) {
  994:     if (!selectedBlock) {
  995:       setFeedback({
  996:         type: "info",
  997:         message: "اختر بلوكًا أولًا حتى يتم إدراج المتغير داخله.",
  998:       });
  999:       return;
 1000:     }
 1001: 
 1002:     updateBlock(selectedBlock.id, (block) => ({
 1003:       ...block,
 1004:       content: `${block.content}${block.content.trim() ? " " : ""}${variable}`,
 1005:     }));
 1006:   }
 1007: 
 1008:   async function runCaseTest() {
 1009:     const caseId = template.previewCaseId.trim();
 1010: 
 1011:     if (!caseId) {
 1012:       setPreviewCase(null);
 1013:       setFeedback({
 1014:         type: "info",
 1015:         message: "لم يتم إدخال Case ID. سيتم عرض بيانات تجريبية داخل المعاينة.",
 1016:       });
 1017:       return;
 1018:     }
 1019: 
 1020:     try {
 1021:       setTestingCase(true);
 1022: 
 1023:       const response = await fetch(
 1024:         `/api/admin/report-templates/preview-case?caseId=${encodeURIComponent(
 1025:           caseId,
 1026:         )}`,
 1027:         { cache: "no-store" },
 1028:       );
 1029: 
 1030:       const result = await response.json();
 1031: 
 1032:       setPreviewCase(result?.data || null);
 1033:       setFeedback({
 1034:         type: result?.data ? "success" : "info",
 1035:         message:
 1036:           result?.message ||
 1037:           "تم تنفيذ الاختبار. إذا لم توجد حالة فعلية ستبقى المعاينة على بيانات تجريبية.",
 1038:       });
 1039:     } catch {
 1040:       setPreviewCase(null);
 1041:       setFeedback({
 1042:         type: "error",
 1043:         message: "تعذر اختبار Case ID. راجع الرقم أو جرّب لاحقًا.",
 1044:       });
 1045:     } finally {
 1046:       setTestingCase(false);
 1047:     }
 1048:   }
 1049: 
 1050:   function toReportTemplateJson(status: TemplateStatus) {
 1051:     return {
 1052:       id: template.id,
 1053:       name: template.name,
 1054:       description: template.description,
 1055:       scope: template.scope,
 1056:       serviceSlug: template.serviceSlug || undefined,
 1057:       workflowSlug: template.workflowSlug || undefined,
 1058:       locationKey: template.locationKey || undefined,
 1059:       status,
 1060:       documentType: template.documentType,
 1061:       designTemplateId: template.designTemplateId || "ministry-form",
 1062:       designPreset: "official-smart-multi-page",
 1063:       designConfig: template.designConfig,
 1064:       previewCaseId: template.previewCaseId,
 1065:       updatedAt: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
 1066:       officialLayout: {
 1067:         pageSize: "A4",
 1068:         ministryLogoOnly: true,
 1069:         headerLocked: true,
 1070:         identityLocked: true,
 1071:         multiPage: true,
 1072:       },
 1073:       workflowBinding: {
 1074:         scope: template.scope,
 1075:         serviceSlug: template.serviceSlug || null,
 1076:         workflowSlug: template.workflowSlug || null,
 1077:         locationKey: template.locationKey || null,
 1078:         fields: workflowFields,
 1079:         usedFieldKeys: Array.from(usedWorkflowFieldKeys),
 1080:       },
 1081:       pages: template.pages.map((page) => ({
 1082:         id: page.id,
 1083:         kind: page.kind === "approval" ? "approval" : page.kind === "evidence" ? "evidence" : "narrative",
 1084:         title: page.title,
 1085:         description: page.description,
 1086:         blocks: page.blocks.map((block) => ({
 1087:           id: block.id,
 1088:           kind: "custom-paragraph",
 1089:           title: block.title,
 1090:           customTitle: block.showTitle ? block.title : "",
 1091:           customContent: block.content,
 1092:           required: false,
 1093:           source: {
 1094:             source: block.source,
 1095:             label: block.source === "library" ? "مكتبة النصوص" : "كتابة مباشرة",
 1096:             description: "بلوك من الاستديو الرسمي الذكي متعدد الصفحات",
 1097:             fieldKey: block.kind,
 1098:           },
 1099:           settings: {
 1100:             smartBlockKind: block.kind,
 1101:             style: block.variant,
 1102:             showTitle: block.showTitle,
 1103:             showMeta: block.showMeta,
 1104:             align: block.align,
 1105:             placement: block.placement || "flow",
 1106:             titleFontSize: block.titleFontSize || "base",
 1107:             contentFontSize: block.contentFontSize || "base",
 1108:             fieldLabelFontSize: block.fieldLabelFontSize || "sm",
 1109:             fieldValueFontSize: block.fieldValueFontSize || "base",
 1110:             evidenceLayout: block.evidenceLayout || null,
 1111:             evidenceFit: block.evidenceFit || null,
 1112:             evidenceAspectRatio: block.evidenceAspectRatio || "LANDSCAPE_4_3",
 1113:             evidenceShowCaptions: block.evidenceShowCaptions !== false,
 1114:             evidenceAutoCreatePages: block.evidenceAutoCreatePages !== false,
 1115:             evidenceEmptyBehavior: block.evidenceEmptyBehavior || "message",
 1116:             snippetId: block.snippetId || null,
 1117:           },
 1118:         })),
 1119:       })),
 1120:       smartStudio: {
 1121:         version: 2,
 1122:         mode: "multi-page-workflow-aware",
 1123:         designTemplateId: template.designTemplateId || "ministry-form",
 1124:         designConfig: template.designConfig,
 1125:         pages: template.pages,
 1126:       },
 1127:     };
 1128:   }
 1129: 
 1130:   async function saveTemplate(nextStatus: TemplateStatus = template.status) {
 1131:     try {
 1132:       setSaving(true);
 1133: 
 1134:       const templateJson = toReportTemplateJson(nextStatus);
 1135: 
 1136:       const payload = {
 1137:         name: template.name || "القالب الرسمي الذكي",

 1151:         ? await fetch(`/api/dashboard/report-templates/${savedTemplateId}`, {
 1152:             method: "PATCH",

 1169:         throw new Error(result?.error || result?.message || "تعذر حفظ القالب.");

 2298: function WorkflowAwarenessPanel({
 2299:   template,

 2367:           <Metric label="المستخدم" value={`${usedCount}`} />

 2369:         </div>
 2370:       </div>
 2371:

 2398:         </div>
 2399:       ) : null}

 2416:               </div>
 2417:

 2419:                 {group.fields.map((field, fieldIndex) => {
 2420:                   const used = usedFieldKeys.has(field.key);
 2421:                   const variable = `{{field.${field.key}}}`;
 2422: 
 2423:                   return (
 2424:                     <div
 2425:                       key={`${field.stepTitle || "field"}-${field.key}-${fieldIndex}`}

 2428:                       <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
 2429:                         <div>

 2434:                             {variable}
 2435:                           </p>
 2436:                         </div>
 2437:

 2439:                           className={[

 2448:                       </div>

 2454:                       >
 2455:                         إدراج المتغير في البلوك المحدد
 2456:                       </button>
 2457:                     </div>
 2458:                   );
 2459:                 })}
 2460:               </div>
 2461:             </div>
 2462:           ))}
 2463:         </div>
 2464:       ) : null}
 2465:     </section>
 2466:   );

 2469: function Metric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
 2470:   return (
 2471:     <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-3 py-2">
 2472:       <p className="text-[10px] font-black text-slate-400">{label}</p>

 2474:     </div>
 2475:   );
