 2478: function HeaderSettingsPanel({
 2479:   settings,
 2480:   onChange,
 2481: }: {
 2482:   settings: ReportHeaderSettings;
 2483:   onChange: (settings: ReportHeaderSettings) => void;
 2484: }) {
 2485:   type NumericHeaderKey =
 2486:     | "heightPx"
 2487:     | "paddingTopPx"
 2488:     | "paddingBottomPx"
 2489:     | "paddingInlinePx"
 2490:     | "itemGapPx"
 2491:     | "logoSizePx"
 2492:     | "headerFontSizePx"
 2493:     | "titleFontSizePx"
 2494:     | "subtitleFontSizePx"
 2495:     | "lineHeight";
 2496: 
 2497:   function updateNumber(key: NumericHeaderKey, value: string) {
 2498:     onChange({
 2499:       ...settings,
 2500:       [key]: Number(value),
 2501:     });
 2502:   }
 2503: 
 2504:   const numberControls: Array<{
 2505:     key: Exclude<NumericHeaderKey, "lineHeight">;
 2506:     label: string;
 2507:     max: number;
 2508:   }> = [
 2509:     { key: "heightPx", label: "ارتفاع الترويسة", max: 420 },
 2510:     { key: "paddingTopPx", label: "مسافة علوية", max: 160 },
 2511:     { key: "paddingBottomPx", label: "مسافة سفلية", max: 160 },
 2512:     { key: "paddingInlinePx", label: "مسافة يمين/يسار", max: 180 },
 2513:     { key: "itemGapPx", label: "الفجوة بين عناصر الترويسة", max: 100 },
 2514:     { key: "logoSizePx", label: "حجم الشعار", max: 240 },
 2515:     { key: "headerFontSizePx", label: "حجم خط الترويسة", max: 48 },
 2516:     { key: "titleFontSizePx", label: "حجم خط العنوان", max: 72 },
 2517:     {
 2518:       key: "subtitleFontSizePx",
 2519:       label: "حجم خط العنوان الفرعي",
 2520:       max: 48,
 2521:     },
 2522:   ];
 2523: 
 2524:   return (
 2525:     <details
 2526:       className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm"
 2527:       dir="rtl"
 2528:     >
 2529:       <summary className="flex cursor-pointer list-none items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 px-4 py-3">
 2530:         <div>
 2531:           <h2 className="text-sm font-black text-slate-900">
 2532:             إعدادات الترويسة
 2533:           </h2>
 2534:           <p className="mt-1 text-xs font-bold leading-6 text-slate-500">
 2535:             القيمة 0 تحافظ على إعداد التصميم الأصلي لضمان توافق القوالب القديمة.
 2536:           </p>
 2537:         </div>
 2538: 
 2539:         <span className="rounded-full bg-white px-4 py-2 text-xs font-black text-emerald-700 ring-1 ring-emerald-100">
 2540:           فتح / إخفاء
 2541:         </span>
 2542:       </summary>
 2543: 
 2544:       <div className="mt-5 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
 2545:         {numberControls.map((control) => (
 2546:           <label key={control.key} className="block">
 2547:             <span className="text-xs font-black text-slate-600">
 2548:               {control.label}
 2549:             </span>
 2550:             <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-2">
 2551:               <input
 2552:                 type="number"
 2553:                 min={0}
 2554:                 max={control.max}
 2555:                 step={1}
 2556:                 value={settings[control.key]}
 2557:                 onChange={(event) =>
 2558:                   updateNumber(control.key, event.target.value)
 2559:                 }
 2560:                 className="min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm font-black text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
 2561:               />
 2562:               <span className="text-[11px] font-black text-slate-400">px</span>
 2563:             </div>
 2564:           </label>
 2565:         ))}
 2566: 
 2567:         <label className="block">
 2568:           <span className="text-xs font-black text-slate-600">سماكة الخط</span>
 2569:           <select
 2570:             value={settings.fontWeight}
 2571:             onChange={(event) =>
 2572:               onChange({
 2573:                 ...settings,
 2574:                 fontWeight: event.target
 2575:                   .value as ReportHeaderSettings["fontWeight"],
 2576:               })
 2577:             }
 2578:             className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm font-black text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
 2579:           >
 2580:             <option value="inherit">حسب التصميم</option>
 2581:             <option value="400">عادي 400</option>
 2582:             <option value="500">متوسط 500</option>
 2583:             <option value="600">شبه عريض 600</option>
 2584:             <option value="700">عريض 700</option>
 2585:             <option value="800">عريض جدًا 800</option>
 2586:             <option value="900">أسود 900</option>
 2587:           </select>
 2588:         </label>
 2589: 
 2590:         <label className="block">
 2591:           <span className="text-xs font-black text-slate-600">
 2592:             تباعد الأسطر
 2593:           </span>
 2594:           <input
 2595:             type="number"
 2596:             min={0}
 2597:             max={3}
 2598:             step={0.05}
 2599:             value={settings.lineHeight}
 2600:             onChange={(event) =>
 2601:               updateNumber("lineHeight", event.target.value)
 2602:             }
 2603:             className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm font-black text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
 2604:           />

 2606:

 2608:           <span className="text-xs font-black text-slate-600">نوع الخط</span>
 2609:           <select
 2610:             value={settings.fontFamily}
 2611:             onChange={(event) =>
 2612:               onChange({
 2613:                 ...settings,
 2614:                 fontFamily: event.target
 2615:                   .value as ReportHeaderSettings["fontFamily"],
 2616:               })
 2617:             }
 2618:             className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm font-black text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
 2619:           >
 2620:             <option value="inherit">حسب التصميم</option>
 2621:             <option value="Cairo">Cairo</option>
 2622:             <option value="Arial">Arial</option>
 2623:           </select>
 2624:         </label>
 2625:       </div>
 2626: 
 2627:       <button
 2628:         type="button"
 2629:         onClick={() => onChange({ ...DEFAULT_REPORT_HEADER_SETTINGS })}
 2630:         className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-black text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-100"
 2631:       >

 2633:       </button>
 2634:     </details>
 2635:   );

 2669: function PreviewBlock({
 2670:   block,
 2671:   context,
 2672:   previewCase,
 2673: }: {
 2674:   block: StudioBlock;
 2675:   context: Record<string, string>;
 2676:   previewCase: PreviewCaseData | null;
 2677: }) {
 2678:   const rendered = renderText(block.content, context);
 2679:   const textAlign = block.align === "center" ? "text-center" : "text-right";
 2680: 
 2681:   if (block.hideWhenMissing && block.boundFieldKey) {
 2682:     const fieldValue = String(context[`field.${block.boundFieldKey}`] || "").trim();
 2683: 
 2684:     if (!fieldValue) {
 2685:       return null;
 2686:     }
 2687:   }
 2688: 
 2689:   if (block.kind === "hero-title") {
 2690:     return (
 2691:       <section className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-5 text-center">
 2692:         <p className="text-xs font-black text-emerald-700">
 2693:           {context["service.name"]}
 2694:         </p>
 2695:         <h1 className="mx-auto mt-3 max-w-[145mm] text-3xl font-black leading-[1.7] text-slate-950">
 2696:           {rendered}
 2697:         </h1>
 2698:         {block.showMeta ? (
 2699:           <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2 text-[11px] font-black text-slate-500">
 2700:             <span className="rounded-full bg-emerald-50 px-3 py-1">
 2701:               {context["case.createdAt"]}
 2702:             </span>
 2703:             <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1">
 2704:               {context["identity.counselorName"]}
 2705:             </span>
 2706:           </div>
 2707:         ) : null}
 2708:       </section>
 2709:     );
 2710:   }
 2711: 
 2712:   if (block.kind === "meta-strip") {
 2713:     return (
 2714:       <section className="rounded-3xl border border-emerald-100 bg-emerald-50 p-4">
 2715:         <div className="grid gap-2 md:grid-cols-2">
 2716:           {splitLines(rendered).map((line) => (
 2717:             <div
 2718:               key={line}
 2719:               className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-100 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-slate-700"
 2720:             >
 2721:               {line}
 2722:             </div>
 2723:           ))}
 2724:         </div>
 2725:       </section>
 2726:     );
 2727:   }
 2728: 
 2729:   if (block.kind === "bullet-list") {
 2730:     return (
 2731:       <section className={getBlockClass(block.variant, textAlign)}>
 2732:         {block.showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={block.title} /> : null}
 2733:         <ul className="space-y-2">
 2734:           {splitLines(rendered).map((line) => (
 2735:             <li key={line} className="flex gap-2 text-sm leading-7 text-slate-700">
 2736:               <span className="mt-2 h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-emerald-600" />
 2737:               <span>{line}</span>
 2738:             </li>
 2739:           ))}
 2740:         </ul>
 2741:       </section>
 2742:     );
 2743:   }
 2744: 
 2745:   if (block.kind === "multi-paragraph") {
 2746:     return (
 2747:       <section className={getBlockClass(block.variant, textAlign)}>
 2748:         {block.showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={block.title} /> : null}
 2749:         <div className="space-y-3">
 2750:           {splitParagraphs(rendered).map((paragraph) => (
 2751:             <p key={paragraph} className="text-sm leading-8 text-slate-700">
 2752:               {paragraph}
 2753:             </p>
 2754:           ))}
 2755:         </div>
 2756:       </section>
 2757:     );
 2758:   }
 2759: 
 2760:   if (block.kind === "dynamic-fields") {
 2761:     return (
 2762:       <section className={getBlockClass(block.variant, textAlign)}>
 2763:         {block.showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={block.title} /> : null}
 2764:         <div className="grid gap-2 md:grid-cols-2">
 2765:           {getWorkflowDynamicFieldCards(previewCase).map(({ label, value }) => (
 2766:             <div
 2767:               key={label}
 2768:               className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white px-4 py-3"
 2769:             >
 2770:               <p className="text-[11px] font-black text-slate-400">{label}</p>
 2771:               <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-black text-slate-800">
 2772:                 {value || "غير متوفر"}
 2773:               </p>
 2774:             </div>
 2775:           ))}
 2776:         </div>
 2777:       </section>
 2778:     );
 2779:   }
 2780: 
 2781:   if (block.kind === "evidence-gallery") {
 2782:     const hasSelectedCase = Boolean(previewCase?.caseId);
 2783:     const realEvidences = filterValidReportEvidenceItems(
 2784:       previewCase?.evidences || [],
 2785:       { allowSampleEvidence: !hasSelectedCase },
 2786:     );
 2787:     const perPage = getEvidencePerPage(block);
 2788:     const startIndex = block.evidenceStartIndex || 0;
 2789: 
 2790:     if (!realEvidences.length && hasSelectedCase) {
 2791:       return null;
 2792:     }
 2793: 
 2794:     if (!realEvidences.length && block.evidenceEmptyBehavior === "hide") {
 2795:       return null;
 2796:     }
 2797: 
 2798:     const placeholderEvidences = createEvidencePlaceholders(perPage, startIndex);
 2799:     const sourceEvidences = realEvidences.length ? realEvidences : placeholderEvidences;
 2800:     const visibleEvidences = sourceEvidences.slice(startIndex, startIndex + perPage);
 2801:     const hiddenCount = Math.max(realEvidences.length - (startIndex + perPage), 0);
 2802:     const isPlaceholderMode = !realEvidences.length;
 2803: 
 2804:     if (block.evidenceLayout === "ATTACHMENT_LIST") {
 2805:       return (
 2806:         <section className={getBlockClass(block.variant, textAlign)}>
 2807:           {block.showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={block.title} /> : null}
 2808: 
 2809:           <div className="space-y-2">
 2810:             {visibleEvidences.map((evidence, index) => (
 2811:               <div
 2812:                 key={evidence.id || evidence.fileUrl || String(index)}
 2813:                 className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-slate-700"
 2814:               >
 2815:                 <span>
 2816:                   {evidence.caption || evidence.title || "مرفق " + (startIndex + index + 1)}
 2817:                 </span>
 2818: 
 2819:                 <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-black text-slate-500">
 2820:                   {isPlaceholderMode ? "معاينة" : "شاهد"}
 2821:                 </span>
 2822:               </div>
 2823:             ))}
 2824:           </div>
 2825:         </section>
 2826:       );
 2827:     }
 2828: 
 2829:     return (
 2830:       <section className={getBlockClass(block.variant, textAlign)}>
 2831:         {block.showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={block.title} /> : null}
 2832: 
 2833:         <div className={getEvidenceGridClass(block)}>
 2834:           {visibleEvidences.map((evidence, index) => {
 2835:             const imageUrl = evidence.imageUrl || evidence.fileUrl || "";
 2836: 
 2837:             return (
 2838:               <figure
 2839:                 key={evidence.id || imageUrl || String(index)}
 2840:                 className="break-inside-avoid overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white"
 2841:               >
 2842:                 {imageUrl && !isPlaceholderMode ? (
 2843:                   <img
 2844:                     src={imageUrl}
 2845:                     alt={evidence.title || "شاهد " + (startIndex + index + 1)}
 2846:                     className={getEvidenceImageClass(block) + " bg-slate-50"}
 2847:                   />
 2848:                 ) : (
 2849:                   <div className={getEvidenceImageHeightClass(block) + " flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-center bg-slate-50 text-center"}>
 2850:                     <div className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 text-2xl">
 2851:                       📎
 2852:                     </div>
 2853:                     <p className="mt-3 text-xs font-black text-slate-500">
 2854:                       {isPlaceholderMode ? "مساحة شاهد للمعاينة" : "شاهد بدون صورة"}
 2855:                     </p>
 2856:                   </div>
 2857:                 )}
 2858: 
 2859:                 {block.evidenceShowCaptions !== false ? (
 2860:                   <figcaption className="max-h-12 overflow-hidden border-t border-slate-100 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold leading-6 text-slate-600">
 2861:                     {evidence.caption || evidence.title || "شاهد " + (startIndex + index + 1)}
 2862:                   </figcaption>
 2863:                 ) : null}
 2864:               </figure>
 2865:             );
 2866:           })}
 2867:         </div>
 2868: 
 2869:         {hiddenCount > 0 && block.evidenceAutoCreatePages === false ? (
 2870:           <p className="mt-3 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-amber-700">
 2871:             يوجد {hiddenCount} شاهد إضافي. سيتم نقله إلى صفحة شواهد إضافية حتى لا يتمدد إطار A4.
 2872:           </p>
 2873:         ) : null}
 2874: 
 2875:         {isPlaceholderMode ? (
 2876:           <p className="mt-3 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-emerald-700">
 2877:             هذه مربعات معاينة فقط. عند اختبار Case ID يحتوي شواهد، سيتم عرض الشواهد الفعلية هنا.
 2878:           </p>
 2879:         ) : null}
 2880:       </section>
 2881:     );
 2882:   }
 2883: 
 2884:   if (block.kind === "closing-note") {
 2885:     return (
 2886:       <section className={getBlockClass(block.variant, textAlign)}>
 2887:         {block.showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={block.title} /> : null}
 2888:         <p className="text-sm leading-8 text-slate-700">{rendered}</p>
 2889: 
 2890:         <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
 2891:           <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-4">
 2892:             <p className="text-xs font-black text-slate-400">
 2893:               الموجه/الموجهة الطلابية
 2894:             </p>
 2895:             <p className="mt-4 text-sm font-black text-slate-800">
 2896:               {context["identity.counselorName"]}
 2897:             </p>
 2898:           </div>
 2899: 
 2900:           <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-4">
 2901:             <p className="text-xs font-black text-slate-400">
 2902:               مدير/مديرة المدرسة
 2903:             </p>
 2904:             <p className="mt-4 text-sm font-black text-slate-800">
 2905:               {context["identity.principalName"]}
 2906:             </p>
 2907:           </div>
 2908:         </div>
 2909:       </section>
 2910:     );
 2911:   }
 2912: 
 2913:   return (
 2914:     <section className={getBlockClass(block.variant, textAlign)}>
 2915:       {block.showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={block.title} /> : null}
 2916:       <p className="whitespace-pre-line text-sm leading-8 text-slate-700">
 2917:         {rendered}
 2918:       </p>
 2919:     </section>
 2920:   );
 2921: }

 2923: 
 2924: function createEvidencePlaceholders(
 2925:   count: number,
 2926:   startIndex: number,
 2927: ): NonNullable<PreviewCaseData["evidences"]> {
 2928:   return Array.from({ length: count }).map((_, index) => {
 2929:     const evidenceNumber = startIndex + index + 1;
 2930: 
 2931:     return {
 2932:       id: "placeholder-evidence-" + evidenceNumber,
 2933:       title: "شاهد تجريبي " + evidenceNumber,
 2934:       caption: "مكان الشاهد داخل التقارير",
 2935:       fileUrl: "",
 2936:       imageUrl: "",
 2937:     };
 2938:   });
 2939: }
 2940: 
 2941: function getEvidencePerPage(block: any) {
 2942:   return getSmartEvidencePerPage(block);
 2943: }
 2944: 
 2945: function getSmartEvidencePerPage(block: any) {
 2946:   const layout = String(block?.evidenceLayout || "TWO_PER_PAGE");
 2947:   const ratio = String(block?.evidenceAspectRatio || "LANDSCAPE_4_3");
 2948:   const fit = String(block?.evidenceFit || "contain");
 2949: 
 2950:   if (layout === "ATTACHMENT_LIST") return 10;
 2951:   if (layout === "ONE_PER_PAGE") return 1;
 2952:   if (layout === "TWO_PER_PAGE") return 2;
 2953: 
 2954:   /*
 2955:     GRID_2X2 is a maximum capacity, not a forced capacity.
 2956:     The system must keep every evidence card inside the A4 frame.
 2957:     Portrait images are tall, so 4 cards can overflow the page.
 2958:   */
 2959:   if (layout === "GRID_2X2") {
 2960:     if (ratio === "PORTRAIT_3_4") return 2;
 2961:     if (ratio === "SQUARE_1_1" && fit === "cover") return 4;
 2962:     if (ratio === "SQUARE_1_1") return 4;
 2963:     if (ratio === "LANDSCAPE_16_9") return 4;
 2964:     return 4;
 2965:   }
 2966: 
 2967:   return 2;
 2968: }
 2969: 
 2970: function getEvidenceGridClass(block: any) {
 2971:   const perPage = getEvidencePerPage(block);
 2972: 
 2973:   if (block.evidenceLayout === "ATTACHMENT_LIST") {
 2974:     return "grid gap-2";
 2975:   }
 2976: 
 2977:   if (perPage <= 1) {
 2978:     return "grid gap-3";
 2979:   }
 2980: 
 2981:   return "grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2";
 2982: }
 2983: 
 2984: 
 2985: function getEvidenceAspectRatioClass(block: StudioBlock) {
 2986:   switch (block.evidenceAspectRatio || "LANDSCAPE_4_3") {
 2987:     case "LANDSCAPE_16_9":
 2988:       return "aspect-video";
 2989:     case "PORTRAIT_3_4":
 2990:       return "aspect-[3/4]";
 2991:     case "SQUARE_1_1":
 2992:       return "aspect-square";
 2993:     case "LANDSCAPE_4_3":
 2994:     default:
 2995:       return "aspect-[4/3]";
 2996:   }
 2997: }
 2998: 
 2999: function getEvidenceImageHeightClass(block: any) {
 3000:   const perPage = getEvidencePerPage(block);
 3001:   const ratio = block.evidenceAspectRatio || "LANDSCAPE_4_3";
 3002: 
 3003:   /*
 3004:     Fixed heights are intentional:
 3005:     they keep evidence inside the printable A4 area.
 3006:     Extra evidence must go to a new Evidence Page instead of stretching A4.
 3007:   */
 3008:   if (perPage <= 1) {
 3009:     switch (ratio) {
 3010:       case "PORTRAIT_3_4":
 3011:         return "h-[185mm]";
 3012:       case "SQUARE_1_1":
 3013:         return "h-[160mm]";
 3014:       case "LANDSCAPE_16_9":
 3015:         return "h-[122mm]";
 3016:       case "LANDSCAPE_4_3":
 3017:       default:
 3018:         return "h-[138mm]";
 3019:     }
 3020:   }
 3021: 
 3022:   if (perPage === 2) {
 3023:     switch (ratio) {
 3024:       case "PORTRAIT_3_4":
 3025:         return "h-[92mm]";
 3026:       case "SQUARE_1_1":
 3027:         return "h-[82mm]";
 3028:       case "LANDSCAPE_16_9":
 3029:         return "h-[58mm]";
 3030:       case "LANDSCAPE_4_3":
 3031:       default:
 3032:         return "h-[66mm]";
 3033:     }
 3034:   }
 3035: 
 3036:   switch (ratio) {
 3037:     case "SQUARE_1_1":
 3038:       return "h-[56mm]";
 3039:     case "LANDSCAPE_16_9":
 3040:       return "h-[42mm]";
 3041:     case "PORTRAIT_3_4":
 3042:       return "h-[82mm]";
 3043:     case "LANDSCAPE_4_3":
 3044:     default:
 3045:       return "h-[48mm]";
 3046:   }
 3047: }
 3048: 
 3049: function getEvidenceImageClass(block: any) {
 3050:   const fit = block.evidenceFit === "cover" ? "object-cover" : "object-contain";
 3051:   return `${getEvidenceImageHeightClass(block)} w-full ${fit}`;
 3052: }
 3053: 
 3054: function EmptyEvidenceMessage() {
 3055:   return (
 3056:     <div className="rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 p-6 text-center">
 3057:       <p className="text-sm font-black text-emerald-800">
 3058:         لا توجد شواهد مرتبطة بالـ Case ID الحالي
 3059:       </p>
 3060: 
 3061:       <p className="mt-2 text-xs font-bold leading-6 text-emerald-700">
 3062:         هذا البلوك لن يعرض أي مرفقات في التقارير النهائي إلا إذا كانت الحالة تحتوي على شواهد.
 3063:       </p>
 3064:     </div>
 3065:   );
 3066: }
 3067: 
 3068: function BlockTitle({ title }: { title: string }) {
 3069:   return (
 3070:     <h3 className="mb-3 text-base font-black text-slate-950">
 3071:       {title}
 3072:     </h3>
 3073:   );
 3074: }

 3076: function getPlacementClass(placement: BlockPlacement) {
 3077:   const fullCenteredBase = "absolute left-0 right-0 w-full";
 3078:   const centeredBase = "absolute left-1/2 w-full max-w-[78%] -translate-x-1/2";
 3079:   const sideBase = "absolute w-full max-w-[58%]";
 3080: 
 3081:   const classes: Record<BlockPlacement, string> = {
 3082:     flow: "",
 3083: 
 3084:     // الخيارات العامة: تبقى بعرضها الطبيعي الكامل مثل وضع الترتيب الطبيعي
 3085:     top: `${fullCenteredBase} top-0`,
 3086:     middle: `${fullCenteredBase} top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2`,
 3087:     bottom: `${fullCenteredBase} bottom-0`,
 3088: 
 3089:     // الخيارات الدقيقة: يمين/وسط/يسار بعرض مخصص حتى لا تملأ الصفحة كاملة
 3090:     "top-right": `${sideBase} right-0 top-0`,
 3091:     "top-center": `${centeredBase} top-0`,
 3092:     "top-left": `${sideBase} left-0 top-0`,
 3093: 
 3094:     "middle-right": `${sideBase} right-0 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2`,
 3095:     "middle-center": `${centeredBase} top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2`,
 3096:     "middle-left": `${sideBase} left-0 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2`,
 3097: 
 3098:     "bottom-right": `${sideBase} bottom-0 right-0`,
 3099:     "bottom-center": `${centeredBase} bottom-0`,
 3100:     "bottom-left": `${sideBase} bottom-0 left-0`,
 3101:   };
 3102: 
 3103:   return classes[placement];
 3104: }
 3105: 
 3106: function getBlockClass(variant: BlockVariant, textAlign: string) {
 3107:   const base = `break-inside-avoid ${textAlign}`;
 3108: 
 3109:   const classes: Record<BlockVariant, string> = {
 3110:     hero: `${base} py-5`,
 3111:     plain: `${base} px-1 py-2`,
 3112:     card: `${base} rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm`,
 3113:     soft: `${base} rounded-3xl border border-emerald-100 bg-emerald-50 p-5`,
 3114:     highlight: `${base} rounded-3xl border border-emerald-200 bg-gradient-to-l from-emerald-50 to-white p-5`,
 3115:     outline: `${base} rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white p-5`,
 3116:     quote: `${base} rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-5`,
 3117:   };
 3118: 
 3119:   return classes[variant];
 3120: }

 3192: 
 3193: function cleanWorkflowDynamicText(value: unknown) {
 3194:   return String(value ?? "").trim();
 3195: }
 3196: 
 3197: function isTechnicalWorkflowText(value: string) {
 3198:   return /^[a-z0-9_/-]+$/i.test(value) && /[a-z_]/i.test(value);
 3199: }
 3200: 
 3201: function translateWorkflowDynamicLabel(key: unknown, label: unknown) {
 3202:   const cleanKey = cleanWorkflowDynamicText(key);
 3203:   const cleanLabel = cleanWorkflowDynamicText(label);
 3204: 
 3205:   if (cleanLabel && cleanLabel !== cleanKey && !isTechnicalWorkflowText(cleanLabel)) {
 3206:     return cleanLabel;
 3207:   }
 3208: 
 3209:   return WORKFLOW_DYNAMIC_FIELD_LABELS[cleanKey] || cleanLabel || cleanKey;
 3210: }
 3211: 
 3212: function translateWorkflowDynamicValue(value: unknown): string {
 3213:   if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") return "";
 3214: 
 3215:   if (Array.isArray(value)) {
 3216:     return value
 3217:       .map((item) => translateWorkflowDynamicValue(item))
 3218:       .filter(Boolean)
 3219:       .join("، ");
 3220:   }
 3221: 
 3222:   if (typeof value === "boolean") {
 3223:     return value ? "نعم" : "لا";
 3224:   }
 3225: 
 3226:   if (typeof value === "object") {
 3227:     const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
 3228:     return (
 3229:       translateWorkflowDynamicValue(record.label) ||
 3230:       translateWorkflowDynamicValue(record.name) ||
 3231:       translateWorkflowDynamicValue(record.value) ||
 3232:       translateWorkflowDynamicValue(record.key) ||
 3233:       ""
 3234:     );
 3235:   }
 3236: 
 3237:   const text = cleanWorkflowDynamicText(value);
 3238:   const normalized = text.toLowerCase();
 3239: 
 3240:   if (WORKFLOW_DYNAMIC_VALUE_LABELS[normalized]) {
 3241:     return WORKFLOW_DYNAMIC_VALUE_LABELS[normalized];
 3242:   }
 3243: 
 3244:   const programMatch = /^program[_-](\d+)$/i.exec(text);
 3245:   if (programMatch) {
 3246:     return `برنامج النشاط رقم ${Number(programMatch[1])}`;
 3247:   }
 3248: 
 3249:   return text;
 3250: }
 3251: 
 3252: function getWorkflowDynamicFieldCards(previewCase: any): WorkflowDynamicFieldCard[] {
 3253:   return (previewCase?.values || [])
 3254:     .map((item: any, index: number) => {
 3255:       const key = cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.fieldKey);
 3256:       const label = translateWorkflowDynamicLabel(
 3257:         item.fieldKey,
 3258:         item.fieldLabel || `حقل ${index + 1}`,
 3259:       );
 3260:       const value = translateWorkflowDynamicValue(item.value);
 3261: 
 3262:       return {
 3263:         key: key || label || `workflow-field-${index + 1}`,
 3264:         label,
 3265:         value,
 3266:       };
 3267:     })
 3268:     .filter((item: any) => item.label && item.value);
 3269: }

  315: function getReportTwoSavedTemplatesStorageKey(serviceSlug: string) {
  316:   return `report-2:saved-runtime-templates:${serviceSlug || "general"}`;

  318: 
  319: function readReportTwoSavedTemplates(
  320:   serviceSlug: string,
  321: ): ReportTwoSavedRuntimeTemplate[] {
  322:   if (typeof window === "undefined") return [];
  323: 
  324:   try {
  325:     const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(
  326:       getReportTwoSavedTemplatesStorageKey(serviceSlug),
  327:     );
  328: 
  329:     if (!raw) return [];
  330: 
  331:     const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
  332: 
  333:     return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [];
  334:   } catch {
  335:     return [];
  336:   }
  337: }
  338: 
  339: function getReportTwoSavedTemplatesStoragePrefix() {
  340:   return "report-2:saved-runtime-templates:";
  341: }
  342: 
  343: function readAllReportTwoSavedTemplates() {
  344:   if (typeof window === "undefined") return [];
  345: 
  346:   const prefix = getReportTwoSavedTemplatesStoragePrefix();
  347:   const items: ReportTwoSavedRuntimeTemplate[] = [];
  348: 
  349:   for (let index = 0; index < window.localStorage.length; index += 1) {
  350:     const key = window.localStorage.key(index);
  351: 
  352:     if (!key || !key.startsWith(prefix)) continue;
  353: 
  354:     const serviceSlug = key.slice(prefix.length);
  355:     const templates = readReportTwoSavedTemplates(serviceSlug);
  356: 
  357:     templates.forEach((template) => {
  358:       items.push({
  359:         ...template,
  360:         serviceSlug: (template as any).serviceSlug || serviceSlug,
  361:       } as ReportTwoSavedRuntimeTemplate);
  362:     });
  363:   }
  364: 
  365:   return items.sort((a, b) => {
  366:     return String((b as any).updatedAt || (b as any).createdAt || "").localeCompare(
  367:       String((a as any).updatedAt || (a as any).createdAt || ""),
  368:     );
  369:   });
  370: }
  371: 
  372: function deleteReportTwoSavedTemplateFromAllServices(templateId: string) {
  373:   if (typeof window === "undefined") return [];
  374: 
  375:   const prefix = getReportTwoSavedTemplatesStoragePrefix();
  376: 
  377:   for (let index = window.localStorage.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
  378:     const key = window.localStorage.key(index);
  379: 
  380:     if (!key || !key.startsWith(prefix)) continue;
  381: 
  382:     const serviceSlug = key.slice(prefix.length);
  383:     const templates = readReportTwoSavedTemplates(serviceSlug);
  384:     const nextTemplates = templates.filter((template) => template.id !== templateId);
  385: 
  386:     if (nextTemplates.length !== templates.length) {
  387:       writeReportTwoSavedTemplates(serviceSlug, nextTemplates);
  388:     }
  389:   }
  390: 
  391:   return readAllReportTwoSavedTemplates();
  392: }
  393: function writeReportTwoSavedTemplates(

  605: function normalizeBlock(value: any, index: number): StudioBlock {
  606:   const kind = cleanText(value?.kind || value?.settings?.smartBlockKind || "section-text") as StudioBlockKind;
  607: 
  608:   return {
  609:     ...createBlock(kind),
  610:     ...value,
  611:     id: cleanText(value?.id) || makeId(`block-${index + 1}`),
  612:     kind,
  613:     title: cleanText(value?.title) || `بلوك ${index + 1}`,
  614:     content: cleanText(value?.content ?? value?.defaultContent ?? value?.customContent ?? ""),
  615:     showTitle: value?.showTitle !== false,
  616:     showMeta: Boolean(value?.showMeta),
  617:     align: value?.align === "center" ? "center" : "right",
  618:     placement: cleanText(value?.placement) || "flow",
  619:   };
  620: }
  621: 
  622: function normalizePage(value: any, index: number): StudioPage {
  623:   const rawBlocks = asArray(value?.blocks);
  624: 
  625:   return {
  626:     id: cleanText(value?.id) || makeId(`page-${index + 1}`),
  627:     kind: cleanText(value?.kind || "content") as StudioPageKind,
  628:     title: cleanText(value?.title) || `صفحة ${index + 1}`,
  629:     description: cleanText(value?.description),
  630:     blocks: rawBlocks.length
  631:       ? rawBlocks.map(normalizeBlock)
  632:       : [createBlock("section-text")],
  633:   };
  634: }

  636: function hydrateTemplate(template: TemplateOption | null): StudioTemplate {
  637:   const raw = asRecord(template?.templateJson);
  638:   const smartStudio = asRecord(raw.smartStudio);
  639:   const source = asArray(smartStudio.pages).length ? smartStudio : raw;
  640:   const pages = asArray(source.pages);
  641: 
  642:   return {
  643:     id: template?.id || "report-2-empty-template",
  644:     name: template?.name || "قالب report-2",
  645:     description: template?.description || "",
  646:     designTemplateId: normalizeDesignId(source.designTemplateId || raw.designTemplateId),
  647:     designConfig: asRecord(source.designConfig).header
  648:       ? {
  649:           header: asRecord(source.designConfig)
  650:             .header as unknown as ReportHeaderSettings,
  651:         }
  652:       : asRecord(raw.designConfig).header
  653:         ? {
  654:             header: asRecord(raw.designConfig)
  655:               .header as unknown as ReportHeaderSettings,
  656:           }
  657:         : undefined,
  658:     pages: pages.length
  659:       ? pages.map(normalizePage)
  660:       : [
  661:           {
  662:             id: "report-2-fallback-page",
  663:             kind: "content",
  664:             title: "صفحة التقرير",
  665:             description: "",
  666:             blocks: [
  667:               createBlock("hero-title"),
  668:               createBlock("dynamic-fields"),
  669:               createBlock("section-text"),
  670:             ],
  671:           },
  672:         ],
  673:   };

  693: function normalizeReportTwoLogoNumber(
  694:   value: number,
  695:   fallback: number,
  696:   min: number,
  697:   max: number,
  698: ) {
  699:   if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
  700:     return fallback;
  701:   }
  702: 
  703:   return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, Math.floor(value)));

  876: function getReportTwoFieldLabel(key: string, label: string): string {
  877:   if (!key && !label) return "";
  878:   const trimmed = label?.trim();
  879:   if (trimmed && trimmed !== key && !/^[a-z0-9_./-]+$/i.test(trimmed)) {
  880:     return trimmed;
  881:   }
  882:   return REPORT_TWO_FIELD_LABELS[key] || REPORT_TWO_FIELD_LABELS[label] || trimmed || key || "";
  883: }
  884: 
  885: function getReportTwoFieldValue(value: unknown): string {
  886:   if (value === null || value === undefined) return "";
  887:   const raw = String(value).trim();
  888:   if (!raw) return "";
  889:   const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
  890:   const direct = REPORT_TWO_VALUE_LABELS[lower] || REPORT_TWO_VALUE_LABELS[raw];
  891:   if (direct) return direct;
  892:   const programMatch = lower.match(/^program_(\d+)$/);
  893:   if (programMatch) return `برنامج النشاط رقم ${programMatch[1]}`;
  894:   if (/^[a-z0-9_./-]+$/i.test(raw) && /[a-z_]/i.test(raw)) return raw;
  895:   return raw;
  896: }

  898: function getPayloadAny(payload: SmartReportPayload) {
  899:   return payload as any;
  900: }

  902: function getRuntimeContext(payload: SmartReportPayload) {
  903:   const data = getPayloadAny(payload);
  904:   const student = data.student || data.caseInfo?.student || {};
  905:   const fields = [...(payload.primaryFields || []), ...(payload.detailFields || [])];
  906: 
  907:   const context: Record<string, string> = {
  908:     "case.id": cleanText(data.caseInfo?.id),
  909:     "case.title": cleanText(data.caseInfo?.title || data.title),
  910:     "case.status": cleanText(data.caseInfo?.status),
  911:     "case.createdAt": cleanText(data.caseInfo?.createdAt),
  912:     "case.updatedAt": cleanText(data.caseInfo?.updatedAt),
  913: 
  914:     "service.name": cleanText(data.service?.name),
  915:     "service.slug": cleanText(data.service?.slug),
  916: 
  917:     "student.name": cleanText(student.name || student.fullName),
  918:     "student.grade": cleanText(student.grade),
  919:     "student.classroom": cleanText(student.classroom),
  920:     "student.stage": cleanText(student.stage),
  921:     "student.guardianName": cleanText(student.guardianName),
  922:     "student.guardianPhone": cleanText(student.guardianPhone),
  923: 
  924:     "identity.ministryName": cleanText(data.identity?.ministryName || "وزارة التعليم"),
  925:     "identity.educationDepartment": cleanText(data.identity?.educationDepartment || "الإدارة العامة للتعليم"),
  926:     "identity.schoolName": cleanText(data.identity?.schoolName || ""),
  927:   };
  928: 
  929:   fields.forEach((field: any, index: number) => {
  930:     const key = cleanText(field.key) || `field-${index + 1}`;
  931:     const label = cleanText(field.label);
  932:     const value = cleanText(field.value);
  933: 
  934:     context[key] = value;
  935:     context[`field.${key}`] = value;
  936: 
  937:     if (label) {
  938:       context[label] = value;
  939:       context[`field.${label}`] = value;
  940:     }
  941:   });
  942: 
  943:   return context;
  944: }
  945: 
  946: function normalizeEvidenceUrl(value: unknown) {
  947:   const url = cleanText(value).replaceAll("\\", "/");
  948: 
  949:   if (!url) return "";
  950:   if (url.startsWith("http://")) return url;
  951:   if (url.startsWith("https://")) return url;
  952:   if (url.startsWith("data:")) return url;
  953:   if (url.startsWith("/")) return url;
  954: 
  955:   return `/${url.replace(/^public\//, "")}`;
  956: }
  957: 
  958: const REPORT_TWO_IMAGE_EVIDENCE_EXTENSION_PATTERN =
  959:   /\.(png|jpe?g|webp|gif|avif)(?:[?#].*)?$/i;
  960: 
  961: function hasReportTwoImageEvidenceExtension(value: unknown) {
  962:   const text = cleanText(value).replaceAll("\\", "/");
  963: 
  964:   if (!text) return false;
  965: 
  966:   return REPORT_TWO_IMAGE_EVIDENCE_EXTENSION_PATTERN.test(text);
  967: }
  968: 
  969: function isReportTwoImageEvidence(item: any) {
  970:   const evidenceType = cleanText(item?.type).toUpperCase();
  971:   const mimeType = cleanText(item?.mimeType).toLowerCase();
  972: 
  973:   if (evidenceType === "IMAGE") return true;
  974:   if (mimeType.startsWith("image/")) return true;
  975: 
  976:   return [
  977:     item?.imageUrl,
  978:     item?.fileUrl,
  979:     item?.url,
  980:     item?.publicUrl,
  981:     item?.thumbnailUrl,
  982:     item?.previewUrl,
  983:     item?.fileName,
  984:     item?.originalName,
  985:     item?.name,
  986:   ].some((value) => hasReportTwoImageEvidenceExtension(value));
  987: }
  988: 
  989: function collectEvidences(payload: SmartReportPayload) {
  990:   const data = getPayloadAny(payload);
  991: 
  992:   const candidates = [
  993:     data.evidence,
  994:     data.evidence?.items,
  995:     data.evidence?.evidences,
  996:     data.evidence?.files,
  997:     data.evidence?.attachments,
  998:     data.evidenceItems,
  999:     data.evidences,
 1000:     data.attachments,
 1001:     data.files,
 1002:     data.caseInfo?.evidence,
 1003:     data.caseInfo?.evidence?.items,
 1004:     data.caseInfo?.evidence?.evidences,
 1005:     data.caseInfo?.evidence?.files,
 1006:     data.caseInfo?.evidence?.attachments,
 1007:     data.caseInfo?.evidenceItems,
 1008:     data.caseInfo?.evidences,
 1009:     data.caseInfo?.attachments,
 1010:     data.caseInfo?.files,
 1011:   ];
 1012: 
 1013:   const collected: any[] = [];
 1014: 
 1015:   candidates.forEach((candidate) => {
 1016:     if (Array.isArray(candidate)) {
 1017:       collected.push(...candidate);
 1018:       return;
 1019:     }
 1020: 
 1021:     if (candidate && typeof candidate === "object") {
 1022:       const record = candidate as Record<string, any>;
 1023: 
 1024:       [
 1025:         record.items,
 1026:         record.evidences,
 1027:         record.files,
 1028:         record.attachments,
 1029:         record.data,
 1030:       ].forEach((value) => {
 1031:         if (Array.isArray(value)) {
 1032:           collected.push(...value);
 1033:         }
 1034:       });
 1035:     }
 1036:   });
 1037: 
 1038:   const seen = new Set<string>();
 1039: 
 1040:   return filterValidReportEvidenceItems(collected)
 1041:     .map((item, index) => {
 1042:       const rawUrl =
 1043:         item.fileUrl ||
 1044:         item.url ||
 1045:         item.imageUrl ||
 1046:         item.publicUrl ||
 1047:         item.path ||
 1048:         item.filePath ||
 1049:         item.storagePath ||
 1050:         item.thumbnailUrl ||
 1051:         item.previewUrl ||
 1052:         item.downloadUrl ||
 1053:         item.secureUrl ||
 1054:         item.src ||
 1055:         "";
 1056: 
 1057:       const fileUrl = normalizeEvidenceUrl(rawUrl);
 1058:       const normalizedUrl = normalizeEvidenceUrl(item.url || "");
 1059:       const imageUrlCandidate = normalizeEvidenceUrl(
 1060:         item.imageUrl ||
 1061:           item.thumbnailUrl ||
 1062:           item.previewUrl ||
 1063:           item.publicUrl ||
 1064:           item.url ||
 1065:           item.fileUrl ||
 1066:           rawUrl,
 1067:       );
 1068:       const imageUrl = isReportTwoImageEvidence(item)
 1069:         ? imageUrlCandidate || fileUrl || normalizedUrl
 1070:         : "";
 1071:       const evidenceType: "IMAGE" | "FILE" = isReportTwoImageEvidence(item)
 1072:         ? "IMAGE"
 1073:         : "FILE";
 1074: 
 1075:       const id =
 1076:         cleanText(item.id) ||
 1077:         cleanText(item.evidenceId) ||
 1078:         cleanText(item.fileId) ||
 1079:         fileUrl ||
 1080:         imageUrl ||
 1081:         `evidence-${index + 1}`;
 1082: 
 1083:       return {
 1084:         id,
 1085:         title:
 1086:           cleanText(item.title || item.fileName || item.originalName || item.name) ||
 1087:           `شاهد ${index + 1}`,
 1088:         url: normalizedUrl || undefined,
 1089:         fileUrl,
 1090:         imageUrl,
 1091:         attachmentId: cleanText(item.attachmentId || item.fileId || item.evidenceId) || undefined,
 1092:         storagePath: cleanText(item.storagePath) || undefined,
 1093:         type: evidenceType,
 1094:         mimeType: cleanText(item.mimeType) || undefined,
 1095:         caption: cleanText(
 1096:           item.caption ||
 1097:             item.note ||
 1098:             item.description ||
 1099:             item.title ||
 1100:             item.fileName ||
 1101:             item.originalName,
 1102:         ),
 1103:       };
 1104:     })
 1105:     .filter((item) => filterValidReportEvidenceItems([item]).length > 0)
 1106:     .filter((item) => {
 1107:       const key = `${item.id}|${item.fileUrl}|${item.imageUrl}|${item.attachmentId || ""}`;
 1108: 
 1109:       if (seen.has(key)) {
 1110:         return false;
 1111:       }
 1112: 
 1113:       seen.add(key);
 1114:       return true;
 1115:     });
 1116: }
 1117: 
 1118: function getPreviewCase(payload: SmartReportPayload, template?: StudioTemplate) {
 1119:   const data = getPayloadAny(payload);
 1120:   const student = data.student || data.caseInfo?.student || {};
 1121:   const hasStudentDataTable =
 1122:     (payload.tables || []).some(isStudentDataTable) ||
 1123:     Boolean(
 1124:       template?.pages.some((page) =>
 1125:         page.blocks.some(
 1126:           (block) => block.kind === "structured-table" && isStudentDataTable(block),
 1127:         ),
 1128:       ),
 1129:     );
 1130:   const fields = [...(payload.primaryFields || []), ...(payload.detailFields || [])].filter(
 1131:     (field) => !hasStudentDataTable || !isStudentIdentityField(field),
 1132:   );
 1133: 
 1134:   return {
 1135:     found: true,
 1136:     caseId: cleanText(data.caseInfo?.id),
 1137:     serviceSlug: cleanText(data.service?.slug),
 1138:     serviceName: cleanText(data.service?.name),
 1139:     title: cleanText(data.caseInfo?.title || data.title),
 1140:     status: cleanText(data.caseInfo?.status),
 1141:     createdAt: cleanText(data.caseInfo?.createdAt),
 1142:     updatedAt: cleanText(data.caseInfo?.updatedAt),
 1143:     hasStudentDataTable,
 1144:     student: {
 1145:       name: cleanText(student.name || student.fullName),
 1146:       nationalId: cleanText(student.nationalId),
 1147:       grade: cleanText(student.grade),
 1148:       classroom: cleanText(student.classroom),
 1149:       stage: cleanText(student.stage),
 1150:       guardianName: cleanText(student.guardianName),
 1151:       guardianPhone: cleanText(student.guardianPhone),
 1152:     },
 1153:     values: fields.map((field: any, index: number) => {
 1154:       const fieldKey =
 1155:         cleanText(field.key) ||
 1156:         cleanText(field.fieldKey) ||
 1157:         cleanText(field.name) ||
 1158:         cleanText(field.id) ||
 1159:         `field-${index + 1}`;
 1160:       const valueItems = getReportTwoReadableFieldValueItems(field);
 1161:       const value =
 1162:         valueItems.length > 1
 1163:           ? valueItems.join("، ")
 1164:           : getReportTwoReadableFieldValue(field);
 1165: 
 1166:       return {
 1167:         fieldKey,
 1168:         fieldLabel: getReportTwoReadableFieldLabel(field, index),
 1169:         value,
 1170:         ...(valueItems.length > 1 ? { valueItems } : {}),
 1171:         rawValue: Array.isArray(field.value)
 1172:           ? field.value.map((item: unknown) => cleanText(item)).filter(Boolean).join("، ")
 1173:           : cleanText(field.value),
 1174:       };
 1175:     }),
 1176:     evidences: collectEvidences(payload),
 1177:   };
 1178: }
 1179: 
 1180: 
 1181: function getEvidencePerPageFromBlock(block: StudioBlock) {
 1182:   const explicitLimit = Number(block.evidenceLimit || 0);
 1183: 
 1184:   if (Number.isFinite(explicitLimit) && explicitLimit > 0) {
 1185:     return Math.max(1, Math.floor(explicitLimit));
 1186:   }
 1187: 
 1188:   if (block.evidenceLayout === "ONE_PER_PAGE") return 1;
 1189:   if (block.evidenceLayout === "GRID_2X2") return 4;
 1190:   if (block.evidenceLayout === "ATTACHMENT_LIST") return 6;
 1191: 
 1192:   return 2;
 1193: }
 1194: 
 1195: function getReportTwoSignatureCardsFromPayload(payload: SmartReportPayload) {
 1196:   const priority = ["teacher", "activity_leader", "counselor", "principal"];
 1197: 
 1198:   const signatures = [...(payload.signatures || [])].sort((a, b) => {
 1199:     const aIndex = priority.indexOf(String(a.key || ""));
 1200:     const bIndex = priority.indexOf(String(b.key || ""));
 1201: 
 1202:     return (aIndex === -1 ? 99 : aIndex) - (bIndex === -1 ? 99 : bIndex);
 1203:   });
 1204: 
 1205:   return signatures
 1206:     .filter((signature) => {
 1207:       return Boolean(
 1208:         signature?.label ||
 1209:           signature?.signerName ||
 1210:           signature?.signerTitle ||
 1211:           signature?.imageUrl,
 1212:       );
 1213:     })
 1214:     .map((signature) => ({
 1215:       key: String(signature.key || ""),
 1216:       label: String(signature.label || "التوقيع"),
 1217:       signerName: String(signature.signerName || ""),
 1218:       signerTitle: String(signature.signerTitle || ""),
 1219:       imageUrl: String(signature.imageUrl || ""),
 1220:       required: Boolean(signature.required),
 1221:     }));
 1222: }
 1223: 
 1224: function isReportTwoSignatureBlock(block: StudioBlock | null | undefined) {
 1225:   if (!block) return false;
 1226: 
 1227:   const kind = cleanText(block.kind);
 1228:   const title = cleanText(block.title);
 1229: 
 1230:   return (
 1231:     kind === "signature-grid" ||
 1232:     kind === "signatures" ||
 1233:     kind === "approval-signatures" ||
 1234:     title.includes("توقيع") ||
 1235:     title.includes("اعتماد") ||
 1236:     Array.isArray((block as any).signatures)
 1237:   );
 1238: }
 1239: 
 1240: function getReportTwoSignatureHiddenKeys(block: StudioBlock | null | undefined) {
 1241:   return new Set(
 1242:     Array.isArray((block as any)?.hiddenSignatureKeys)
 1243:       ? ((block as any).hiddenSignatureKeys as string[]).map((key) => cleanText(key))
 1244:       : [],
 1245:   );
 1246: }
 1247: 
 1248: function getReportTwoOrderedSignatureCards(
 1249:   signatures: ReturnType<typeof getReportTwoSignatureCardsFromPayload>,
 1250:   block: StudioBlock | null | undefined,
 1251: ) {
 1252:   const order = Array.isArray((block as any)?.signatureOrder)
 1253:     ? ((block as any).signatureOrder as string[]).map((key) => cleanText(key)).filter(Boolean)
 1254:     : [];
 1255: 
 1256:   const byKey = new Map(signatures.map((signature) => [signature.key, signature]));
 1257:   const used = new Set<string>();
 1258:   const ordered = order
 1259:     .map((key) => byKey.get(key))
 1260:     .filter((signature): signature is NonNullable<typeof signature> => {
 1261:       if (!signature || used.has(signature.key)) return false;
 1262: 
 1263:       used.add(signature.key);
 1264:       return true;
 1265:     });
 1266: 
 1267:   return [
 1268:     ...ordered,
 1269:     ...signatures.filter((signature) => !used.has(signature.key)),
 1270:   ];
 1271: }
 1272: 
 1273: function getReportTwoConfiguredSignatureCards(
 1274:   signatures: ReturnType<typeof getReportTwoSignatureCardsFromPayload>,
 1275:   block: StudioBlock | null | undefined,
 1276: ) {
 1277:   const hiddenKeys = getReportTwoSignatureHiddenKeys(block);
 1278: 
 1279:   return getReportTwoOrderedSignatureCards(signatures, block).filter(
 1280:     (signature) => !hiddenKeys.has(signature.key),
 1281:   );
 1282: }
 1283: 
 1284: function moveReportTwoSignatureOrder(
 1285:   currentOrder: unknown,
 1286:   allKeys: string[],
 1287:   key: string,
 1288:   direction: -1 | 1,
 1289: ) {
 1290:   const normalizedKeys = allKeys.map((item) => cleanText(item)).filter(Boolean);
 1291:   const current = Array.isArray(currentOrder)
 1292:     ? currentOrder.map((item) => cleanText(item)).filter(Boolean)
 1293:     : [];
 1294: 
 1295:   const order = [
 1296:     ...current.filter((item) => normalizedKeys.includes(item)),
 1297:     ...normalizedKeys.filter((item) => !current.includes(item)),
 1298:   ];
 1299: 
 1300:   const index = order.indexOf(key);
 1301:   const targetIndex = index + direction;
 1302: 
 1303:   if (index < 0 || targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= order.length) {
 1304:     return order;
 1305:   }
 1306: 
 1307:   const next = [...order];
 1308:   const currentItem = next[index];
 1309: 
 1310:   next[index] = next[targetIndex];
 1311:   next[targetIndex] = currentItem;
 1312: 
 1313:   return next;
 1314: }
 1315: 
 1316: function toggleReportTwoSignatureHiddenKey(currentHiddenKeys: unknown, key: string) {
 1317:   const hidden = new Set(
 1318:     Array.isArray(currentHiddenKeys)
 1319:       ? currentHiddenKeys.map((item) => cleanText(item)).filter(Boolean)
 1320:       : [],
 1321:   );
 1322: 
 1323:   if (hidden.has(key)) {
 1324:     hidden.delete(key);
 1325:   } else {
 1326:     hidden.add(key);
 1327:   }
 1328: 
 1329:   return Array.from(hidden);
 1330: }
 1331: 
 1332: function withReportTwoSignatureBlock(
 1333:   template: StudioTemplate,
 1334:   payload: SmartReportPayload,
 1335: ): StudioTemplate {
 1336:   const signatures = getReportTwoSignatureCardsFromPayload(payload);
 1337:   let signatureWasPrepared = false;
 1338: 
 1339:   return {
 1340:     ...template,
 1341:     pages: template.pages.map((page) => ({
 1342:       ...page,
 1343:       blocks: page.blocks.flatMap((block) => {
 1344:         if (!isReportTwoSignatureBlock(block)) {
 1345:           return [block];
 1346:         }
 1347: 
 1348:         if (signatureWasPrepared) {
 1349:           return [];
 1350:         }
 1351: 
 1352:         signatureWasPrepared = true;
 1353: 
 1354:         return [{
 1355:           ...block,
 1356:           kind: "signature-grid" as StudioBlockKind,
 1357:           title: block.title || "تواقيع الاعتماد",
 1358:           content: "",
 1359:           variant: block.variant || "soft",
 1360:           source: block.source || "manual",
 1361:           showTitle: block.showTitle ?? false,
 1362:           showMeta: false,
 1363:           align: "center",
 1364:           placement: "flow",
 1365:           signatures: getReportTwoConfiguredSignatureCards(signatures, block),
 1366:         } as StudioBlock];
 1367:       }),
 1368:     })),
 1369:   };
 1370: }
 1371: 
 1372: function getStructuredTableBlock(
 1373:   table: SmartReportTable,
 1374:   sourceBlock?: StudioBlock,
 1375: ): StudioBlock {
 1376:   const settings = table.settings || {};
 1377:   const savedTitle = cleanText(sourceBlock?.title);
 1378:   const normalizedSavedTitle = savedTitle
 1379:     .replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, "$1_$2")
 1380:     .toLowerCase()
 1381:     .replace(/[\s-]+/g, "_")
 1382:     .replace(/^selected_/, "")
 1383:     .replace(/_(?:json|values?)$/, "")
 1384:     .replace(/^_|_$/g, "");
 1385:   const normalizedSourceKey = cleanText(table.sourceFieldKey)
 1386:     .toLowerCase()
 1387:     .replace(/[\s-]+/g, "_")
 1388:     .replace(/^selected_/, "")
 1389:     .replace(/_(?:json|values?)$/, "")
 1390:     .replace(/^_|_$/g, "");
 1391:   const title =
 1392:     !savedTitle || normalizedSavedTitle === normalizedSourceKey ? table.title : savedTitle;
 1393:   const studentDataTable = isStudentDataTable({
 1394:     sourceFieldKey: table.sourceFieldKey,
 1395:     sourceTableId: table.id,
 1396:     columns: table.columns,
 1397:   });
 1398: 
 1399:   return {
 1400:     ...(sourceBlock || createBlock("structured-table")),
 1401:     id: sourceBlock?.id || `structured-table-${table.id.replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/gi, "-")}`,
 1402:     kind: "structured-table",
 1403:     title: studentDataTable ? table.title : title,
 1404:     ...(studentDataTable ? { studentTableTitle: table.title } : {}),
 1405:     visible: sourceBlock?.visible !== false,
 1406:     showTitle: studentDataTable ? true : sourceBlock?.showTitle !== false,
 1407:     source: sourceBlock?.source || "payload-table",
 1408:     sourceTableId: table.id,
 1409:     sourceFieldKey: table.sourceFieldKey,
 1410:     columns: table.columns.map((column) => column.label),
 1411:     columnWidths: table.columns.map((column) => column.width || 0),
 1412:     rows: table.rows.map((row) =>
 1413:       table.columns.map((column) => cleanText(row.cells[column.key])),
 1414:     ),
 1415:     rowMetadata: table.rows.map((row) => ({
 1416:       gender: row.metadata?.gender || null,
 1417:     })),
 1418:     tableSettings: {
 1419:       highlightHeader: true,
 1420:       rounded: true,
 1421:       colorTheme: "light-gray",
 1422:       repeatHeader: settings.repeatHeader !== false,
 1423:       compact: Boolean(settings.compact),
 1424:       stripedRows: settings.stripedRows !== false,
 1425:       highlightFirstColumn: Boolean(settings.highlightFirstColumn),
 1426:       ...(sourceBlock?.tableSettings || {}),
 1427:     },
 1428:   } as StudioBlock;
 1429: }
 1430: 
 1431: function withReportTwoStructuredTables(
 1432:   template: StudioTemplate,
 1433:   payload: SmartReportPayload,
 1434: ): StudioTemplate {
 1435:   const tables = Array.isArray(payload.tables)
 1436:     ? payload.tables.map(normalizeSmartReportTablePresentation)
 1437:     : [];
 1438:   if (!tables.length) {
 1439:     return normalizeStudentDataTableBlockOrder(template) as StudioTemplate;
 1440:   }
 1441: 
 1442:   const tableById = new Map(tables.map((table) => [table.id, table]));
 1443:   const boundTableIds = new Set<string>();
 1444:   let pages = template.pages.map((page) => ({
 1445:     ...page,
 1446:     blocks: page.blocks.flatMap((block) => {
 1447:       const canBindStructuredTable =
 1448:         block.kind === "structured-table" ||
 1449:         Boolean(cleanText(block.sourceTableId) || cleanText(block.sourceFieldKey));
 1450:       if (!canBindStructuredTable) return [block];
 1451:       const sourceTableId = cleanText(block.sourceTableId);
 1452:       const sourceFieldKey = cleanText(block.sourceFieldKey);
 1453:       const table =
 1454:         tableById.get(sourceTableId) ||
 1455:         tables.find((item) => item.sourceFieldKey === sourceFieldKey);
 1456:       if (!table) return [block];
 1457:       if (boundTableIds.has(table.id)) return [];
 1458:       boundTableIds.add(table.id);
 1459:       return [getStructuredTableBlock(table, block)];
 1460:     }),
 1461:   }));
 1462: 
 1463:   const missingTables = tables.filter((table) => !boundTableIds.has(table.id));
 1464:   if (!missingTables.length) {
 1465:     return normalizeStudentDataTableBlockOrder({ ...template, pages }) as StudioTemplate;
 1466:   }
 1467: 
 1468:   let targetPageIndex = -1;
 1469:   for (let index = pages.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
 1470:     if (pages[index].blocks.some((block) => block.kind === "dynamic-fields")) {
 1471:       targetPageIndex = index;
 1472:       break;
 1473:     }
 1474:   }
 1475:   if (targetPageIndex < 0) {
 1476:     targetPageIndex = pages.findIndex((page) => page.kind !== "evidence");
 1477:   }
 1478:   if (targetPageIndex < 0) targetPageIndex = 0;
 1479: 
 1480:   pages = pages.map((page, pageIndex) => {
 1481:     if (pageIndex !== targetPageIndex) return page;
 1482:     const insertionIndex = page.blocks.findIndex(
 1483:       (block) => block.kind === "evidence-gallery" || block.kind === "signature-grid",
 1484:     );
 1485:     const index = insertionIndex < 0 ? page.blocks.length : insertionIndex;
 1486:     return {
 1487:       ...page,
 1488:       blocks: [
 1489:         ...page.blocks.slice(0, index),
 1490:         ...missingTables.map((table) => getStructuredTableBlock(table)),
 1491:         ...page.blocks.slice(index),
 1492:       ],
 1493:     };
 1494:   });
 1495: 
 1496:   return normalizeStudentDataTableBlockOrder({ ...template, pages }) as StudioTemplate;
 1497: }
 1498: 
 1499: function normalizeReportTwoLogicalTemplate(
 1500:   template: StudioTemplate,
 1501:   payload: SmartReportPayload,
 1502: ) {
 1503:   return withSingleReportTwoEvidenceBlock(
 1504:     withReportTwoSignatureBlock(
 1505:       withReportTwoStructuredTables(template, payload),
 1506:       payload,
 1507:     ),
 1508:   );
 1509: }

 1511: function normalizeReportTwoBlockForRuntime(
 1512:   block: StudioBlock,
 1513:   previewCase: ReturnType<typeof getPreviewCase>,
 1514: ): StudioBlock {
 1515:   const sourceBlockId = cleanText(block.sourceBlockId) || block.id;
 1516: 
 1517:   if (isReportTwoDynamicFieldsBlock(block)) {
 1518:     return {
 1519:       ...block,
 1520:       sourceBlockId,
 1521:       kind: "dynamic-fields" as StudioBlockKind,
 1522:       dynamicFields: getDynamicFieldsForBlock(block, previewCase),
 1523:     };
 1524:   }
 1525: 
 1526:   if (block.kind === "evidence-gallery") {
 1527:     return {
 1528:       ...block,
 1529:       sourceBlockId,
 1530:       evidenceStartIndex: Math.max(0, Number(block.evidenceStartIndex || 0)),
 1531:       evidenceEmptyBehavior: previewCase.evidences.length
 1532:         ? block.evidenceEmptyBehavior
 1533:         : block.evidenceEmptyBehavior || "hide",
 1534:     };
 1535:   }
 1536: 
 1537:   return {
 1538:     ...block,
 1539:     sourceBlockId,
 1540:   };
 1541: }

 1543: function prepareReportTwoSemanticTemplate(
 1544:   template: StudioTemplate,
 1545:   previewCase: ReturnType<typeof getPreviewCase>,
 1546: ): StudioTemplate {
 1547:   let evidenceWasPrepared = false;
 1548: 
 1549:   return {
 1550:     ...template,
 1551:     pages: template.pages.map((page) => ({
 1552:       ...page,
 1553:       blocks: page.blocks.flatMap((block) => {
 1554:         if (block.kind === "evidence-gallery") {
 1555:           if (evidenceWasPrepared) {
 1556:             return [];
 1557:           }
 1558: 
 1559:           evidenceWasPrepared = true;
 1560:         }
 1561:

 1563:       }),
 1564:     })),
 1565:   };
 1566: }

 1568: function withSingleReportTwoEvidenceBlock(template: StudioTemplate): StudioTemplate {
 1569:   let evidenceWasKept = false;
 1570: 
 1571:   return {
 1572:     ...template,
 1573:     pages: template.pages.map((page) => ({
 1574:       ...page,
 1575:       blocks: page.blocks.filter((block) => {
 1576:         if (block.kind !== "evidence-gallery") {
 1577:           return true;
 1578:         }
 1579: 
 1580:         if (evidenceWasKept) {
 1581:           return false;
 1582:         }
 1583: 
 1584:         evidenceWasKept = true;
 1585:         return true;
 1586:       }),
 1587:     })),
 1588:   };
 1589: }
 1590: function getWritableReportTwoPageId(
 1591:   activePageId: string,
 1592:   _activePage: StudioPage | undefined,
 1593:   template: StudioTemplate,
 1594: ) {
 1595:   const directPage = template.pages.find((page) => page.id === activePageId);
 1596: 
 1597:   if (directPage) return directPage.id;
 1598: 
 1599:   return template.pages[0]?.id || "";
 1600: }
 1601: 
 1602: function resolveReportTwoEquivalentPageId(
 1603:   pages: StudioPage[],
 1604:   activePageId: string,
 1605: ) {
 1606:   if (!pages.length) return "";
 1607:   if (pages.some((page) => page.id === activePageId)) return activePageId;
 1608: 
 1609:   return pages[0]?.id || "";
 1610: }
 1611: 
 1612: function reorderReportTwoPages(
 1613:   pages: StudioPage[],
 1614:   sourcePageId: string,
 1615:   direction: "previous" | "next",
 1616: ) {
 1617:   const index = pages.findIndex((page) => page.id === sourcePageId);
 1618: 
 1619:   if (index < 0) return pages;
 1620: 
 1621:   const targetIndex = direction === "previous" ? index - 1 : index + 1;
 1622: 
 1623:   if (targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= pages.length) {
 1624:     return pages;
 1625:   }
 1626: 
 1627:   const nextPages = [...pages];
 1628:   const [page] = nextPages.splice(index, 1);
 1629:   nextPages.splice(targetIndex, 0, page);
 1630: 
 1631:   return nextPages;
 1632: }

 1635: function isReportTwoDynamicFieldsBlock(block: StudioBlock | null | undefined) {
 1636:   if (!block) return false;
 1637: 
 1638:   const kind = cleanText(block.kind);
 1639:   const smartKind = cleanText((block as any).settings?.smartBlockKind);
 1640:   const title = cleanText(block.title);
 1641: 
 1642:   return (
 1643:     kind === "dynamic-fields" ||
 1644:     kind === "field-list" ||
 1645:     kind === "case-meta" ||
 1646:     kind === "student-summary" ||
 1647:     kind === "service-summary" ||
 1648:     smartKind === "dynamic-fields" ||
 1649:     smartKind === "field-list" ||
 1650:     title.includes("حقول") ||
 1651:     title.includes("بيانات الحالة")
 1652:   );
 1653: }
 1654: function getReportTwoLookupKey(value: unknown) {
 1655:   return cleanText(value).toLowerCase();
 1656: }
 1657: 
 1658: function getReportTwoDisplayText(value: unknown): string {
 1659:   if (Array.isArray(value)) {
 1660:     return value
 1661:       .map((item) => getReportTwoDisplayText(item))
 1662:       .filter(Boolean)
 1663:       .join("، ");
 1664:   }
 1665: 
 1666:   if (value && typeof value === "object") {
 1667:     const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
 1668: 
 1669:     const candidates = [
 1670:       record.displayValue,
 1671:       record.valueLabel,
 1672:       record.optionLabel,
 1673:       record.selectedOptionLabel,
 1674:       record.labelAr,
 1675:       record.nameAr,
 1676:       record.titleAr,
 1677:       record.label,
 1678:       record.name,
 1679:       record.title,
 1680:       record.text,
 1681:       record.value,
 1682:     ];
 1683: 
 1684:     for (const candidate of candidates) {
 1685:       const text = cleanText(candidate);
 1686: 
 1687:       if (text) return text;
 1688:     }
 1689: 
 1690:     return "";
 1691:   }
 1692: 
 1693:   return cleanText(value);
 1694: }
 1695: 
 1696: function getReportTwoOptionCollections(field: any) {
 1697:   return [
 1698:     field.options,
 1699:     field.dynamicFieldOptions,
 1700:     field.fieldOptions,
 1701:     field.choices,
 1702:     field.items,
 1703:     field.field?.options,
 1704:     field.field?.dynamicFieldOptions,
 1705:     field.dynamicField?.options,
 1706:     field.workflowField?.options,
 1707:   ].filter(Array.isArray) as any[][];
 1708: }
 1709: 
 1710: function getReportTwoOptionArabicLabel(field: any, rawValue: unknown) {
 1711:   const target = getReportTwoLookupKey(rawValue);
 1712: 
 1713:   if (!target) return "";
 1714: 
 1715:   for (const options of getReportTwoOptionCollections(field)) {
 1716:     for (const option of options) {
 1717:       const optionValues = [
 1718:         option.value,
 1719:         option.key,
 1720:         option.code,
 1721:         option.slug,
 1722:         option.id,
 1723:       ].map(getReportTwoLookupKey);
 1724: 
 1725:       if (!optionValues.includes(target)) {
 1726:         continue;
 1727:       }
 1728: 
 1729:       const label = getReportTwoDisplayText({
 1730:         displayValue: option.displayValue,
 1731:         valueLabel: option.valueLabel,
 1732:         optionLabel: option.optionLabel,
 1733:         labelAr: option.labelAr,
 1734:         nameAr: option.nameAr,
 1735:         titleAr: option.titleAr,
 1736:         label: option.label,
 1737:         name: option.name,
 1738:         title: option.title,
 1739:         text: option.text,
 1740:       });
 1741: 
 1742:       if (label) return label;
 1743:     }
 1744:   }
 1745: 
 1746:   return "";
 1747: }
 1748: 
 1749: function getReportTwoReadableFieldLabel(field: any, index: number) {
 1750:   const key =
 1751:     cleanText(field.key) ||
 1752:     cleanText(field.fieldKey) ||
 1753:     cleanText(field.name) ||
 1754:     cleanText(field.id) ||
 1755:     `field-${index + 1}`;
 1756: 
 1757:   const candidates = [
 1758:     field.displayLabel,
 1759:     field.fieldLabel,
 1760:     field.labelAr,
 1761:     field.nameAr,
 1762:     field.titleAr,
 1763:     field.label,
 1764:     field.title,
 1765:     field.field?.label,
 1766:     field.dynamicField?.label,
 1767:     field.workflowField?.label,
 1768:   ];
 1769: 
 1770:   for (const candidate of candidates) {
 1771:     const text = cleanText(candidate);
 1772: 
 1773:     if (text && text !== key) return text;
 1774:   }
 1775: 
 1776:   const fromMap = getReportTwoFieldLabel(key, field.label);
 1777: 
 1778:   if (fromMap && fromMap !== key) return fromMap;
 1779: 
 1780:   return key;
 1781: }
 1782: 
 1783: function uniqueReportTwoValueItems(items: string[]) {
 1784:   return Array.from(
 1785:     new Set(items.map((item) => cleanText(item)).filter(Boolean)),
 1786:   );
 1787: }
 1788: 
 1789: function getReportTwoReadableFieldValueItems(field: any) {
 1790:   const directArrays = [
 1791:     field.valueItems,
 1792:     field.values,
 1793:     field.items,
 1794:     field.selectedValues,
 1795:     field.selectedOptions,
 1796:     Array.isArray(field.value) ? field.value : null,
 1797:   ].filter(Array.isArray) as unknown[][];
 1798: 
 1799:   const items = uniqueReportTwoValueItems(
 1800:     directArrays.flatMap((entries) =>
 1801:       entries.map((entry) => getReportTwoDisplayText(entry)).filter(Boolean),
 1802:     ),
 1803:   );
 1804: 
 1805:   return items.length > 1 ? items : [];
 1806: }
 1807: 
 1808: function getReportTwoReadableFieldValue(field: any) {
 1809:   const readableItems = getReportTwoReadableFieldValueItems(field);
 1810: 
 1811:   if (readableItems.length > 1) {
 1812:     return readableItems.join("، ");
 1813:   }
 1814: 
 1815:   const explicitCandidates = [
 1816:     field.displayValue,
 1817:     field.formattedValue,
 1818:     field.valueLabel,
 1819:     field.labelValue,
 1820:     field.optionLabel,
 1821:     field.selectedOptionLabel,
 1822:     field.answerLabel,
 1823:     field.answerText,
 1824:     field.textValue,
 1825:     field.valueText,
 1826:   ];
 1827: 
 1828:   for (const candidate of explicitCandidates) {
 1829:     const text = getReportTwoDisplayText(candidate);
 1830: 
 1831:     if (text) return text;
 1832:   }
 1833: 
 1834:   const optionLabel = getReportTwoOptionArabicLabel(field, field.value);
 1835: 
 1836:   if (optionLabel) return optionLabel;
 1837: 
 1838:   const rawValue = cleanText(field.value);
 1839: 
 1840:   if (!rawValue) return "";
 1841: 
 1842:   const fromMap = getReportTwoFieldValue(rawValue);
 1843: 
 1844:   if (fromMap && fromMap !== rawValue) return fromMap;
 1845: 
 1846:   return rawValue;
 1847: }
 1848: 
 1849: function getReportTwoDynamicFieldsFromPreviewCase(
 1850:   previewCase: ReturnType<typeof getPreviewCase>,
 1851: ): ReportTwoDynamicField[] {
 1852:   const fields = (previewCase.values || [])
 1853:     .map((item: any, index: number) => {
 1854:       const key = cleanText(item.fieldKey) || `workflow-field-${index + 1}`;
 1855:       const label = cleanText(item.fieldLabel) || key || `حقل ${index + 1}`;
 1856:       const value = cleanText(item.value);
 1857:       const valueItems = Array.isArray(item.valueItems)
 1858:         ? uniqueReportTwoValueItems(item.valueItems)
 1859:         : [];
 1860: 
 1861:       return {
 1862:         id: key || `workflow-field-${index + 1}`,
 1863:         key,
 1864:         label,
 1865:         value,
 1866:         ...(valueItems.length > 1 ? { valueItems } : {}),
 1867:         visible: Boolean(label && (value || valueItems.length)),
 1868:       };
 1869:     })
 1870:     .filter((item) => item.label && (item.value || item.valueItems?.length))
 1871:     .filter(
 1872:       (item) =>
 1873:         !previewCase.hasStudentDataTable || !isStudentIdentityField(item),
 1874:     );
 1875: 
 1876:   return ensureUniqueReportTwoDynamicFieldIds(fields);
 1877: }
 1878: 
 1879: function getDynamicFieldsForBlock(
 1880:   block: StudioBlock | null,
 1881:   previewCase: ReturnType<typeof getPreviewCase>,
 1882: ) {
 1883:   const sourceFields = getReportTwoDynamicFieldsFromPreviewCase(previewCase);
 1884: 
 1885:   if (!block?.dynamicFields?.length) {
 1886:     return sourceFields;
 1887:   }
 1888: 
 1889:   const sourceByKey = new Map<string, ReportTwoDynamicField>();
 1890: 
 1891:   sourceFields.forEach((field) => {
 1892:     [field.id, field.key, field.label].forEach((value) => {
 1893:       const key = getReportTwoLookupKey(value);
 1894: 
 1895:       if (key) {
 1896:         sourceByKey.set(key, field);
 1897:       }
 1898:     });
 1899:   });
 1900: 
 1901:   const fields = block.dynamicFields
 1902:     .filter(
 1903:       (field) =>
 1904:         !previewCase.hasStudentDataTable || !isStudentIdentityField(field),
 1905:     )
 1906:     .map((field, index) => {
 1907:     const source =
 1908:       sourceByKey.get(getReportTwoLookupKey(field.key)) ||
 1909:       sourceByKey.get(getReportTwoLookupKey(field.id)) ||
 1910:       sourceByKey.get(getReportTwoLookupKey(field.label)) ||
 1911:       sourceFields[index];
 1912: 
 1913:     const key = field.key || source?.key || `dynamic-field-${index + 1}`;
 1914:     const configuredValue = cleanText(field.value);
 1915:     const configuredValueItems = Array.isArray(field.valueItems)
 1916:       ? uniqueReportTwoValueItems(field.valueItems)
 1917:       : [];
 1918:     const sourceValue = cleanText(source?.value);
 1919:     const sourceValueItems = Array.isArray(source?.valueItems)
 1920:       ? uniqueReportTwoValueItems(source.valueItems)
 1921:       : [];
 1922:     const matchesSourceValue =
 1923:       Boolean(configuredValue && sourceValue) &&
 1924:       getReportTwoLookupKey(configuredValue) === getReportTwoLookupKey(sourceValue);
 1925:     const shouldUseSourceValueItems =
 1926:       sourceValueItems.length > 1 &&
 1927:       ((configuredValueItems.length > 0 &&
 1928:         configuredValueItems.every((item) => isReportTwoTechnicalValue(item))) ||
 1929:         (configuredValueItems.length === 0 &&
 1930:           (!configuredValue ||
 1931:             isReportTwoTechnicalValue(configuredValue) ||
 1932:             matchesSourceValue)));
 1933:     const valueItems = shouldUseSourceValueItems
 1934:       ? sourceValueItems
 1935:       : configuredValueItems;
 1936:     const value =
 1937:       configuredValue && !shouldUseSourceValueItems
 1938:         ? configuredValue
 1939:         : sourceValue || getReportTwoFieldValue(field.value) || configuredValue;
 1940: 
 1941:     return {
 1942:       id: field.id || source?.id || `dynamic-field-${index + 1}`,
 1943:       key,
 1944:       label: field.label ?? cleanText(source?.label) ?? getReportTwoFieldLabel(field.key, field.label) ?? key,
 1945:       value,
 1946:       ...(valueItems.length > 1 ? { valueItems } : {}),
 1947:       visible: field.visible !== false,
 1948:     };
 1949:   });
 1950: 
 1951:   return ensureUniqueReportTwoDynamicFieldIds(fields);
 1952: }
 1953: function getReportTwoTableSettings(
 1954:   settings?: Record<string, any>,
 1955: ): ReportTwoTableSettings {
 1956:   const validThemes = ["light-gray", "soft-blue", "green", "none"] as const;
 1957:   const colorTheme = settings?.colorTheme;
 1958:   return {
 1959:     highlightHeader: settings?.highlightHeader !== false,
 1960:     highlightFirstColumn: settings?.highlightFirstColumn !== false,
 1961:     stripedRows: settings?.stripedRows !== false,
 1962:     rounded: settings?.rounded !== false,
 1963:     compact: Boolean(settings?.compact),
 1964:     repeatHeader: settings?.repeatHeader !== false,
 1965:     colorTheme: validThemes.includes(colorTheme) ? colorTheme : "light-gray",
 1966:   };
 1967: }
 1968: 
 1969: function normalizeReportTwoTableColumns(columns?: string[]) {
 1970:   const cleaned = Array.isArray(columns)
 1971:     ? columns.map((column) => cleanText(column)).filter(Boolean)
 1972:     : [];
 1973: 
 1974:   return cleaned.length ? cleaned : ["المجال", "الإجراء", "ملاحظات"];
 1975: }
 1976: 
 1977: function normalizeReportTwoTableRows(rows: unknown, columnsCount: number) {
 1978:   const sourceRows = Array.isArray(rows) && rows.length
 1979:     ? rows
 1980:     : [
 1981:         Array.from({ length: columnsCount }).map(() => ""),
 1982:         Array.from({ length: columnsCount }).map(() => ""),
 1983:         Array.from({ length: columnsCount }).map(() => ""),
 1984:       ];
 1985: 
 1986:   return sourceRows.map((row: any) => {
 1987:     const cells = Array.isArray(row) ? row : [];
 1988:     const normalized = Array.from({ length: columnsCount }).map((_, index) =>
 1989:       cleanText(cells[index]),
 1990:     );
 1991: 
 1992:     return normalized;
 1993:   });
 1994: }
 1995: 
 1996: function createReportTwoTableDraft(block: StudioBlock): ReportTwoTableDraft {
 1997:   const columns = normalizeReportTwoTableColumns(block.columns);
 1998:   const rows = normalizeReportTwoTableRows(block.rows, columns.length);
 1999: 
 2000:   return {
 2001:     blockId: block.id,
 2002:     title: cleanText(block.title) || "جدول",
 2003:     columns,
 2004:     rows,
 2005:     settings: getReportTwoTableSettings(block.tableSettings),
 2006:   };
 2007: }
 2008: 
 2009: function countReportTwoFilledCells(rows: string[][]) {
 2010:   return rows.reduce(
 2011:     (total, row) =>
 2012:       total + row.filter((cell) => cleanText(cell).length > 0).length,
 2013:     0,
 2014:   );
 2015: }

 2018:   draft: ReportTwoTableDraft,
 2019:   title: string,
 2020: ): ReportTwoTableDraft {
 2021:   return {
 2022:     ...draft,
 2023:     title,
 2024:   };
 2025: }

 2027: function updateReportTwoTableColumn(
 2028:   draft: ReportTwoTableDraft,
 2029:   columnIndex: number,
 2030:   value: string,
 2031: ): ReportTwoTableDraft {
 2032:   const columns = draft.columns.map((column, index) =>
 2033:     index === columnIndex ? value : column,
 2034:   );
 2035: 
 2036:   return {
 2037:     ...draft,
 2038:     columns,
 2039:     rows: normalizeReportTwoTableRows(draft.rows, columns.length),
 2040:   };
 2041: }
 2042: 
 2043: function updateReportTwoTableCell(
 2044:   draft: ReportTwoTableDraft,
 2045:   rowIndex: number,
 2046:   columnIndex: number,
 2047:   value: string,
 2048: ): ReportTwoTableDraft {
 2049:   const rows = normalizeReportTwoTableRows(draft.rows, draft.columns.length);
 2050: 
 2051:   rows[rowIndex] = rows[rowIndex].map((cell, index) =>
 2052:     index === columnIndex ? value : cell,
 2053:   );
 2054: 
 2055:   return {
 2056:     ...draft,
 2057:     rows,
 2058:   };
 2059: }

 2061: function addReportTwoTableRow(draft: ReportTwoTableDraft): ReportTwoTableDraft {
 2062:   return {
 2063:     ...draft,
 2064:     rows: [
 2065:       ...normalizeReportTwoTableRows(draft.rows, draft.columns.length),
 2066:       Array.from({ length: draft.columns.length }).map(() => ""),
 2067:     ],
 2068:   };
 2069: }
 2070: 
 2071: function removeReportTwoTableRow(
 2072:   draft: ReportTwoTableDraft,
 2073:   rowIndex: number,
 2074: ): ReportTwoTableDraft {
 2075:   const rows = normalizeReportTwoTableRows(draft.rows, draft.columns.length);
 2076: 
 2077:   if (rows.length <= 1) {
 2078:     return draft;
 2079:   }
 2080: 
 2081:   return {
 2082:     ...draft,
 2083:     rows: rows.filter((_, index) => index !== rowIndex),
 2084:   };
 2085: }
 2086: 
 2087: function addReportTwoTableColumn(
 2088:   draft: ReportTwoTableDraft,
 2089: ): ReportTwoTableDraft {
 2090:   const columns = [...draft.columns, `عمود ${draft.columns.length + 1}`];
 2091: 
 2092:   return {
 2093:     ...draft,
 2094:     columns,
 2095:     rows: normalizeReportTwoTableRows(draft.rows, draft.columns.length).map(
 2096:       (row) => [...row, ""],
 2097:     ),
 2098:   };
 2099: }
 2100: 
 2101: function removeReportTwoTableColumn(
 2102:   draft: ReportTwoTableDraft,
 2103:   columnIndex: number,
 2104: ): ReportTwoTableDraft {
 2105:   if (draft.columns.length <= 1) {
 2106:     return draft;
 2107:   }
 2108: 
 2109:   const columns = draft.columns.filter((_, index) => index !== columnIndex);
 2110:   const rows = normalizeReportTwoTableRows(draft.rows, draft.columns.length).map(
 2111:     (row) => row.filter((_, index) => index !== columnIndex),
 2112:   );
 2113: 
 2114:   return {
 2115:     ...draft,
 2116:     columns,
 2117:     rows: normalizeReportTwoTableRows(rows, columns.length),
 2118:   };
 2119: }

 2121: function updateReportTwoTableSetting(
 2122:   draft: ReportTwoTableDraft,

 2129:       ...draft.settings,
 2130:       [key]: value,
 2131:     },
 2132:   };

 2135: function isReportTwoTechnicalValue(value: unknown) {
 2136:   const text = cleanText(value);
 2137: 
 2138:   if (!text) return false;
 2139: 
 2140:   return /^[a-z0-9_./-]+$/i.test(text) && /[a-z_]/i.test(text);
 2141: }
