 2143: function getReportTwoFilledTableCells(block: StudioBlock) {
 2144:   const columns = normalizeReportTwoTableColumns(block.columns);
 2145:   const rows = normalizeReportTwoTableRows(block.rows, columns.length);
 2146: 
 2147:   return countReportTwoFilledCells(rows);
 2148: }
 2149: 
 2150: function getReportTwoSmartAlertSortValue(type: ReportTwoSmartAlertType) {
 2151:   if (type === "error") return 0;
 2152:   if (type === "warning") return 1;
 2153:   if (type === "info") return 2;
 2154: 
 2155:   return 3;
 2156: }
 2157: 
 2158: function getReportTwoSmartAlerts({
 2159:   visiblePreviewTemplate,
 2160:   previewCase,
 2161:   activeHeaderValues,
 2162:   activeLogoSettings,
 2163: }: {
 2164:   visiblePreviewTemplate: StudioTemplate;
 2165:   previewCase: ReturnType<typeof getPreviewCase>;
 2166:   activeHeaderValues: ReportTwoHeaderValues;
 2167:   activeLogoSettings: ReportTwoLogoSettings;
 2168: }) {
 2169:   const alerts: ReportTwoSmartAlert[] = [];
 2170:   const pages = visiblePreviewTemplate.pages || [];
 2171: 
 2172:   if (!pages.length) {
 2173:     alerts.push({
 2174:       id: "no-pages",
 2175:       type: "error",
 2176:       title: "لا توجد صفحات في التقرير",
 2177:       description: "أضف صفحة محتوى حتى يظهر التقرير بشكل صحيح.",
 2178:       action: "open-page",
 2179:     });
 2180: 
 2181:     return alerts;
 2182:   }
 2183: 
 2184:   if (!cleanText(activeLogoSettings.url)) {
 2185:     alerts.push({
 2186:       id: "missing-logo",
 2187:       type: "warning",
 2188:       title: "الشعار غير محدد",
 2189:       description: "ضع شعارًا للتقرير أو استخدم الشعار الافتراضي.",
 2190:       action: "restore-header",
 2191:     });
 2192:   }
 2193: 
 2194:   const missingHeaderFields = Object.entries(activeHeaderValues).filter(
 2195:     ([, value]) => !cleanText(value),
 2196:   );
 2197: 
 2198:   if (missingHeaderFields.length) {
 2199:     alerts.push({
 2200:       id: "missing-header-values",
 2201:       type: "warning",
 2202:       title: "الترويسة غير مكتملة",
 2203:       description: "بعض قيم أعلى الصفحة فارغة. يمكن استعادتها من بيانات الحالة.",
 2204:       action: "restore-header",
 2205:     });
 2206:   }
 2207: 
 2208:   pages.forEach((page, pageIndex) => {
 2209:     if (!page.blocks.length) {
 2210:       alerts.push({
 2211:         id: `empty-page-${page.id}`,
 2212:         type: "warning",
 2213:         title: "صفحة بدون محتوى",
 2214:         description: `الصفحة ${pageIndex + 1} لا تحتوي على بلوكات.`,
 2215:         pageId: page.id,
 2216:         action: "open-page",
 2217:       });
 2218:     }
 2219: 
 2220:     page.blocks.forEach((block) => {
 2221:       if (isReportTwoDynamicFieldsBlock(block)) {
 2222:         const fields = getDynamicFieldsForBlock(block, previewCase);
 2223:         const visibleFields = fields.filter((field) => field.visible !== false);
 2224:         const technicalFields = visibleFields.filter(
 2225:           (field) =>
 2226:             isReportTwoTechnicalValue(field.label) ||
 2227:             isReportTwoTechnicalValue(field.value),
 2228:         );
 2229: 
 2230:         if (!visibleFields.length) {
 2231:           alerts.push({
 2232:             id: `no-visible-fields-${page.id}-${block.id}`,
 2233:             type: "warning",
 2234:             title: "لا توجد حقول ظاهرة",
 2235:             description: "بلوك الحقول موجود، لكن كل الحقول مخفية أو فارغة.",
 2236:             pageId: page.id,
 2237:             blockId: block.id,
 2238:             action: "open-block",
 2239:           });
 2240:         }
 2241: 
 2242:         if (visibleFields.length > 14) {
 2243:           alerts.push({
 2244:             id: `too-many-fields-${page.id}-${block.id}`,
 2245:             type: "warning",
 2246:             title: "حقول كثيرة في صفحة واحدة",
 2247:             description: "عدد الحقول كبير وقد يصعّب قراءة التقرير. أخفِ غير المهم أو انقل جزءًا لصفحة أخرى.",
 2248:             pageId: page.id,
 2249:             blockId: block.id,
 2250:             action: "open-block",
 2251:           });
 2252:         }
 2253: 
 2254:         if (technicalFields.length) {
 2255:           alerts.push({
 2256:             id: `technical-fields-${page.id}-${block.id}`,
 2257:             type: "warning",
 2258:             title: "حقول تقنية ظاهرة",
 2259:             description: "توجد أسماء أو قيم تقنية مثل activity_domain أو program_02. يمكن إخفاؤها تلقائيًا.",
 2260:             pageId: page.id,
 2261:             blockId: block.id,
 2262:             action: "hide-technical-fields",
 2263:           });
 2264:         }
 2265:       }
 2266: 
 2267:       if (block.kind === "evidence-gallery") {
 2268:         const evidenceCount = previewCase.evidences.length;
 2269:         const perPage = getEvidencePerPageFromBlock(block);
 2270: 
 2271:         if (!evidenceCount) {
 2272:           alerts.push({
 2273:             id: `no-evidence-${page.id}-${block.id}`,
 2274:             type: "info",
 2275:             title: "لا توجد شواهد",
 2276:             description: "التقرير يحتوي على بلوك شواهد، لكن لا توجد مرفقات في الحالة.",
 2277:             pageId: page.id,
 2278:             blockId: block.id,
 2279:             action: "open-block",
 2280:           });
 2281:         }
 2282: 
 2283:         if (evidenceCount > perPage) {
 2284:           alerts.push({
 2285:             id: `evidence-overflow-${page.id}-${block.id}`,
 2286:             type: "info",
 2287:             title: "الشواهد موزعة على صفحات",
 2288:             description: `يوجد ${evidenceCount} شواهد، ويظهر ${perPage} في الصفحة. الباقي ينتقل تلقائيًا لصفحات شواهد.`,
 2289:             pageId: page.id,
 2290:             blockId: block.id,
 2291:             action: "focus-preview",
 2292:           });
 2293:         }
 2294:       }
 2295: 
 2296:       if (block.kind === "signature-grid") return 34;
 2297: 
 2298:   if (block.kind === "report-one-table") {
 2299:         const columns = normalizeReportTwoTableColumns(block.columns);
 2300:         const rows = normalizeReportTwoTableRows(block.rows, columns.length);
 2301:         const filledCells = getReportTwoFilledTableCells(block);
 2302: 
 2303:         if (!filledCells) {
 2304:           alerts.push({
 2305:             id: `empty-table-${page.id}-${block.id}`,
 2306:             type: "info",
 2307:             title: "جدول فارغ",
 2308:             description: "يوجد جدول بدون محتوى. عدّله أو احذفه إذا لم يكن مطلوبًا.",
 2309:             pageId: page.id,
 2310:             blockId: block.id,
 2311:             action: "open-block",
 2312:           });
 2313:         }
 2314: 
 2315:         if (columns.length > 4 || rows.length > 7) {
 2316:           alerts.push({
 2317:             id: `large-table-${page.id}-${block.id}`,
 2318:             type: "warning",
 2319:             title: "جدول كبير",
 2320:             description: "الجدول كبير وقد لا يكون مريحًا داخل A4. يفضّل تقليل الأعمدة أو نقله لصفحة مستقلة.",
 2321:             pageId: page.id,
 2322:             blockId: block.id,
 2323:             action: "open-block",
 2324:           });
 2325:         }
 2326:       }
 2327: 
 2328:       if (
 2329:         (block.kind === "section-text" || block.kind === "multi-paragraph") &&
 2330:         cleanText(block.content).length > 700
 2331:       ) {
 2332:         alerts.push({
 2333:           id: `long-text-${page.id}-${block.id}`,
 2334:           type: "warning",
 2335:           title: "فقرة طويلة",
 2336:           description: "الفقرة طويلة وقد تضغط الصفحة. اختصرها أو انقل جزءًا منها لبلوك جديد.",
 2337:           pageId: page.id,
 2338:           blockId: block.id,
 2339:           action: "open-block",
 2340:         });
 2341:       }
 2342:     });
 2343:   });
 2344: 
 2345:   if (!alerts.length) {
 2346:     alerts.push({
 2347:       id: "ready",
 2348:       type: "success",
 2349:       title: "التقرير يبدو جيدًا",
 2350:       description: "لا توجد تنبيهات مهمة حاليًا. يمكنك المتابعة بأمان.",
 2351:       action: "focus-preview",
 2352:     });
 2353:   }
 2354: 
 2355:   return alerts.sort(
 2356:     (firstAlert, secondAlert) =>
 2357:       getReportTwoSmartAlertSortValue(firstAlert.type) -
 2358:       getReportTwoSmartAlertSortValue(secondAlert.type),
 2359:   );
 2360: }
 2361: 
 2362: function getReportTwoFinalCheckItems({
 2363:   smartAlerts,
 2364:   visiblePreviewTemplate,
 2365:   activeHeaderValues,
 2366:   activeLogoSettings,
 2367:   lastAutoSavedAt,
 2368: }: {
 2369:   smartAlerts: ReportTwoSmartAlert[];
 2370:   visiblePreviewTemplate: StudioTemplate;
 2371:   activeHeaderValues: ReportTwoHeaderValues;
 2372:   activeLogoSettings: ReportTwoLogoSettings;
 2373:   lastAutoSavedAt: string;
 2374: }): ReportTwoFinalCheckItem[] {
 2375:   const errors = smartAlerts.filter((alert) => alert.type === "error");
 2376:   const warnings = smartAlerts.filter((alert) => alert.type === "warning");
 2377:   const pages = visiblePreviewTemplate.pages || [];
 2378:   const hasPages = pages.length > 0;
 2379:   const hasBlocks = pages.some((page) => page.blocks.length > 0);
 2380:   const headerComplete = Object.values(activeHeaderValues).every((value) =>
 2381:     Boolean(cleanText(value)),
 2382:   );
 2383:   const logoReady = Boolean(cleanText(activeLogoSettings.url));
 2384: 
 2385:   return [
 2386:     {
 2387:       id: "pages",
 2388:       label: "صفحات التقرير موجودة",
 2389:       description: hasPages
 2390:         ? `عدد الصفحات الحالية: ${pages.length}.`
 2391:         : "لا توجد صفحات داخل التقرير.",
 2392:       passed: hasPages,
 2393:       required: true,
 2394:     },
 2395:     {
 2396:       id: "blocks",
 2397:       label: "يوجد محتوى داخل التقرير",
 2398:       description: hasBlocks
 2399:         ? "التقرير يحتوي على بلوكات ومحتوى قابل للعرض."
 2400:         : "أضف بلوكًا واحدًا على الأقل قبل الاعتماد.",
 2401:       passed: hasBlocks,
 2402:       required: true,
 2403:     },
 2404:     {
 2405:       id: "header",
 2406:       label: "ترويسة التقرير مكتملة",
 2407:       description: headerComplete
 2408:         ? "قيم الترويسة الأساسية مكتملة."
 2409:         : "هناك قيمة أو أكثر فارغة في الترويسة.",
 2410:       passed: headerComplete,
 2411:       required: true,
 2412:     },
 2413:     {
 2414:       id: "logo",
 2415:       label: "الشعار جاهز",
 2416:       description: logoReady
 2417:         ? "يوجد شعار مستخدم في الترويسة."
 2418:         : "اختر شعارًا أو استعد الشعار الافتراضي.",
 2419:       passed: logoReady,
 2420:       required: false,
 2421:     },
 2422:     {
 2423:       id: "errors",
 2424:       label: "لا توجد أخطاء مانعة",
 2425:       description: errors.length
 2426:         ? `يوجد ${errors.length} خطأ مانع يحتاج إصلاحًا.`
 2427:         : "لا توجد أخطاء مانعة حاليًا.",
 2428:       passed: errors.length === 0,
 2429:       required: true,
 2430:     },
 2431:     {
 2432:       id: "warnings",
 2433:       label: "التنبيهات تمت مراجعتها",
 2434:       description: warnings.length
 2435:         ? `يوجد ${warnings.length} تنبيه. يمكن الاعتماد بعد مراجعتها.`
 2436:         : "لا توجد تنبيهات مهمة.",
 2437:       passed: warnings.length === 0,
 2438:       required: false,
 2439:     },
 2440:     {
 2441:       id: "draft",
 2442:       label: "المسودة محفوظة",
 2443:       description: lastAutoSavedAt
 2444:         ? `آخر حفظ: ${formatReportTwoSavedAt(lastAutoSavedAt)}.`
 2445:         : "سيتم حفظ المسودة تلقائيًا قبل التأكيد.",
 2446:       passed: Boolean(lastAutoSavedAt),
 2447:       required: false,
 2448:     },
 2449:   ];
 2450: }
 2451: 
 2452: function getReportTwoFinalCheckPassed(items: ReportTwoFinalCheckItem[]) {
 2453:   return items.every((item) => !item.required || item.passed);
 2454: }
 2455: function getReportTwoPreparedExecutionSummary(payload: SmartReportPayload) {
 2456:   return cleanText(
 2457:     (payload as any)?.narrative?.body ||
 2458:       (payload as any)?.executionSummary ||
 2459:       (payload as any)?.summary ||
 2460:       "",
 2461:   );
 2462: }
 2463: 
 2464: function isReportTwoExecutionSummaryPlaceholderText(input: unknown) {
 2465:   const content = cleanText(input).toLowerCase();
 2466: 
 2467:   if (!content) return true;
 2468:   if (content.includes("workflow")) return true;
 2469:   if (content.includes("تم توثيق هذا الجزء")) return true;
 2470:   if (content.includes("اكتب النص هنا")) return true;
 2471:   if (content.includes("{{execution")) return true;
 2472:   if (content.includes("{{report.execution")) return true;
 2473:   if (content.includes("{{case.execution")) return true;
 2474: 
 2475:   return false;
 2476: }
 2477: 
 2478: function isReportTwoExecutionSummaryBlock(block: StudioBlock) {
 2479:   if (
 2480:     block.kind !== "section-text" &&
 2481:     block.kind !== "multi-paragraph" &&
 2482:     block.kind !== "plain-text"
 2483:   ) {
 2484:     return false;
 2485:   }
 2486: 
 2487:   const title = cleanText(block.title).toLowerCase();
 2488:   const content = cleanText(block.content).toLowerCase();
 2489: 
 2490:   if (title.includes("وصف") && title.includes("تنفيذ")) return true;
 2491:   if (content.includes("workflow")) return true;
 2492:   if (content.includes("تم توثيق هذا الجزء")) return true;
 2493: 
 2494:   return false;
 2495: }
 2496: 
 2497: function shouldAutoApplyReportTwoExecutionSummary(block: StudioBlock) {
 2498:   if (!isReportTwoExecutionSummaryBlock(block)) {
 2499:     return false;
 2500:   }
 2501: 
 2502:   const rawBlock = block as any;
 2503: 
 2504:   if (rawBlock.autoFilledFromExecutionSummary === true) {
 2505:     return true;
 2506:   }
 2507: 
 2508:   return isReportTwoExecutionSummaryPlaceholderText(block.content);
 2509: }
 2510: 
 2511: function applyReportTwoPreparedExecutionSummary(
 2512:   template: StudioTemplate,
 2513:   payload: SmartReportPayload,
 2514: ): StudioTemplate {
 2515:   const showExecutionDescriptionInReport =
 2516:     (payload as any)?.narrative?.visible !== false;
 2517: 
 2518:   if (!showExecutionDescriptionInReport) {
 2519:     return {
 2520:       ...template,
 2521:       pages: template.pages.map((page) => ({
 2522:         ...page,
 2523:         blocks: page.blocks.filter(
 2524:           (block) => !isReportTwoExecutionSummaryBlock(block),
 2525:         ),
 2526:       })),
 2527:     };
 2528:   }
 2529: 
 2530:   const summary = getReportTwoPreparedExecutionSummary(payload);
 2531: 
 2532:   if (!summary) return template;
 2533: 
 2534:   let replaced = false;
 2535: 
 2536:   return {
 2537:     ...template,
 2538:     pages: template.pages.map((page) => ({
 2539:       ...page,
 2540:       blocks: page.blocks.map((block) => {
 2541:         if (replaced || !shouldAutoApplyReportTwoExecutionSummary(block)) {
 2542:           return block;
 2543:         }
 2544: 
 2545:         replaced = true;
 2546: 
 2547:         return {
 2548:           ...block,
 2549:           content: summary,
 2550:           autoFilledFromExecutionSummary: true,
 2551:         };
 2552:       }),
 2553:     })),
 2554:   };
 2555: }
 2556: function getBlockKindName(kind: StudioBlockKind) {
 2557:   if (kind === "hero-title") return "عنوان رئيسي";
 2558:   if (kind === "meta-strip") return "شريط بيانات";
 2559:   if (kind === "plain-text") return "نص بسيط";
 2560:   if (kind === "section-text") return "فقرة";
 2561:   if (kind === "multi-paragraph") return "فقرات";
 2562:   if (kind === "highlight-note") return "ملاحظة بارزة";
 2563:   if (kind === "bullet-list") return "قائمة";
 2564:   if (kind === "dynamic-fields") return "حقول الحالة";
 2565:   if (kind === "evidence-gallery") return "الشواهد";
 2566:   if (kind === "closing-note") return "خاتمة";
 2567:   if (kind === "report-one-table") return "جدول";
 2568:   if (kind === "structured-table") return "جدول بيانات";
 2569:   if (kind === "signature-grid") return "تواقيع الاعتماد";
 2570: 
 2571:   return "بلوك";
 2572: }

 2897:   function fixReportTwoSmartAlert(alert: ReportTwoSmartAlert) {
 2898:     if (alert.action === "hide-technical-fields") {
 2899:       setTemplate((current) => ({
 2900:         ...current,
 2901:         pages: current.pages.map((page) => ({

 2913:                   !isReportTwoTechnicalValue(field.label) &&
 2914:                   !isReportTwoTechnicalValue(field.value),
 2915:               }),
 2916:             );
 2917: 
 2918:             return {
 2919:               ...block,
 2920:               dynamicFields: fields,
 2921:             };
 2922:           }),
 2923:         })),
 2924:       }));
 2925: 
 2926:       return;
 2927:     }

 2930:       setHeaderValues(null);
 2931:       setHeaderAlignments(null);
 2932:       setLogoSettings(null);
 2933:       return;
 2934:     }
 2935:

 2937:   }

 3009:   function sanitizeReportTwoPdfFileNamePart(value: unknown) {
 3010:     return cleanText(value)
 3011:       .replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]+/g, "-")
 3012:       .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
 3013:       .replace(/-+/g, "-")
 3014:       .trim();
 3015:   }

 3024:           (preparedPayload as any)?.caseInfo?.title ||
 3025:           defaultTitle,
 3026:       ) || defaultTitle
 3027:     );
 3028: 
 3029:     return (
 3030:       sanitizeReportTwoPdfFileNamePart(
 3031:         (previewCase as any)?.title ||
 3032:           (previewCase as any)?.caseTitle ||
 3033:           (preparedPayload as any)?.caseInfo?.title ||
 3034:           "تقرير معتمد",
 3035:       ) || "تقرير معتمد"

 3052:   function buildReportTwoStudioUrl(nextMode: "preview" | "edit") {
 3053:     const params = new URLSearchParams();
 3054: 
 3055:     params.set("mode", nextMode);
 3056: 
 3057:     if (selectedVariantId) {
 3058:       params.set("variant", selectedVariantId);
 3059:     }
 3060: 
 3061:     if (selectedTemplateOptionId) {
 3062:       params.set("templateId", selectedTemplateOptionId);
 3063:     }
 3064: 
 3065:     return `/dashboard/report-2/cases/${encodeURIComponent(caseId)}/studio?${params.toString()}`;
 3066:   }

 3079:     }

 3084:       query ? `?${query}` : ""
 3085:     }`;

 3088:   function buildReportTwoPdfExportSnapshot() {
 3089:     if (!isReportDesignId(template.designTemplateId)) {
 3090:       throw new Error("REPORT_TWO_PRINT_DESIGN_MISSING");
 3091:     }
 3092: 
 3093:     return {
 3094:       template: signedVisiblePreviewTemplate,
 3095:       context: editableRuntimeContext,
 3096:       previewCase,
 3097:       sourcePayload: preparedPayload,
 3098:       designTemplateId: template.designTemplateId,
 3099:       variantId: selectedVariantId || null,
 3100:     };
 3101:   }

 3103:   function buildReportTwoSnapshotHtml() {
 3104:     const source = reportTwoPreviewExportRef.current;
 3105: 
 3106:     if (!source) return "";
 3107: 
 3108:     const clone = source.cloneNode(true) as HTMLElement;
 3109:     clone.removeAttribute("aria-hidden");
 3110:     clone.removeAttribute("style");
 3111:     clone.className = "report-two-a4-host report-two-snapshot-approved-root";
 3112: 
 3113:     return `
 3114:       <style>
 3115:         .report-two-snapshot-approved-root {
 3116:           background: #ffffff;
 3117:           direction: rtl;
 3118:         }
 3119: 
 3120:         .report-two-snapshot-approved-root .pdf-report-page {
 3121:           width: 210mm !important;
 3122:           min-width: 210mm !important;
 3123:           max-width: 210mm !important;
 3124:           height: 297mm !important;
 3125:           min-height: 297mm !important;
 3126:           max-height: 297mm !important;
 3127:           margin: 0 auto 16px auto !important;
 3128:           overflow: hidden !important;
 3129:           background: #ffffff !important;
 3130:           box-shadow: none !important;
 3131:         }
 3132: 
 3133:         @media print {
 3134:           .report-two-snapshot-approved-root .pdf-report-page {
 3135:             margin: 0 !important;
 3136:             break-after: page;
 3137:             page-break-after: always;
 3138:           }
 3139:

 3141:             break-after: auto;
 3142:             page-break-after: auto;
 3143:           }
 3144:         }
 3145:       </style>

 3150:   async function createReportTwoApprovedSnapshotInternal(options?: {
 3151:     showSuccessPopup?: boolean;
 3152:     allowReuseExisting?: boolean;
 3153:   }) {
 3154:     const showSuccessPopup = options?.showSuccessPopup !== false;
 3155:     const allowReuseExisting = options?.allowReuseExisting !== false;
 3156: 
 3157:     if (reportTwoApprovalSubmitting) return null;
 3158: 
 3159:     if (approvedSnapshot && allowReuseExisting) {
 3160:       return approvedSnapshot;
 3161:     }
 3162: 
 3163:     if (!signedVisiblePreviewTemplate.pages.length) {
 3164:       setReportTwoActionModal({
 3165:         title: "اعتماد التقرير",
 3166:         message: "لا توجد صفحات جاهزة للاعتماد.",
 3167:       });
 3168:       return null;
 3169:     }
 3170: 
 3171:     if (!signedVisiblePreviewTemplate.pages.length) {
 3172:       setPopup({
 3173:         type: "alert",
 3174:         title: "اعتماد التقرير",
 3175:         message: "لا توجد صفحات جاهزة للاعتماد.",
 3176:       });
 3177:       return null;
 3178:     }
 3179: 
 3180:     const snapshotHtml = buildReportTwoSnapshotHtml();
 3181: 
 3182:     if (!snapshotHtml.trim()) {
 3183:       setReportTwoActionModal({
 3184:         title: "اعتماد التقرير",
 3185:         message: "تعذر التقاط معاينة التقرير الحالية للاعتماد.",
 3186:       });
 3187:       return null;
 3188:     }
 3189: 
 3190:     if (!snapshotHtml.trim()) {
 3191:       setPopup({
 3192:         type: "alert",
 3193:         title: "اعتماد التقرير",
 3194:         message: "تعذر التقاط معاينة التقرير الحالية للاعتماد.",
 3195:       });
 3196:       return null;
 3197:     }
 3198: 
 3199:     setReportTwoApprovalSubmitting(true);
 3200: 
 3201:     try {
 3202:       const activeTemplate = templates.find(
 3203:         (item) => item.id === selectedTemplateOptionId,
 3204:       );
 3205:       const response = await fetch(
 3206:         `/api/dashboard/report-2/cases/${encodeURIComponent(caseId)}/approve`,
 3207:         {
 3208:           method: "POST",
 3209:           headers: {
 3210:             "Content-Type": "application/json",
 3211:           },
 3212:           body: JSON.stringify({
 3213:             reportTitle: getReportTwoSnapshotTitle(),
 3214:             templateId: selectedTemplateOptionId || null,
 3215:             templateName: activeTemplate?.name || template.name || null,
 3216:             variantId: selectedVariantId || null,
 3217:             snapshotPayload: preparedPayload,
 3218:             snapshotTemplateJson: signedVisiblePreviewTemplate,
 3219:             snapshotPagesJson: signedVisiblePreviewTemplate.pages,
 3220:             snapshotHtml,
 3221:             editorState: createReportTwoDraftSnapshot(),
 3222:             approvedEditConfirmed: persistedReport?.status === "APPROVED",
 3223:           }),
 3224:         },
 3225:       );
 3226:       const text = await response.text();
 3227:       let result: any = {};
 3228: 
 3229:       try {
 3230:         result = text ? JSON.parse(text) : {};
 3231:       } catch {
 3232:         result = {
 3233:           error: text || `HTTP ${response.status}`,
 3234:         };
 3235:       }
 3236: 
 3237:       if (!response.ok) {
 3238:         throw new Error(
 3239:           result?.details ||
 3240:             result?.error ||
 3241:             `APPROVAL_FAILED_${response.status}`,
 3242:         );
 3243:       }
 3244: 
 3245:       const nextApprovedSnapshot = {
 3246:         id: result.snapshot?.id,
 3247:         previewUrl:
 3248:           result.previewUrl ||
 3249:           `/dashboard/report-2/snapshots/${result.snapshot?.id}/preview`,
 3250:       };
 3251: 
 3252:       setApprovedSnapshot(nextApprovedSnapshot);
 3253:       setPersistedReport({
 3254:         id: result.snapshot.id,
 3255:         status: "APPROVED",
 3256:         version: result.snapshot.version,
 3257:         approvedAt: result.snapshot.approvedAt,
 3258:         editorState: result.snapshot.editorState,
 3259:         previewUrl: nextApprovedSnapshot.previewUrl,
 3260:       });
 3261: 
 3262:       if (showSuccessPopup) {
 3263:         setReportTwoActionModal({
 3264:           title: "تم اعتماد التقرير",
 3265:           message: "تم حفظ نسخة ثابتة من التقرير في أرشيف التقارير المعتمدة.",
 3266:           linkHref: nextApprovedSnapshot.previewUrl,
 3267:           linkLabel: "استعراض التقرير المعتمد",
 3268:         });
 3269:         return nextApprovedSnapshot;
 3270:       }
 3271: 
 3272:       if (showSuccessPopup) {
 3273:         setPopup({
 3274:           type: "alert",
 3275:           title: "تم اعتماد التقرير",
 3276:           message: "تم حفظ نسخة ثابتة من التقرير في أرشيف التقارير المعتمدة.",
 3277:         });
 3278:       }
 3279: 
 3280:       return nextApprovedSnapshot;
 3281:     } catch (error) {
 3282:       console.error(error);
 3283:       setReportTwoActionModal({
 3284:         title: "اعتماد التقرير",
 3285:         message: "تعذر اعتماد التقرير. حاول مرة أخرى.",
 3286:       });
 3287:       return null;
 3288:       setPopup({
 3289:         type: "alert",
 3290:         title: "اعتماد التقرير",
 3291:         message: "تعذر اعتماد التقرير. حاول مرة أخرى.",
 3292:       });
 3293:       return null;
 3294:     } finally {
 3295:       setReportTwoApprovalSubmitting(false);
 3296:     }
 3297:   }

 3301:

 3353:             snapshotHtml,
 3354:           }),
 3355:         },
 3356:       );
 3357:       const text = await response.text();
 3358:       let result: any = {};
 3359: 
 3360:       try {
 3361:         result = text ? JSON.parse(text) : {};
 3362:       } catch {
 3363:         result = {
 3364:           error: text || `HTTP ${response.status}`,
 3365:         };
 3366:       }
 3367:

 3369:         throw new Error(
 3370:           result?.details ||
 3371:             result?.error ||

 3373:         );

 3379:           result.previewUrl ||

 3381:       });

 3383:         type: "alert",
 3384:         title: "تم اعتماد التقرير",
 3385:         message: "تم حفظ نسخة ثابتة من التقرير في أرشيف التقارير المعتمدة.",
 3386:       });

 3391:         title: "اعتماد التقرير",
 3392:         message: "تعذر اعتماد التقرير. حاول مرة أخرى.",

 3399:   async function exportReportTwoPdfInternal(options?: {
 3400:     fileName?: string;
 3401:     snapshot?: ReturnType<typeof buildReportTwoPdfExportSnapshot>;
 3402:   }) {
 3403:     if (reportTwoPdfExporting) return null;
 3404: 
 3405:     if (!visiblePreviewTemplate.pages.length) {
 3406:       setReportTwoActionModal({
 3407:         title: "تصدير PDF",
 3408:         message: "لا توجد صفحات جاهزة للتصدير.",
 3409:       });
 3410:       return null;
 3411:     }
 3412: 
 3413:     try {
 3414:       const fileName = options?.fileName || getReportTwoPdfFileName();
 3415:       const result = await runPrintExport({
 3416:         exportUrl: `/api/dashboard/report-2/cases/${encodeURIComponent(caseId)}/export/pdf`,
 3417:         fileName,
 3418:         method: "POST",
 3419:         body: {
 3420:           fileName,
 3421:           snapshot: options?.snapshot || buildReportTwoPdfExportSnapshot(),
 3422:         },
 3423:         blockedTitle: "معاينة الطباعة",
 3424:         blockedMessage:
 3425:           "تم حظر فتح نافذة المعاينة تلقائياً. استخدم الزر أدناه لفتح معاينة الطباعة في نافذة جديدة.",
 3426:         errorTitle: "تصدير PDF",
 3427:         errorMessage: "تعذر تصدير التقرير. حاول مرة أخرى.",
 3428:       });
 3429: 
 3430:       return result === "downloaded"
 3431:         ? ("downloaded" as const)
 3432:         : result === "opened" || result === "blocked"
 3433:           ? ("preview-fallback" as const)
 3434:           : null;
 3435:     } catch (error) {
 3436:       console.error(error);
 3437:       setReportTwoActionModal({
 3438:         title: "تصدير PDF",
 3439:         message: "تعذر تصدير التقرير. حاول مرة أخرى.",
 3440:       });
 3441:       return null;
 3442:     }
 3443:   }

 3445:   async function exportReportTwoPdf() {
 3446:     await exportReportTwoPdfInternal();
 3447:   }

 3450:     if (reportTwoApprovalSubmitting || reportTwoPdfExporting) return;
 3451: 
 3452:     const approval = await createReportTwoApprovedSnapshotInternal({
 3453:       showSuccessPopup: false,
 3454:       allowReuseExisting: true,
 3455:     });
 3456:

 3458:       return;

 3462:       fileName: getReportTwoSnapshotTitle(),

 3465:

 3471:         linkLabel: "استعراض التقرير المعتمد",

 3476:         type: "alert",

 3496:   function uploadReportTwoLogo(file: File | null) {
 3497:     if (!file) return;
 3498: 
 3499:     const reader = new FileReader();
 3500: 
 3501:     reader.onload = () => {
 3502:       const result = String(reader.result || "");
 3503: 
 3504:       if (!result) return;
 3505: 
 3506:       updateReportTwoLogoSettings({
 3507:         url: result,
 3508:         filter: "none",
 3509:       });
 3510:     };
 3511: 
 3512:     reader.readAsDataURL(file);
 3513:   }

 3551:   function createReportTwoDraftSnapshot(): ReportTwoDraftSnapshot {
 3552:     return {
 3553:       version: 1,

 3568:   function restoreReportTwoDraftSnapshot(snapshot: ReportTwoDraftSnapshot) {
 3569:     restoringDraftRef.current = true;
 3570: 
 3571:     setSelectedTemplateOptionId(snapshot.selectedTemplateOptionId || selectedTemplateOptionId);
 3572:     setActiveSavedRuntimeTemplateId(snapshot.activeSavedRuntimeTemplateId || "");
 3573:     setRuntimeTemplateName(snapshot.runtimeTemplateName || "");
 3574:     const restoredTemplate = normalizeReportTwoLogicalTemplate(
 3575:       cloneReportTwoTemplate(snapshot.template),
 3576:       preparedPayload,
 3577:     );
 3578:     setTemplate(restoredTemplate);
 3579:     setHeaderValues(snapshot.headerValues || null);
 3580:     setHeaderAlignments(snapshot.headerAlignments || null);
 3581:     setLogoSettings(snapshot.logoSettings || null);
 3582:     setActivePageId(
 3583:       resolveReportTwoEquivalentPageId(
 3584:         restoredTemplate.pages,
 3585:         snapshot.activePageId || activePageId,
 3586:       ),
 3587:     );
 3588:     setSelectedBlockId(snapshot.selectedBlockId || "");
 3589:     setFinalCheckConfirmedAt(snapshot.finalCheckConfirmedAt || null);
 3590:     setFinalChecklistConfirmed(Boolean(snapshot.finalCheckConfirmedAt));
 3591:   }

 3593:   function saveReportTwoDraftNow(showMessage = true) {
 3594:     const snapshot = createReportTwoDraftSnapshot();
 3595:     const serialized = JSON.stringify(snapshot);
 3596: 
 3597:     window.localStorage.setItem(reportTwoDraftStorageKey, serialized);
 3598:     lastDraftSerializedRef.current = serialized;
 3599:     setLastAutoSavedAt(snapshot.savedAt);
 3600: 
 3601:     if (showMessage) {
 3602:       setPopup({ type: "alert", title: "حفظ المسودة", message: "تم حفظ المسودة الحالية." });
 3603:     }
 3604:   }

 3606:   async function persistReportTwoDraft(approvedEditConfirmed = false) {
 3607:     if (reportTwoSaveSubmitting) return;
 3608:     const snapshotHtml = buildReportTwoSnapshotHtml();
 3609:     if (!snapshotHtml.trim()) {
 3610:       setPopup({ type: "alert", title: "حفظ التقرير", message: "تعذر التقاط معاينة التقرير الحالية." });
 3611:       return;
 3612:     }
 3613: 
 3614:     if (persistedReport?.status === "APPROVED" && !approvedEditConfirmed) {
 3615:       setPopup({
 3616:         type: "confirm",
 3617:         title: "تحرير تقرير معتمد",
 3618:         message: "سيتم تعديل محتوى التقرير مع بقاء حالة الاعتماد وتاريخ الاعتماد كما هما.",
 3619:         onConfirm: () => void persistReportTwoDraft(true),
 3620:       });
 3621:       return;
 3622:     }
 3623: 
 3624:     saveReportTwoDraftNow(false);
 3625:     setReportTwoSaveSubmitting(true);
 3626:     try {
 3627:       const activeTemplate = templates.find((item) => item.id === selectedTemplateOptionId);
 3628:       const response = await fetch(
 3629:         `/api/dashboard/report-2/cases/${encodeURIComponent(caseId)}/save`,
 3630:         {
 3631:           method: "PUT",
 3632:           headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
 3633:           body: JSON.stringify({
 3634:             reportTitle: getReportTwoSnapshotTitle(),
 3635:             templateId: selectedTemplateOptionId || null,
 3636:             templateName: activeTemplate?.name || template.name || null,
 3637:             variantId: selectedVariantId || null,
 3638:             snapshotPayload: preparedPayload,
 3639:             snapshotTemplateJson: signedVisiblePreviewTemplate,
 3640:             snapshotPagesJson: signedVisiblePreviewTemplate.pages,
 3641:             snapshotHtml,
 3642:             editorState: createReportTwoDraftSnapshot(),
 3643:             expectedVersion: persistedReport?.version ?? null,
 3644:             approvedEditConfirmed,
 3645:           }),
 3646:         },
 3647:       );
 3648:       const result = await response.json();
 3649:       if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.error || "تعذر حفظ التقرير.");
 3650:       setPersistedReport({
 3651:         id: result.report.id,
 3652:         status: result.report.status,
 3653:         version: result.report.version,
 3654:         approvedAt: result.report.approvedAt,
 3655:         editorState: result.report.editorState,
 3656:         previewUrl: result.previewUrl,
 3657:       });
 3658:       setReportTwoActionModal({
 3659:         title: result.report.status === "APPROVED" ? "تم حفظ التقرير المعتمد" : "تم حفظ التقرير",
 3660:         message: result.message,
 3661:         linkHref: result.previewUrl,
 3662:         linkLabel: "معاينة التقرير",
 3663:       });
 3664:     } catch (error) {
 3665:       setPopup({ type: "alert", title: "حفظ التقرير", message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "تعذر حفظ التقرير." });
 3666:     } finally {
 3667:       setReportTwoSaveSubmitting(false);
 3668:     }
 3669:   }

 3671:   function undoReportTwoLastChange() {
 3672:     const previous = undoSnapshots[0];
 3673: 
 3674:     if (!previous) {
 3675:       setPopup({ type: "alert", title: "التراجع", message: "لا يوجد تعديل سابق للرجوع إليه." });
 3676:       return;
 3677:     }
 3678: 
 3679:     restoreReportTwoDraftSnapshot(previous);
 3680: 
 3681:     const serialized = JSON.stringify(previous);
 3682:     window.localStorage.setItem(reportTwoDraftStorageKey, serialized);
 3683:     lastDraftSerializedRef.current = serialized;
 3684:     setLastAutoSavedAt(previous.savedAt);
 3685:     setUndoSnapshots((current) => current.slice(1));
 3686:   }

 3692:   function saveCurrentRuntimeTemplate() {
 3693:     const name = runtimeTemplateName.trim();
 3694: 
 3695:     if (!name) {
 3696:       setPopup({ type: "alert", title: "حفظ القالب", message: "اكتب اسم القالب أولًا." });
 3697:       return;
 3698:     }
 3699: 
 3700:     const now = new Date().toISOString();
 3701:     const existingId = activeSavedRuntimeTemplateId || "";
 3702:     const id = existingId || makeId("saved-runtime-template");
 3703: 
 3704:     const item: ReportTwoSavedRuntimeTemplate = {
 3705:       id,
 3706:       name,
 3707:       serviceSlug: serviceSlugForSavedTemplates,
 3708:       sourceTemplateId: selectedTemplateOptionId,
 3709:       createdAt:
 3710:         savedRuntimeTemplates.find((template) => template.id === id)?.createdAt ||
 3711:         now,
 3712:       updatedAt: now,
 3713:       template: cloneReportTwoTemplate(template),
 3714:       headerValues,
 3715:       headerAlignments,
 3716:       logoSettings,
 3717:     };
 3718: 
 3719:     const nextItems = [
 3720:       item,
 3721:       ...savedRuntimeTemplates.filter((template) => template.id !== id),
 3722:     ];
 3723: 
 3724:     persistSavedRuntimeTemplates(nextItems);
 3725:     setActiveSavedRuntimeTemplateId(id);
 3726:     setRuntimeTemplateName(name);
 3727: 
 3728:     setPopup({ type: "alert", title: "حفظ القالب", message: "تم حفظ قالب التقرير الحالي." });
 3729:   }

 3731:   function applySavedRuntimeTemplate(templateId: string) {
 3732:     const saved = savedRuntimeTemplates.find((item) => item.id === templateId);
 3733: 
 3734:     if (!saved) return;
 3735: 
 3736:     const restoredTemplate = normalizeReportTwoLogicalTemplate(
 3737:       cloneReportTwoTemplate(saved.template),
 3738:       preparedPayload,
 3739:     );
 3740:     setTemplate(restoredTemplate);
 3741:     setSelectedTemplateOptionId(saved.sourceTemplateId || selectedTemplateOptionId);
 3742:     setHeaderValues(saved.headerValues || null);
 3743:     setHeaderAlignments(saved.headerAlignments || null);
 3744:     setLogoSettings(saved.logoSettings || null);
 3745:     setActiveSavedRuntimeTemplateId(saved.id);
 3746:     setSelectedQuickSavedTemplateId(saved.id);
 3747:     setRuntimeTemplateName(saved.name);
 3748: 
 3749:     setActivePageId(
 3750:       resolveReportTwoEquivalentPageId(restoredTemplate.pages, activePageId),
 3751:     );
 3752:     setSelectedBlockId("");
 3753:   }

 3814:   function updateBlock(blockId: string, updater: (block: StudioBlock) => StudioBlock) {
 3815:     const runtimeBlock =
 3816:       visiblePreviewTemplate.pages
 3817:         .flatMap((page) => page.blocks)
 3818:         .find((block) => block.id === blockId) || null;
 3819: 
 3820:     const sourceBlockId =
 3821:       cleanText((runtimeBlock as any)?.sourceBlockId) || blockId;
 3822: 
 3823:     setTemplate((current) => ({
 3824:       ...current,
 3825:       pages: current.pages.map((page) => ({
 3826:         ...page,
 3827:         blocks: page.blocks.map((block) =>
 3828:           block.id === sourceBlockId ? updater(block) : block,
 3829:         ),
 3830:       })),
 3831:     }));
 3832:   }

 3834:   function addPage() {
 3835:     const page = createPage(template.pages.length + 1);
 3836: 
 3837:     setTemplate((current) => ({
 3838:       ...current,
 3839:       pages: [...current.pages, page],
 3840:     }));
 3841: 
 3842:     setActivePageId(page.id);
 3843:     setSelectedBlockId("");
 3844:   }

 3846:   function canDeleteReportTwoPage(pageId: string) {
 3847:     if (!template.pages.some((page) => page.id === pageId)) return false;
 3848:     if (protectedPageIds.includes(pageId)) return false;
 3849:     if (template.pages.length <= 1) return false;
 3850: 
 3851:     return true;
 3852:   }
 3853: 
 3854:   function canMoveReportTwoPage(
 3855:     pageId: string,
 3856:     direction: "previous" | "next",
 3857:   ) {
 3858:     const pageIds = visiblePreviewTemplate.pages.map((page) => page.id);
 3859:     const index = pageIds.indexOf(pageId);
 3860: 
 3861:     if (index < 0) return false;
 3862: 
 3863:     return direction === "previous"
 3864:       ? index > 0
 3865:       : index < pageIds.length - 1;
 3866:   }
 3867: 
 3868:   function moveReportTwoPage(
 3869:     pageId: string,
 3870:     direction: "previous" | "next",
 3871:   ) {
 3872:     if (!canMoveReportTwoPage(pageId, direction)) return;
 3873: 
 3874:     setTemplate((current) => ({
 3875:       ...current,
 3876:       pages: reorderReportTwoPages(current.pages, pageId, direction),
 3877:     }));
 3878:     setActivePageId(pageId);
 3879:   }
 3880: 
 3881:   function deleteReportTwoPage(pageId: string) {
 3882:     if (!template.pages.some((page) => page.id === pageId)) return;
 3883: 
 3884:     if (!canDeleteReportTwoPage(pageId)) {
 3885:       setPopup({ type: "alert", title: "حذف الصفحة", message: "لا يمكن حذف صفحة قادمة من قالب الاستديو الأصلي." });
 3886:       return;
 3887:     }
 3888: 
 3889:     setPopup({
 3890:       type: "confirm",
 3891:       title: "حذف الصفحة",
 3892:       message: "هل تريد حذف هذه الصفحة من التقرير؟",
 3893:       onConfirm: () => {
 3894:         const remainingPages = template.pages.filter(
 3895:           (page) => page.id !== pageId,
 3896:         );
 3897: 
 3898:         setTemplate((current) => ({
 3899:           ...current,
 3900:           pages: remainingPages,
 3901:         }));
 3902: 
 3903:         setActivePageId(remainingPages[0]?.id || "");
 3904:         setSelectedBlockId("");
 3905:       },
 3906:     });
 3907:   }

 3913:   function addBlock(kind: StudioBlockKind) {
 3914:     if (kind === "evidence-gallery" || kind === "signature-grid") {
 3915:       const existing = template.pages
 3916:         .flatMap((page) => page.blocks.map((block) => ({ block, pageId: page.id })))
 3917:         .find(({ block }) =>
 3918:           kind === "evidence-gallery"
 3919:             ? block.kind === "evidence-gallery"
 3920:             : isReportTwoSignatureBlock(block),
 3921:         );
 3922: 
 3923:       if (existing) {
 3924:         setActivePageId(existing.pageId);
 3925:         setSelectedBlockId(existing.block.id);
 3926:         setSemanticPlacementNotice(
 3927:           kind === "evidence-gallery"
 3928:             ? "كتلة الشواهد موجودة بالفعل في النموذج المنطقي."
 3929:             : "كتلة التوقيعات موجودة بالفعل في النموذج المنطقي.",
 3930:         );
 3931:         return;
 3932:       }
 3933:     }
 3934: 
 3935:     const targetPageId = getWritableReportTwoPageId(
 3936:       activePageId,
 3937:       activePage,
 3938:       template,
 3939:     );
 3940: 
 3941:     const targetPage = template.pages.find((page) => page.id === targetPageId);
 3942: 
 3943:     if (!targetPage) return;
 3944: 
 3945:     const block = createBlock(kind);
 3946:     const nextTemplate = {
 3947:       ...template,
 3948:       pages: template.pages.map((page) =>
 3949:         page.id === targetPage.id
 3950:           ? {
 3951:               ...page,
 3952:               blocks: [...page.blocks, block],
 3953:             }
 3954:           : page,
 3955:       ),
 3956:     };
 3957:     setTemplate(applyReportTwoPreparedExecutionSummary(nextTemplate, preparedPayload));
 3958: 
 3959:     setActivePageId(targetPage.id);
 3960:     setSelectedBlockId(block.id);
 3961:     setSemanticPlacementNotice("");
 3962:   }
 3963: 
 3964:   function removeSelectedBlock() {
 3965:     if (!selectedBlock || !activePage) return;
 3966: 
 3967:     const writablePageId = getWritableReportTwoPageId(activePageId, activePage, template);
 3968:     const sourcePage = template.pages.find((page) => page.id === writablePageId);
 3969:     if (!sourcePage) return;
 3970: 
 3971:     const blockExists = sourcePage.blocks.some((b) => b.id === selectedBlock.id);
 3972:     if (!blockExists) return;
 3973: 
 3974:     if (sourcePage.blocks.length <= 1) {
 3975:       setPopup({ type: "alert", title: "حذف البلوك", message: "لا يمكن حذف آخر بلوك داخل الصفحة." });
 3976:       return;
 3977:     }
 3978: 
 3979:     const remainingBlocks = sourcePage.blocks.filter(
 3980:       (block) => block.id !== selectedBlock.id,
 3981:     );
 3982: 
 3983:     updatePage(sourcePage.id, (page) => ({
 3984:       ...page,
 3985:       blocks: remainingBlocks,
 3986:     }));
 3987: 
 3988:     setSelectedBlockId(remainingBlocks[0]?.id || "");
 3989:   }
 3990: 
 3991:   function moveBlock(direction: "up" | "down") {
 3992:     if (!selectedBlock) return;
 3993: 
 3994:     const sourcePage = template.pages.find((page) =>
 3995:       page.blocks.some((block) => block.id === selectedBlock.id),
 3996:     );
 3997:     if (!sourcePage) return;
 3998: 
 3999:     const index = sourcePage.blocks.findIndex(
 4000:       (block) => block.id === selectedBlock.id,
 4001:     );
 4002: 
 4003:     if (index < 0) return;
 4004: 
 4005:     const selectedIsStudentTable =
 4006:       selectedBlock.kind === "structured-table" && isStudentDataTable(selectedBlock);
 4007:     if (selectedIsStudentTable) {
 4008:       setSemanticPlacementNotice("جدول بيانات الطلاب يظهر دائمًا في بداية التقرير.");
 4009:       return;
 4010:     }
 4011:     if (isReportTwoSignatureBlock(selectedBlock)) {
 4012:       setSemanticPlacementNotice("كتلة التوقيعات تظهر دائمًا في نهاية التقرير.");
 4013:       return;
 4014:     }
 4015: 
 4016:     const targetIndex = direction === "up" ? index - 1 : index + 1;
 4017: 
 4018:     if (targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= sourcePage.blocks.length) return;
 4019:     const targetBlock = sourcePage.blocks[targetIndex];
 4020:     if (direction === "down" && isReportTwoSignatureBlock(targetBlock)) {
 4021:       setSemanticPlacementNotice("لا يمكن وضع محتوى بعد كتلة التوقيعات.");
 4022:       return;
 4023:     }
 4024:     if (
 4025:       direction === "up" &&
 4026:       targetBlock.kind === "structured-table" &&
 4027:       isStudentDataTable(targetBlock)
 4028:     ) {
 4029:       setSemanticPlacementNotice("جدول بيانات الطلاب يظهر دائمًا في بداية التقرير.");
 4030:       return;
 4031:     }
 4032: 
 4033:     const nextBlocks = [...sourcePage.blocks];
 4034:     const [block] = nextBlocks.splice(index, 1);
 4035:     nextBlocks.splice(targetIndex, 0, block);
 4036: 
 4037:     updatePage(sourcePage.id, (page) => ({
 4038:       ...page,
 4039:       blocks: nextBlocks,
 4040:     }));
 4041:   }
 4042:

 4044:   function updateDynamicField(
 4045:     blockId: string,
 4046:     fieldId: string,
 4047:     patch: Partial<ReportTwoDynamicField>,
 4048:   ) {
 4049:     updateBlock(blockId, (block) => ({
 4050:       ...block,
 4051:       dynamicFields: getDynamicFieldsForBlock(block, previewCase).map((field) =>
 4052:         field.id === fieldId
 4053:           ? {
 4054:               ...field,
 4055:               ...patch,
 4056:             }
 4057:           : field,
 4058:       ),
 4059:     }));
 4060:   }
 4061: 
 4062:   function moveDynamicField(
 4063:     blockId: string,
 4064:     fieldId: string,
 4065:     direction: "up" | "down",
 4066:   ) {
 4067:     updateBlock(blockId, (block) => {
 4068:       const fields = getDynamicFieldsForBlock(block, previewCase);
 4069:       const index = fields.findIndex((field) => field.id === fieldId);
 4070: 
 4071:       if (index < 0) return block;
 4072: 
 4073:       const targetIndex = direction === "up" ? index - 1 : index + 1;
 4074: 
 4075:       if (targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= fields.length) return block;
 4076: 
 4077:       const nextFields = [...fields];
 4078:       const [field] = nextFields.splice(index, 1);
 4079:       nextFields.splice(targetIndex, 0, field);
 4080: 
 4081:       return {
 4082:         ...block,
 4083:         dynamicFields: nextFields,
 4084:       };
 4085:     });
 4086:   }

 4090:       ...block,
 4091:       dynamicFields: getReportTwoDynamicFieldsFromPreviewCase(previewCase),
 4092:     }));
 4093:   }

 6458:                         className="rounded-2xl bg-emerald-700 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-black text-white transition hover:bg-emerald-800"
 6459:                       >
 6460:                         + إضافة صف
 6461:                       </button>
 6462: 
 6463:                       <button
 6464:                         type="button"
 6465:                         onClick={() =>
 6466:                           updateEditingTableDraft((draft) =>
 6467:                             addReportTwoTableColumn(draft),
 6468:                           )
 6469:                         }
 6470:                         className="rounded-2xl bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm font-black text-emerald-800 ring-1 ring-emerald-100 transition hover:bg-emerald-50"
 6471:                       >
 6472:                         + إضافة عمود
 6473:                       </button>

FILE: components\report-2\report-two-active-preview-renderer.tsx
============================================================
    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useMemo } from "react";
    4: import { ReportDesignRenderer } from "@/components/report-engine/design-renderers/report-design-renderer";
    5: import { isReportDesignId } from "@/components/report-engine/design-renderers/report-design-registry";
    6: import { applyStructuredTableDisplayMetadataToTemplate } from "@/lib/report-engine/report-structured-table-display";
    7: import {
    8:   OFFICIAL_ACTIVITY_CARD_VARIANT_ID,
    9:   ReportTwoOfficialActivitySignatureStyle,
   10: } from "@/components/report-2/report-two-official-activity-signature-style";
   11: 
   12: export function ReportTwoActivePreviewRenderer({ template, context, previewCase, sourcePayload, variantId }: {
   13:   template: any;
   14:   context: Record<string, string>;
   15:   previewCase: any;
   16:   sourcePayload?: unknown;
   17:   variantId?: string | null;
   18: }) {
   19:   const displayTemplate = useMemo(
   20:     () => applyStructuredTableDisplayMetadataToTemplate(template, sourcePayload),
   21:     [template, sourcePayload],
   22:   ) as any;
   23:   const pages = Array.isArray(displayTemplate?.pages) ? displayTemplate.pages : [];
   24:   const activePage = pages[0] || null;
   25:   const designId = isReportDesignId(displayTemplate?.designTemplateId)
   26:     ? displayTemplate.designTemplateId
   27:     : null;
   28: 
   29:   if (!designId) {
   30:     return (
   31:       <section className="p-8 text-center font-bold text-red-700" dir="rtl">
   32:         تعذر تحديد تصميم التقرير المحفوظ.
   33:       </section>
   34:     );
   35:   }
   36: 
   37:   return (
   38:     <section
   39:       className={[
   40:         "report-two-a4-host report-two-persisted-active-root",
   41:         variantId === OFFICIAL_ACTIVITY_CARD_VARIANT_ID
   42:           ? "report-two-official-activity-card"
   43:           : "",
   44:       ].join(" ")}
   45:     >
   46:       <ReportTwoOfficialActivitySignatureStyle
   47:         enabled={variantId === OFFICIAL_ACTIVITY_CARD_VARIANT_ID}
   48:       />
   49:       <ReportDesignRenderer
   50:         renderMode="stack"
   51:         chromeLayout="split"
   52:         designId={designId}
   53:         template={displayTemplate}
   54:         activePage={activePage}
   55:         activePageId={activePage?.id || ""}
   56:         context={context || {}}
   57:         previewCase={previewCase || null}
   58:         onActivePageChange={() => undefined}
   59:         onAddPage={() => undefined}
   60:         onMovePage={() => undefined}
   61:         onDeletePage={() => undefined}
   62:         canMovePage={() => false}
   63:         canDeletePage={() => false}
   64:       />
   65:     </section>
   66:   );
   67: }

FILE: components\report-2\report-two-print-document.tsx
============================================================
    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import {
    4:   getDesignLogoSrc,
    5:   ReportDesignRenderer,
    6:   type ReportDesignId,
    7: } from "@/components/report-engine/design-renderers/report-design-renderer";
    8: import { isReportDesignId } from "@/components/report-engine/design-renderers/report-design-registry";
    9: import { ReportTwoSnapshotPrintController } from "@/components/report-2/report-two-snapshot-print-controller";
   10: import { applyStructuredTableDisplayMetadataToTemplate } from "@/lib/report-engine/report-structured-table-display";
   11: import {
   12:   OFFICIAL_ACTIVITY_CARD_VARIANT_ID,
   13:   ReportTwoOfficialActivitySignatureStyle,
   14: } from "@/components/report-2/report-two-official-activity-signature-style";
   15: 
   16: type PrintDocumentSnapshot = {
   17:   template: any;
   18:   context: Record<string, string>;
   19:   previewCase: any;
   20:   sourcePayload?: unknown;
   21:   designTemplateId?: ReportDesignId;
   22:   variantId?: string | null;
   23: };
   24: 
   25: export function ReportTwoPrintDocument({
   26:   snapshot,
   27:   autoPrint = false,
   28: }: {
   29:   snapshot: PrintDocumentSnapshot;
   30:   autoPrint?: boolean;
   31: }) {
   32:   const template = applyStructuredTableDisplayMetadataToTemplate(
   33:     snapshot.template || { pages: [] },
   34:     snapshot.sourcePayload,
   35:   ) as any;
   36:   const pages = Array.isArray(template.pages) ? template.pages : [];
   37:   const context = snapshot.context || {};
   38:   const previewCase = snapshot.previewCase || null;
   39:   const requestedDesignId =
   40:     snapshot.designTemplateId || template.designTemplateId;
   41:   const designId = isReportDesignId(requestedDesignId)
   42:     ? requestedDesignId
   43:     : null;
   44: 
   45:   const logoSrc = getDesignLogoSrc(context);
   46: 
   47:   if (!designId) {
   48:     return (
   49:       <main className="p-8 text-center font-bold text-red-700" dir="rtl">
   50:         تعذر تحديد تصميم التقرير المحفوظ للطباعة.
   51:       </main>
   52:     );
   53:   }
   54: 
   55:   return (
   56:     <main
   57:       className={[
   58:         "report-two-print-document",
   59:         snapshot.variantId === OFFICIAL_ACTIVITY_CARD_VARIANT_ID
   60:           ? "report-two-official-activity-card"
   61:           : "",
   62:       ].join(" ")}
   63:       dir="rtl"
   64:     >
   65:       {autoPrint ? <ReportTwoSnapshotPrintController /> : null}
   66:       <ReportTwoOfficialActivitySignatureStyle
   67:         enabled={snapshot.variantId === OFFICIAL_ACTIVITY_CARD_VARIANT_ID}
   68:       />
   69:       <style>{`
   70:         @page {
   71:           size: A4;
   72:           margin: 0;
   73:         }
   74: 
   75:         :root,
   76:         html,
   77:         body {
   78:           color-scheme: light !important;
   79:         }
   80: 
   81:         * {
   82:           box-sizing: border-box;
   83:           -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
   84:           print-color-adjust: exact;
   85:         }
   86: 
   87:         html,
   88:         body {
   89:           margin: 0 !important;
   90:           padding: 0 !important;
   91:           background: #ffffff !important;
   92:           color: #0f172a;
   93:           direction: rtl;
   94:         }
   95: 
   96:         body {
   97:           overflow: visible !important;
   98:         }
   99: 
  100:         .report-two-print-document {
  101:           background: #ffffff;
  102:         }
  103: 
  104:         .report-two-print-document .pdf-report-page {
  105:           width: 210mm !important;
  106:           min-width: 210mm !important;
  107:           max-width: 210mm !important;
  108:           height: 297mm !important;
  109:           min-height: 297mm !important;
  110:           max-height: 297mm !important;
  111:           overflow: hidden !important;
  112:           break-after: page !important;
  113:           page-break-after: always !important;
  114:           page-break-inside: avoid !important;
  115:         }
  116: 
  117:         .report-two-print-document .pdf-report-page:last-child {
  118:           break-after: auto !important;
  119:           page-break-after: auto !important;
  120:         }
  121: 
  122:         .report-two-print-document img {
  123:           max-width: 100%;
  124:         }
  125:       `}</style>
  126:       {logoSrc && <link rel="preload" as="image" href={logoSrc} />}
  127: 
  128:       <ReportDesignRenderer
  129:         designId={designId}
  130:         template={template}
  131:         activePage={pages[0] || null}
  132:         activePageId={pages[0]?.id || ""}
  133:         context={context}
  134:         previewCase={previewCase}
  135:         renderMode="stack"
  136:         chromeLayout="none"
  137:         onActivePageChange={() => undefined}
  138:         onAddPage={() => undefined}
  139:         onMovePage={() => undefined}
  140:         onDeletePage={() => undefined}
  141:         canMovePage={() => false}
  142:         canDeletePage={() => false}
  143:       />
  144:     </main>
  145:   );
  146: }

   91: 
   92: export function getReportTemplate(templateId: ReportTemplateId) {
   93:   return (
   94:     reportTemplates.find((template) => template.id === templateId) ||
   95:     reportTemplates[0]
   96:   );
   97: }
   98: 
   99: export function getReportValue(
  100:   report: OfficialReportData,
  101:   key: ReportTemplateFieldKey
  102: ) {
  103:   switch (key) {
  104:     case "title":
  105:       return report.title;
  106:     case "subtitle":
  107:       return report.subtitle;
  108:     case "serviceName":
  109:       return report.serviceName;
  110:     case "category":
  111:       return report.category;
  112:     case "reportDate":
  113:       return report.reportDate;
  114:     case "targetGroup":
  115:       return report.targetGroup;
  116:     case "cover.programTitle":
  117:       return report.cover.programTitle;
  118:     case "cover.executionDate":
  119:       return report.cover.executionDate;
  120:     case "cover.shortDescription":
  121:       return report.cover.shortDescription;
  122:     case "sections":
  123:       return report.sections?.length ? report.sections : null;
  124:     case "evidences":
  125:       return report.evidences?.length ? report.evidences : null;
  126:     case "approval":
  127:       return report.approval;
  128:     default:
  129:       return null;
  130:   }
  131: }
  132: 
  133: export function getMissingReportFields(
  134:   report: OfficialReportData,
  135:   templateId: ReportTemplateId
  136: ) {
  137:   const template = getReportTemplate(templateId);
  138: 
  139:   return template.requiredFields.filter((field) => {
  140:     const value = getReportValue(report, field);
  141: 
  142:     if (Array.isArray(value)) {
  143:       return value.length === 0;
  144:     }
  145: 
  146:     return !value;
  147:   });
  148: }

5. QUESTIONS TO ANSWER
============================================================
1. In what exact order are narrative, evidence, and signature blocks created?
2. Is long narrative one indivisible block?
3. Can multi-paragraph content be split across physical pages?
4. What happens when a single generic block is taller than one A4 page?
5. Does the engine preserve that block intact even when it overflows?
6. Can evidence/signatures disappear because an oversized previous block occupies the same physical page?
7. Does adding a manual logical page change where special blocks are appended?
8. Are evidence/signatures always appended only to the last physical page of a logical page?
9. Does report preview and final print use the exact same block/page source?
10. What generic block types need safe splitting support?
